Abstract: This paper is concerned with what intermediate syntactic categories children acquire during first language development, and in what order. Maturational theories make different predictions. Bottom-up accounts (GROWING) propose that lexical and inflectional structure emerges first, while inward accounts (INWARD) predict early access to discourse-related categories. We computationally operationalise these hypotheses of staged syntactic emergence using statistical grammar induction, asking what each proposed ordering makes learnable when input and learning algorithm are held constant. Our framework makes category acquisition explicit and allows us to explore how different maturational orderings shape the structure that can be learned under identical conditions. Based on this operationalisation, the GROWING account significantly outperforms the INWARD account across three evaluation metrics.

</details>


### [82] [Do Agents Need to Plan Step-by-Step? Rethinking Planning Horizon in Data-Centric Tool Calling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08477)
*Naoki Otani,Nikita Bhutani,Hannah Kim,Dan Zhang,Estevam Hruschka*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过控制实验研究，发现全视野规划（FH）加上懒惰重规划在明确定义的数据密集型任务中，与单步执行（SH）达到同等精度，但节省2-3倍token开销，表明在此类任务中无需逐步骤急切监控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 质疑基于LLM的智能体在处理复杂数据密集型任务时必须采用逐步骤急切执行监控（单步视野）的默认假设，探究全视野规划是否能在此类任务中更高效。

Method: 控制实验研究，将规划视野作为关键架构特征进行隔离，系统分析拓扑复杂度和工具鲁棒性对两种范式（全视野规划与单步执行）的影响。

Result: 全视野规划+懒惰重规划在知识库问答和多跳问答任务中，在不同深度、宽度和鲁棒性水平下均与单步执行达到精度同等水平，同时消耗2-3倍更少的token。

Conclusion: 对于明确定义的数据密集型任务，逐步骤急切监控往往是不必要的，全视野规划配合按需重规划是一种更高效的默认策略。

Abstract: Explicit planning is a critical capability for LLM-based agents solving complex data-centric tasks, which require precise tool calling over external data sources. Existing strategies fall into two paradigms based on planning horizon: (1) full-horizon (FH), which generates a complete plan before execution, and (2) single-step horizon (SH), which interleaves each action (tool call) with incremental reasoning and observation. While step-by-step execution is a common default under the assumption that eager execution monitoring is necessary for adaptability, we revisit this assumption for well-defined data-centric tasks. Our controlled empirical study isolates planning horizon as the key architectural feature and systematically analyzes the effects of topological complexity and tool robustness on both paradigms. Our experiments across Knowledge Base Question Answering and Multi-hop QA show that FH planning with lazy replanning achieves accuracy parity with SH across varying depths, breadths, and robustness levels, while using 2-3x fewer tokens. These findings suggest that for well-defined data-centric tasks, eager step-wise monitoring is often unnecessary, and full-horizon planning with on-demand replanning can offer a more efficient default.

</details>


### [83] [NARRA-Gym for Evaluating Interactive Narrative Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08503)
*Yue Huang,Yuchen Ma,Jiayi Ye,Wenjie Wang,Zipeng Ling,Xingjian Hu,Yuexing Hao,Zichen Chen,Zhangchen Xu,Yunhong He,Zhengqing Yuan,Yujun Zhou,Kehan Guo,Chaoran Chen,Toby Jia-Jun Li,Stefan Feuerriegel,Xiangliang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: NARRA-Gym是一个可执行的交互式叙事评估环境，通过将稀疏情感种子转化为完整故事剧集，评估LLM在多轮交互中的故事生成、长上下文管理、角色模拟、共情个性化等能力。对9个前沿LLM的评估显示，模型在流畅性、鲁棒性、用户体验等方面存在显著差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评测大多聚焦于静态提示、孤立故事生成或事后评分，无法衡量模型在多轮交互中联合管理故事生成、长上下文状态与节奏、角色模拟、共情个性化以及基于故事的工件生成等能力。

Method: 构建NARRA-Gym可执行评估环境，将稀疏情感种子转化为完整交互式故事剧集，记录包括故事构建、记忆更新、规划、节奏干预和可选工件合成在内的完整模型参与轨迹。使用LLM-as-judge对8个基准人格进行控制评估，并开展人类评估。

Result: 不同模型、人格和评估维度间存在显著差异：能生成流畅故事的模型在鲁棒性、用户体验或抵抗敏感个性化方面仍可能失败。

Conclusion: 交互式叙事为评估LLM的长程、用户自适应行为提供了超越孤立故事质量的有用基准。

Abstract: Interactive narrative tasks require LLMs to sustain a coherent, evolving story while adapting to a user over multiple turns. However, suitable benchmarks for this setting are limited: existing evaluations often focus on static prompts, isolated story generations, or post-hoc ratings, and therefore miss whether models can jointly manage story generation, long-context state and pacing, character simulation, empathic personalization, and story-grounded artifacts. We introduce NARRA-Gym, an executable evaluation environment that turns a sparse emotional seed into a complete interactive story episode and logs the full model-in-the-loop trajectory, including story construction, memory updates, planning, pacing interventions, and optional artifact synthesis. We evaluate nine frontier LLMs using a controlled LLM-as-judge sweep over eight benchmark personas and a human evaluation in which participants rate customized model outputs. Our results show substantial variation across models, personas, and evaluation dimensions: models that produce fluent stories can still fail on robustness, user experience, or resistance-sensitive personalization. These findings suggest that interactive narrative offers a useful benchmark for evaluating long-horizon, user-adaptive LLM behavior beyond isolated story quality.

</details>


### [84] [A Single Layer to Explain Them All:Understanding Massive Activations in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08504)
*Zeru Shi,Zhenting Wang,Fan Yang,Qifan Wang,Ruixiang Tang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发现并命名了大规模语言模型中的"大规模涌现层"（ME Layer），该层是巨大激活值首次出现的特定层，并通过残差连接传播到深层。研究发现ME层的RMSNorm和FFN参数共同导致大规模激活的产生，且一旦形成，该token表示在层间几乎不变，降低了注意力模块接收的隐藏表示多样性。为此提出了一种简单有效的方法来降低大规模激活token的刚性，在无训练和微调设置下均能提升指令遵循和数学推理等任务性能，同时通过削弱注意力沉点的影响来缓解该问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究大规模语言模型中巨大激活值（massive activations）的起源，理解其形成机制以及对模型表示多样性的影响。

Method: 首先通过实验观察识别出"大规模涌现层"（ME Layer），分析RMSNorm和FFN参数在其中的作用；然后提出降低大规模激活token刚性的方法，通过削弱注意力沉点的影响来改善模型性能。

Result: 所提方法在无训练和微调设置下均能一致提升LLM在指令遵循和数学推理等任务上的性能，同时有效缓解了注意力沉点问题。

Conclusion: 大规模激活源于特定层中RMSNorm和FFN参数的共同作用，其导致的表示刚性限制了模型性能，所提方法通过降低这种刚性能够有效改善LLM表现并缓解注意力沉点。

Abstract: We investigate the origins of massive activations in large language models (LLMs) and identify a specific layer named the \textbf{Massive Emergence Layer (ME Layer)}, that is consistently observed across model families, where massive activations first emerge and subsequently propagate to deeper layers through residual connections. We show that, within the ME Layer both the RMSNorm and the FFN parameters jointly contribute to the emergence of massive activations. Once formed, the massive activation token representation remains largely invariant across layers, reducing the diversity of hidden representations passed to the attention module. Motivated by this limitation, we propose a simple and effective method to reduce the rigidity of the massive activation token. Our approach consistently improves LLM performance across multiple tasks, including instruction following and math reasoning, in both training free and fine tuning settings. Moreover, we show that our method mitigates attention sinks by selectively weakening their influence, elucidating their origin at the hidden state level and shedding new light on principled mitigation strategies.

</details>


### [85] [A Single Neuron Is Sufficient to Bypass Safety Alignment in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08513)
*Hamid Kazemi,Atoosa Chegini,Maria Safi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 安全对齐通过两种机制不同的神经元系统运作：拒绝神经元（控制有害知识是否被表达）和概念神经元（编码有害知识本身）。通过分别靶向每个系统中的单个神经元，无需训练或提示工程，即可实现安全失败——压制拒绝神经元可绕过安全对齐，放大概念神经元可从无害提示诱导有害内容。这表明安全对齐并非稳健分布在模型权重中，而是由单个神经元中介，每个神经元都因果上足以控制拒绝行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究语言模型安全对齐的机制基础，特别是安全行为是否由少数关键神经元中介，以及安全失败是否可以通过操纵单个神经元实现。

Method: 识别并靶向拒绝神经元（控制有害知识表达）和概念神经元（编码有害知识本身）中的单个神经元，通过抑制或放大操作，在七个模型（两个系列，1.7B至70B参数）上测试安全对齐的失效情况。

Result: 抑制任何单个拒绝神经元即可绕过多种有害请求的安全对齐；放大概念神经元可从无害提示诱导有害内容。两种操作均无需训练或提示工程。

Conclusion: 安全对齐并非分布式稳健地编码在模型权重中，而是依赖于少数因果充分的单个神经元来门控拒绝行为，这揭示了安全对齐的脆弱性。

Abstract: Safety alignment in language models operates through two mechanistically distinct systems: refusal neurons that gate whether harmful knowledge is expressed, and concept neurons that encode the harmful knowledge itself. By targeting a single neuron in each system, we demonstrate both directions of failure -- bypassing safety on explicit harmful requests via suppression, and inducing harmful content from innocent prompts via amplification -- across seven models spanning two families and 1.7B to 70B parameters, without any training or prompt engineering. Our findings suggest that safety alignment is not robustly distributed across model weights but is mediated by individual neurons that are each causally sufficient to gate refusal behavior -- suppressing any one of the identified refusal neurons bypasses safety alignment across diverse harmful requests.

</details>


### [86] [Coordinates of Capability: A Unified MTMM-Geometric Framework for LLM Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08522)
*Adib Sakhawat,Tahsin Islam,Takia Farhin,Syed Rifat Raiyan,Hasan Mahmud,Md Kamrul Hasan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于多特质多方法（MTMM）框架的大语言模型评估系统化知识体系，通过将九种评估指标统一解释为共享潜在坐标空间中的几何测量，将模型行为分解为三个正交的潜在维度（不稳定性与敏感性、位置与对齐、覆盖与表达力），从而在理论上区分任务无关扰动与真实能力跨度，为稳健的基准测试设计提供领域无关的分类体系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大语言模型评估面临构建效度的关键挑战：碎片化的基准测试和临时性指标常将方法方差（如提示敏感性）与真实潜在能力混为一谈，缺乏系统化的理论框架来统一和区分这些因素。

Method: 提出广义的MTMM框架，将九种评估指标（包括释义不稳定性、漂移分数、奥弗顿宽度、多元主义分数等）形式化并统一解释为共享潜在坐标空间中的几何测量，从而将模型行为分解为三个正交的潜在维度。

Result: 实现了评估指标的空间统一，将模型行为系统性地分解为三个正交的潜在维度，提供了理论上稳健、经验上稳定的领域无关基准设计分类体系。

Conclusion: 该框架通过系统分离任务无关扰动与真实能力跨度，为LLM评估提供了理论基础的构建效度改进方案，有望推动更稳健和可解释的基准测试设计。

Abstract: The evaluation of Large Language Models (LLMs) faces a critical challenge in construct validity, where fragmented benchmarks and ad hoc metrics frequently conflate method variance, such as prompt sensitivity, with true latent capabilities. Concurrently, emerging research suggests that LLM capabilities and outputs can be modeled as continuous geometric manifolds. In this Systematization of Knowledge (SoK), we bridge these paradigms by proposing a generalized Multi-Trait Multi-Method (MTMM) framework for LLM evaluation. We formalize and unify nine evaluation metrics, including Paraphrase Instability, Drift Score, Overton Width, and Pluralism Score, interpreting them not as isolated scalar values but as geometric measurements within a shared latent coordinate space. This spatial unification factorizes model behavior into three orthogonal latent dimensions: (1) Instability and Sensitivity, (2) Position and Alignment, and (3) Coverage and Expressiveness. By systematically separating task-irrelevant perturbations from true capability spans, the framework provides a theoretically grounded and domain-agnostic taxonomy for robust and empirically stable benchmark design.

</details>


### [87] [Source or It Didn't Happen: A Multi-Agent Framework for Citation Hallucination Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08583)
*Mingzhe Li,Zhiqiang Lin,Shiqing Ma*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CiteTracer 是一个基于多智能体级联架构的引文幻觉检测系统，采用12级分类法将引文分为真实、潜在和幻觉三类，通过PDF/BibTeX提取、多源检索、确定性字段匹配和分类专家判断实现97.1%的检测准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有引文幻觉检测器常将验证简化为二元二分类（存在/不存在），依赖脆弱的解析或不完整的检索，缺乏面向审计者的细粒度信号。本文旨在将引文幻觉检测重构为符合分类法的细粒度判定问题。

Method: 提出12级分类法涵盖真实、潜在和幻觉三类引文；构建CiteTracer级联多智能体系统，包括结构化引文提取（PDF/BibTeX）、多源证据检索（缓存、URL抓取、学术搜索、Web搜索）、确定性字段匹配，以及针对模糊案例的类别专家判定器。构建了2,450条合成基准和957条真实世界伪造引文基准。

Result: 合成基准上达到97.1%准确率，真实/潜在/幻觉的F1分别为97.0/95.8/98.5；真实世界数据集上检测出97.1%的伪造引文且无弃权。

Conclusion: CiteTracer通过细粒度分类法和多智能体级联架构有效提升了引文幻觉检测的准确率和鲁棒性，代码已开源。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used in scientific writing, yet they can fabricate citation-shaped references that appear plausible but fail bibliographic verification. Existing detectors often reduce verification to binary found/not-found decisions and rely on brittle parsing or incomplete retrieval, offering little field-level signal to auditors. We reframe citation hallucination detection as taxonomy-aligned field-level adjudication and introduce a 12-code taxonomy spanning Real, Potential, and Hallucinated citations. Based on this taxonomy, we build CiteTracer, a cascading multi-agent detector that extracts structured citations from PDF and BibTeX, retrieves evidence through cache lookup, URL fetch, scholar connectors, and web search, applies deterministic field matching, and routes ambiguous cases to class-specialist judgers. We release a benchmark of 2,450 synthetic citations built from real seeds with controlled LLM mutations, paired with 957 real-world fabricated citations drawn from ICLR 2026 and an anonymous conference desk-rejected submissions. CiteTracer reaches 97.1% accuracy on the synthetic benchmark, with class-level F1 scores of 97.0, 95.8, and 98.5 for Real, Potential, and Hallucinated, respectively, and detects 97.1% of fabrications on the real-world set without abstaining. Code: https://github.com/aaFrostnova/CiteTracer.

</details>


### [88] [100,000+ Movie Reviews from Kazakhstan: Russian, Kazakh, and Code-Switched Texts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08600)
*Rustem Yeshpanov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个包含100,502条哈萨克斯坦电影评论的多语言公开数据集（2001-2025年，4,943部电影），以俄语为主，包含哈萨克语和代码混合文本。评论经过语言和情感极性的人工标注，11,309条评论附有用户评分。实验表明，Transformer模型在情感三分类上优于传统BoW/TF-IDF基线，但在五分类评分任务中因类别不平衡和相邻级别区分困难而表现不佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前缺乏针对哈萨克斯坦电影评论领域的高质量多语言情感分析数据集，现有资源很少覆盖俄语-哈萨克语代码混合文本，且公开可用的大型标注语料库不足。

Method: 从kino.kz爬取100,502条评论数据，人工标注语言和情感极性；使用BoW/TF-IDF作为经典基线，对比mBERT、XLM-RoBERTa、RemBERT等多语言Transformer模型；设计了情感三分类和五级评分分类两个任务，并在泄漏控制评估下进行实验。

Result: Transformer模型在情感三分类任务中始终优于经典基线方法；五级评分分类任务因严重类别不平衡以及相邻评分等级之间的细微差异，在泄漏控制评估下仍然具有挑战性。

Conclusion: 该数据集为多语言（特别是俄语-哈萨克语）情感分析提供了有价值的资源，验证了Transformer模型在情感极性分类上的优势，同时指出了评分分类任务的主要困难在于类别不平衡和评分边界模糊。

Abstract: We present a new publicly available corpus of 100,502 movie reviews from Kazakhstan collected from kino.kz, spanning 2001-2025 and covering 4,943 unique titles. The dataset is multilingual, consisting mainly of Russian reviews alongside Kazakh and code-switched texts. Reviews are manually annotated for language and sentiment polarity, and 11,309 reviews additionally contain explicit user-provided ratings. We define two sentiment tasks -- three-way polarity classification and five-class score classification -- and benchmark classical BoW/TF-IDF baselines against multilingual transformer models (mBERT, XLM-RoBERTa, RemBERT). Experimental results show that transformer models consistently outperform classical baselines on polarity classification, while score classification remains challenging under leakage-controlled evaluation due to severe class imbalance and subtle distinctions between adjacent rating levels.

</details>


### [89] [PARD-2: Target-Aligned Parallel Draft Model for Dual-Mode Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08632)
*Zihao An,Taichi Liu,Ziqiong Liu,Dong Li,Ruofeng Liu,Emad Barsoum*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出PARD-2，一种双模式投机解码框架，通过置信度自适应令牌（CAT）优化重新加权令牌，将草稿模型优化目标从令牌预测准确性转向最大化接收长度，在Llama3.1-8B上实现高达6.94倍无损加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有草稿模型训练目标与推理时最大化连续令牌接收的目标不一致，缺乏针对接收长度的直接优化。

Method: 基于PARD提出PARD-2，采用置信度自适应令牌（CAT）优化，自适应地重新加权每个令牌以更好地对齐验证过程，并支持目标依赖和目标无关两种模式。

Result: 在多种模型和任务上，PARD-2实现高达6.94倍无损加速，在Llama3.1-8B上超越EAGLE-3 1.9倍、超越PARD 1.3倍。

Conclusion: 通过草稿模型训练目标重定义和CAT优化，PARD-2显著提升了投机解码的加速效果，且单一草稿模型支持双模式运行。

Abstract: Speculative decoding accelerates Large Language Models (LLMs) inference by using a lightweight draft model to propose candidate tokens that are verified in parallel by the target model. However, existing draft model training objectives are not directly aligned with the inference-time goal of maximizing consecutive token acceptance. To address this issue, we reformulate the draft model optimization objective, shifting the focus from token prediction accuracy to the overall acceptance length. In this paper, we build upon PARD to propose PARD-2, a dual-mode speculative decoding framework with Confidence-Adaptive Token (CAT) optimization. This approach adaptively reweights each token to better align with the verification process. Notably, PARD-2 enables a single draft model to support both target-dependent and target-independent modes. Experiments across diverse models and tasks demonstrate that PARD-2 achieves up to 6.94$\times$ lossless acceleration, surpassing EAGLE-3 by 1.9$\times$ and PARD by 1.3$\times$ on Llama3.1-8B. Our code is available at https://github.com/AMD-AGI/PARD.

</details>


### [90] [EdgeFlowerTune: Evaluating Federated LLM Fine-Tuning Under Realistic Edge System Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08636)
*Jiaxiang Geng,Yiyi Lu,Lunyu Zhao,Yan Gao,Nicholas D. Lane,Bing Luo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: EdgeFlowerTune是一个面向真实边缘系统约束的联邦LLM微调基准，联合评估模型质量与系统成本（通信、延迟、内存、能耗、鲁棒性），引入三种互补评估协议，并在真实Android手机和NVIDIA边缘开发板上实现。实验表明，仅看准确率会导致误导性结论，相似质量的方法在可部署性上可能存在显著差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦LLM微调研究大多基于跨孤岛或仿真设置，忽视了实际边缘设备的资源和运行时约束，导致方法的实际可部署性存疑。需要一种能同时评估模型质量与系统成本的真实设备基准。

Method: 提出EdgeFlowerTune基准，包含三种评估协议：Quality-under-Budget（预算下质量）、Cost-to-Target（达标成本）、Robustness（鲁棒性）；基于Flower和MobileFineTuner构建真实设备平台，覆盖商业Android手机和NVIDIA边缘开发板。

Result: 仅依赖准确率评估会产生误导性结论；具有相似最终质量的方法在考虑真实系统约束后，可部署性可能存在显著差异。

Conclusion: EdgeFlowerTune为联邦LLM微调在边缘场景下的系统感知评估提供了可复现的基准，揭示了考虑系统约束对于正确评估方法实用性的必要性。

Abstract: Federated fine-tuning offers a promising paradigm for adapting large language models (LLMs) on edge devices by leveraging the rich, diverse, and continuously generated data from smartphones and IoT devices without compromising user data privacy. Such edge-side adaptation can improve model personalization, robustness, and responsiveness to local contexts. However, the practical feasibility of federated LLM fine-tuning on real edge devices remains unclear, as most existing work focuses on cross-silo or simulation-based settings, overlooking the resource and runtime constraints that determine whether a method is deployable on real edge systems. We present EdgeFlowerTune, a deployment-oriented benchmark for federated LLM fine-tuning under realistic edge-system constraints. EdgeFlowerTune jointly evaluates model quality and system costs, including communication, wall-clock latency, memory usage, energy consumption, and robustness to dynamic edge conditions. To compare methods in terms of effectiveness, efficiency, and robustness, EdgeFlowerTune introduces three complementary protocols: Quality-under-Budget, Cost-to-Target, and Robustness. We instantiate EdgeFlowerTune as a real-device platform built on Flower and MobileFineTuner, spanning commercial Android smartphones and NVIDIA edge development boards. Our benchmark results show that accuracy-only evaluation can lead to misleading conclusions: methods with similar final quality may differ substantially in deployability once realistic system constraints are considered. EdgeFlowerTune provides a reproducible benchmark for system-aware evaluation of federated LLM fine-tuning at the edge.

</details>


### [91] [AgentCollabBench: Diagnosing When Good Agents Make Bad Collaborators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08647)
*Aritra Mazumder,Shubhashis Roy Dipta,Nusrat Jahan Lia,Tanzila Khan,Kainat Raisa Hossain,Nehaa Shri,Shubhrangshu Debsarkar,Humayra Tasnim,Gour Gupal Talukder Shawon,Debjoty Mitra,Sumaiya Ahmed Rani,Al Jami Islam Anik,Al Nafeu Khan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: AgentCollabBench是一个包含900个经人工验证任务的诊断基准，用于检测多智能体系统中因协作导致的四种行为风险（指令衰减、错误信念传染、上下文泄露、追踪持久性）。评估四种LLM后发现，模型存在特定漏洞，且通信拓扑是影响多跳信息存亡的关键因素，聚合节点会形成"合成瓶颈"丢弃少数约束。结论是多智能体可靠性本质上是结构问题，仅提升模型智能无法替代架构设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估方法仅关注最终输出结果，无法检测多智能体协作过程中推理链被静默破坏的问题（如约束被丢弃、错误通过共识传播等），因此需要一种能在部署前量化这些过程性漏洞的诊断基准。

Method: 构建了900个经人工验证的跨领域任务基准AgentCollabBench，每个任务针对四种行为风险之一。评估四种LLM（GPT 4.1 mini、Gemini 2.5 Flash Lite、Qwen-3.5-35B-A3B、Llama 3.1 8B Instruct），分析通信拓扑结构对信息存活的影响。

Result: 不同模型呈现特有的脆弱性分布（如Qwen在追踪持久性和指令稳定性上领先，GPT 4.1 mini在泄漏控制和错误信念抵抗上领先）；通信拓扑解释了7-40%的多跳信息存活方差；收敛DAG节点存在"合成瓶颈"，会丢弃少数分支携带的约束，而线性链中没有此结构。

Conclusion: 不合理的拓扑结构可以无声消除高性能模型的安全保障，多智能体系统的可靠性本质上是结构问题，仅提升模型智能无法替代架构设计。

Abstract: Multi-agent systems achieve state-of-the-art outcomes through peer collaboration. However, when an agent in the pipeline silently drops a constraint, the system's final output may look correct even though the reasoning chain was quietly corrupted, and existing outcome-based evaluations are blind to such multi-hop process failures. To make these vulnerabilities measurable before deployment, we introduce AgentCollabBench, a diagnostic benchmark of 900 human-validated tasks spanning software engineering, DevOps, and data engineering. Each task isolates one of four behavioral risks: instruction decay (does a constraint survive peer pressure?), false-belief contagion (does a falsehood spread through consensus?), context leakage (does information bleed between tasks?), and tracer durability (does marked data reach the final agent?). Evaluating four modern LLMs (GPT 4.1 mini, Gemini 2.5 Flash Lite, Qwen-3.5-35B-A3B, and Llama 3.1 8B Instruct), we expose model-specific vulnerability profiles invisible to outcome-only evaluation; Qwen-3.5-35B-A3B, for example, leads on tracer durability and instruction stability, while GPT 4.1 mini leads on leakage containment and false-belief resistance. Beyond per-model differences, communication topology emerges as a primary risk factor that explains 7-40% of the variance in multi-hop information survival. The effect traces to a synthesis bottleneck specific to converging-DAG nodes: an agent weighing competing parent inputs discards constraints carried by a minority branch, a bottleneck structurally absent from linear chains. AgentCollabBench demonstrates that suboptimal topology can silently erase the safeguards of highly capable models, arguing that multi-agent reliability is fundamentally a structural problem and that scaling model intelligence alone is no substitute for architecture.

</details>


### [92] [Hint Tuning: Less Data Makes Better Reasoners](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08665)
*Siqi Fan,Minghao Li,Xiaoqian Ma,Xiusheng Huang,Zhuo Chen,Bowen Qin,Liujie Zhang,Shuo Shang,Weihang Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Hint Tuning通过让推理模型根据问题难度动态调整推理深度，仅用1000条自标注数据，在Qwen3-Thinking和DeepSeek-R1-Distill等主流推理模型上实现平均31.5%的token缩减，同时保持竞争性准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大推理模型在链式推理中无论问题难易都生成5-8倍冗余token，造成计算浪费，而现有方法依赖大规模蒸馏数据或昂贵的强化学习，效率低下。

Method: 利用instruct模型作为难度探针：通过测试instruct模型在不同提示条件下的解题表现，自动将训练数据分为No-Hint（直接回答）、Sparse-Hint（最小前缀）和Full-Hint（完整推理）三类，将难度标注转化为可衡量的instruct模型与推理模型之间的一致性检查。

Result: 在Qwen3-Thinking和DeepSeek-R1-Distill等多个尺度的模型上实现24%-66%的token缩减（平均31.5%），在五个基准上保持竞争性准确率。

Conclusion: Hint Tuning通过简单的与instruct模型能力对齐的方式，比需要大规模蒸馏数据集或强化学习的方法更高效地实现了推理深度校准。

Abstract: Large reasoning models achieve high accuracy through extended chain-of-thought but generate 5--8 more tokens than necessary, applying verbose reasoning uniformly regardless of problem difficulty. We propose Hint Tuning, a data-efficient approach that teaches models to calibrate reasoning depth. Our key insight: the corresponding instruct model serves as an ideal difficulty probe. By testing what the instruct model can solve with varying guidance, we automatically construct training data across three states: No-Hint (direct answer), Sparse-Hint (minimal prefix), and Full-Hint (complete reasoning). This converts the abstract challenge of difficulty labeling into a measurable consistency check between the instruct and reasoning models. With only 1K self-annotated samples, Hint Tuning achieves 24--66% token reduction (31.5% average) across mainstream reasoning models (Qwen3-Thinking, DeepSeek-R1-Distill) at multiple scales (4B--32B) while maintaining competitive accuracy on five benchmarks. Unlike methods requiring massive distillation datasets or expensive RL, we achieve superior efficiency through simple alignment with the instruct model's capabilities.

</details>


### [93] [AgentForesight: Online Auditing for Early Failure Prediction in Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08715)
*Boxuan Zhang,Jianing Zhu,Zeru Shi,Dongfang Liu,Ruixiang Tang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出AgentForesight框架，将LLM多智能体系统中的错误检测从事后归因重构为在线审计。通过构建AFTraj-2K数据集和训练AgentForesight-7B模型（使用粗到细的强化学习），实现了在轨迹展开过程中实时检测首个决定性错误。该方法在多个基准上超越GPT-4.1和DeepSeek-V4-Pro，性能提升达+19.9%，步骤定位误差降低3倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将多智能体系统中的错误检测视为事后归因（post-hoc failure attribution），即在整条轨迹结束后才诊断错误步骤和负责智能体。这种范式丧失了在轨迹执行过程中进行干预的机会，无法在错误发生的第一时间阻止其级联扩散。

Method: 1) 构建AFTraj-2K数据集，覆盖编码、数学和智能体领域，通过严格筛选保留安全轨迹，并通过多LLM评审共识标注不安全轨迹的决定性错误步骤。2) 提出AgentForesight-7B模型，采用粗到细的强化学习训练策略：先训练风险预判先验（在相邻安全/不安全前缀对上的错误边界学习），再通过三轴奖励（什么、哪里、谁）精化为精确的步骤级定位能力。

Result: 在AFTraj-2K和外部Who&When基准上，AgentForesight-7B超越GPT-4.1和DeepSeek-V4-Pro等领先商业模型，性能提升最高达+19.9%，步骤定位误差降低3倍。

Conclusion: AgentForesight将多智能体系统的错误检测从事后分析转变为在线审计，在轨迹执行过程中即可识别首个决定性错误，为运行时干预提供了可能，标志着从被动诊断到主动监控的范式转变。

Abstract: LLM-based multi-agent systems are increasingly deployed on long-horizon tasks, but a single decisive error is often accepted by downstream agents and cascades into trajectory-level failure. Existing work frames this as \emph{post-hoc failure attribution}, diagnosing the responsible agent and step after the trajectory has ended. However, this paradigm forfeits any opportunity to intervene while trajectory is still unfolding. In this work, we introduce AgentForesight, a framework that reframes this problem as online auditing: at each step of an unfolding trajectory, an auditor observes only the current prefix and must either continue the run or alarm at the earliest decisive error, without access to future steps. To this end, we curate AFTraj-2K, a corpus of agentic trajectories across Coding, Math, and Agentic domains, in which safe trajectories are retained under a strict curation pipeline and unsafe trajectories are annotated at the step of their decisive error via consensus among multiple LLM judges. Built on that, we develop AgentForesight-7B, a compact online auditor trained with a coarse-to-fine reinforcement learning recipe that first equips it with a risk-anticipation prior at the failure boundary on adjacent safe/unsafe prefix pairs, then sharpens this prior into precise step-level localization under a three-axis reward jointly targeting the what, where, and who of an audit verdict. Across AFTraj-2K and an external Who\&When benchmark, AgentForesight-7B outperforms leading proprietary models, including GPT-4.1 and DeepSeek-V4-Pro, achieving up to +19.9% performance gain and 3$\times$ lower step localization error, opening the loop from post-hoc failures detection to enabling deployment-time intervention. Project page: https://zbox1005.github.io/agent-foresight/

</details>


### [94] [Breaking the Impasse: Dual-Scale Evolutionary Policy Training for Social Language Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08721)
*Minzheng Wang,Run Luo,Yanbo Wang,Zichen Liu,Yuqiao Tan,Tao Tan,Xu Nan,Yinhe Zheng,Wenji Mao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对开放型社交语言游戏中的策略同质化收敛问题（演化停滞），本文提出双尺度演化策略训练（DEPT），通过时间尺度演化感知机制检测策略崩溃，并利用非对称优势重塑动态调节优化空间，恢复梯度信号以维持持续的策略探索，实验证明其有效避免了策略退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将基于可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）扩展到开放型社交语言游戏的自博弈训练时，智能体会因策略空间巨大而收敛到同质化行为，导致对局结果确定性增强、策略演化所需的梯度信号消失，即演化停滞问题。

Method: 提出DEPT框架，包含：(1) 时间尺度演化感知机制，通过量化双尺度价值基线散度与对局熵值检测策略崩溃；(2) 非对称优势重塑，在检测到策略崩溃后动态调节优化景观以进行干预，恢复梯度信号并强制持续策略探索。

Result: 在多个社交语言游戏上的实验表明，DEPT优于强基线方法，有效避免了策略退化，驱动了社交语言智能体的持续演化。

Conclusion: DEPT通过感知与干预机制解决了开放型社交语言游戏中的演化停滞问题，为RLVR在复杂社交场景的应用提供了可行路径。

Abstract: While Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) has proven effective for closed-ended tasks, extending it to open-ended social language games via self-play reveals a critical issue: evolution impasse. Due to the vast strategy space, language agents frequently converge to homogenized behaviors, leading to deterministic match outcomes that eliminate the gradient signals necessary for policy evolution. To tackle this issue, we propose Dual-scale Evolutionary Policy Training (DEPT) for social language games. DEPT introduces a time-scaled evolutionary perception mechanism that detects impasse by quantifying dual-scale value baseline divergence alongside match entropy. Upon perceiving the collapse, it then activates asymmetric advantage reshaping to dynamically modulate the optimization landscape for intervention. Thus, our method effectively restores gradient signals and enforces sustained strategic exploration. Extensive experiments on multiple social language games demonstrate that DEPT outperforms strong baselines, avoiding policy degeneration and driving the continuous evolution of social language agents.

</details>


### [95] [Training with Harnesses: On-Policy Harness Self-Distillation for Complex Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08741)
*Zhengyang Zhao,Lu Ma,Wentao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出On-Policy Harness Self-Distillation（OPHSD）方法，通过将推理时增强工作流（harness）作为教师进行自蒸馏，将外部工作流的能力内部化到基础模型中，使得模型在无需推理时工作流的情况下仍能保持强性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理时增强（inference-time harness）虽能提升LLM在复杂推理任务上的表现，但基础模型本身的固有能力并未改变。现有方法依赖外部工作流作为永久组件，但工作流带来的能力无法内化到模型自身中，存在效率与泛化性的瓶颈。

Method: 采用on-policy的自蒸馏框架：使用当前模型结合harness（如draft-verify、plan-solve）作为教师，生成监督信号，让学生模型仅靠自身进行学习。教师与学生共享同一初始模型参数，但教师额外接入harness进行推理。

Result: 在文本分类的draft-verify和数学推理的plan-solve任务上，OPHSD一致优于强基线（如相较OPSD在HMMT25上提升+10.83%）。分析表明，推理时重新挂载harness不仅无额外收益，甚至可能降低性能。

Conclusion: 复杂的推理时工作流无需作为永久组件；它们可以作为临时的训练脚手架，其收益可以永久地反馈回基础模型中。

Abstract: Inference-time harnesses substantially improve large language models on complex reasoning tasks. However, the intrinsic capabilities of the underlying model remain unchanged by the addition of these external workflows. To bridge this gap, we introduce \emph{On-Policy Harness Self-Distillation} (OPHSD), which employs the harness-augmented current model as a teacher for self-distillation, thereby introducing extra supervisory signals from the harness beyond training data. OPHSD internalizes task-specific harness capabilities into the student model, yielding robust generalizability and strong standalone performance across diverse reasoning tasks. Evaluated across draft--verify harness for text classification and plan--solve for mathematical reasoning tasks, OPHSD consistently outperforms strong baselines (e.g., +10.83\% over OPSD on HMMT25). Our analysis further indicates that reattaching the harness during inference yields no additional benefits and can even degrade performance, suggesting that complex harnesses need not always be permanent fixtures; instead, they can serve as temporary training scaffolds whose benefits are permanently fed back into the base model. Our code and training data are available at https://github.com/zzy1127/OPHSD-On-Policy-Harness-Self-Distillation.

</details>


### [96] [Narrative Landscape: Mapping Narrative Dispositions Across LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08742)
*Donghoon Jung,Jiwoo Choi,Songeun Chae,Seohyon Jung*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一个量化框架，通过重复、受控的 elicitation 实验，将LLM的倾向（disposition）刻画为稳定且模型特有的输出规律。基于六种前沿模型和三种指令类型，在结构化叙事约束选择任务中，通过Jaccard相似度衡量一致性、逆Simpson指数衡量多样性，并引入Narrative Landscape进行PCA可视化。结果揭示了模型家族间的“刚性-探索”谱系，且指令类型会改变选择空间的几何结构，即使标量指标相似，其背后的选择拓扑也可能存在质的差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究对LLM的输出倾向缺乏系统性的量化刻画方法，难以区分哪些是稳定、模型特有的规律，哪些是任务或指令变化的偶然表现。作者希望建立一个通用框架，能够跨模型、跨指令类型地定量描述和比较LLM的“性格”或“倾向”。

Method: 使用结构化叙事约束选择任务（Narrative constraint-selection task），对六种前沿模型（frontier models）在三种指令类型下进行重复、受控的 elicitation。用Jaccard相似度衡量跨复现的选择重叠度（一致性），用逆Simpson指数衡量选项间分散程度（多样性）。进一步引入Narrative Landscape，基于PCA将各模型的选择剖面投射到共享空间进行可视化比较。

Result: 发现了跨模型家族的“刚性-探索”谱系（rigidity-exploration spectrum）；指令类型会改变选择空间的几何结构（geometry of selection spaces），即使标量指标（如一致性、多样性得分）看起来相近，其背后的选择拓扑（selection topologies）可能存在质的差异。

Conclusion: LLM的倾向可以通过“一致性”和“多样性”两个维度进行有效量化；模型家族之间存在系统性差异，且指令类型对选择行为的影响不能仅靠标量指标捕捉，需要结合空间几何分析才能全面理解。

Abstract: This study proposes a quantitative framework for profiling LLM dispositions as stable, model-specific regularities in output under repeated, controlled elicitation. Using a structured narrative constraint-selection task administered across six frontier models and three instruction types, we operationalize disposition through two dimensions: "consistency", measured as cross-replication selection overlap via Jaccard similarity, and "diversity", measured as dispersion across options via the inverse Simpson index. We further introduce Narrative Landscape, a PCA-based visualization that maps each model's selection profile into a shared space for direct comparison. Results reveal a clear rigidity-exploration spectrum across model families and show that instruction types shift the geometry of selection spaces even when scalar metrics appear similar, indicating that comparable scores can mask qualitatively distinct selection topologies.

</details>


### [97] [SimReg: Achieving Higher Performance in the Pretraining via Embedding Similarity Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08809)
*Yan Sun,Guoxia Wang,Jinle Zeng,JiaBin Yang,Shuai Li,Li Shen,Dacheng Tao,DianHai Yu,Haifeng Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出SimReg——一种嵌入相似度正则化损失函数，通过拉近同标签token表示、推开异标签token表示来提升大语言模型预训练效率和零样本性能，在稠密和MoE架构上实现30%+训练加速和1%+下游性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于下一个token预测的大语言模型预训练中，token嵌入具有上下文依赖性，导致类内方差高、类间相似度高，阻碍了表示学习效率。虽然相似度正则化在监督微调和分类任务中有效，但在大规模LLM预训练中的应用和效果尚未被充分探索。

Method: 提出SimReg损失函数：一方面通过相似度正则化鼓励同一序列中相同真实标签的token表示更相似，另一方面通过对比损失强制不同标签的token表示相互分离。该机制通过增大多分类间隔来实现更高效的分类学习。

Result: 在稠密和MoE两种架构的实验中，SimReg一致地实现了超过30%的训练收敛加速，并在标准零样本下游基准上平均提升超过1%的性能。消融实验和分析提供了超参数调优和损失有效性的实用见解。

Conclusion: 嵌入相似度正则化在大规模LLM预训练中有效，通过扩大分类间隔提升了表示学习效率和模型性能，且适用于不同架构（稠密和MoE）。

Abstract: Pretraining large language models (LLMs) with next-token prediction has led to remarkable advances, yet the context-dependent nature of token embeddings in such models results in high intra-class variance and inter-class similarity, thus hindering the efficiency of representation learning. While similarity-based regularization has demonstrated benefit in supervised fine-tuning and classification tasks, its application and efficacy in large-scale LLM pretraining remains underexplored. In this work, we propose the SimReg, an embedding similarity regularization loss that explicitly encourages token representations with the same ground-truth label within each sequence to be more similar, while enforcing separation from different-label tokens via a contrastive loss. Our analysis reveals that this mechanism introduces gains by enlarging multi-classification margins, thereby enabling more efficient classification. Extensive experiments across dense and Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures demonstrate that SimReg consistently accelerates training convergence by over 30% and improves average zero-shot downstream performance by over 1% across standard benchmarks. Further ablation studies and analyses offer practical insights into hyperparameter tuning and loss effectiveness.

</details>


### [98] [Generating Leakage-Free Benchmarks for Robust RAG Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08838)
*Jiayi Liu,Jiaxing Zhang,Bowen Jin,Jennifer Neville*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出SeedRG，一种半合成基准生成流水线，通过从种子数据集中提取推理图并进行类型约束的实体替换，生成结构相似但新颖的实例，同时引入推理图一致性检查和知识泄漏过滤，以缓解RAG评估中的知识泄漏和基准老化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG基准数据集中许多问题无需检索即可从LLM参数记忆中得到答案（知识泄漏），且基准随时间老化导致评估不可靠。

Method: SeedRG从种子基准数据集中提取问题-上下文对的推理图，通过类型约束的实体替换生成新实例，并采用推理图一致性检查和知识泄漏过滤器两步骤确保质量。

Result: 生成的实例在保留原始推理模式的同时，不太可能存在于模型的参数知识中，有效缓解了知识泄漏问题。

Conclusion: SeedRG能够生成可靠的RAG评估基准，解决知识泄漏和基准老化问题。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) is widely used to augment large language models (LLMs) with external knowledge. However, many benchmark datasets, designed to test RAG performance, comprise many questions that can already be answered from an LLM's parametric memory. This leads to unreliable evaluation. We refer to this phenomenon as knowledge leakage: cases where RAG tasks are solvable without retrieval. This issue worsens over time due to benchmark aging. As benchmarks are reused for training, their contents are increasingly absorbed into model parameters, making them less effective for evaluating retrieval.
  We introduce SeedRG, a semi-synthetic benchmark generation pipeline that mitigates knowledge leakage and addresses the issue of benchmark aging. Starting from a seed benchmark dataset, SeedRG extracts a reasoning graph from question-context pairs to capture their underlying reasoning structure, and then generates new examples via type-constrained entity replacement. This process produces structurally similar but novel instances that are unlikely to exist in the model's parametric knowledge, while preserving the original reasoning patterns. To ensure quality, we incorporate two verification steps: (1) a reasoning-graph consistency check to maintain task difficulty, and (2) a knowledge-leakage filter to exclude instances answerable without retrieval.

</details>


### [99] [ReST-KV: Robust KV Cache Eviction with Layer-wise Output Reconstruction and Spatial-Temporal Smoothing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08840)
*Yongqi An,Chang Lu,Kuan Zhu,Tao Yu,Chaoyang Zhao,Hong Wu,Ming Tang,Jinqiao Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出ReST-KV，一种结合逐层输出重建和时空平滑的KV缓存驱逐方法，通过优化问题形式化KV驱逐，在长上下文基准上超越SOTA，解码延迟降低10.61倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有KV驱逐方法通常保留高注意力权重的KV对，但忽视了token移除导致的注意力再分配效应以及KV选择中的时空动态性。

Method: 将KV缓存驱逐建模为最小化输出差异的优化问题，通过逐层重建直接建模每个token移除对模型输出的影响，捕捉注意力再分配效应；设计指数移动平均平滑处理时间变化，自适应窗口机制捕捉空间模式。

Result: 在LongBench上超越SOTA 2.58%，RULER上超越15.2%，在Needle-in-a-Haystack和InfiniteBench上持续超越现有方法，128k上下文长度下解码延迟降低10.61倍。

Conclusion: ReST-KV通过综合考虑输出重建和时空平滑，为KV缓存驱逐提供了更全面的视角，显著提升了长上下文推理效率。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) face growing challenges in efficient generative inference due to the increasing memory demands of Key-Value (KV) caches, especially for long sequences. Existing eviction methods typically retain KV pairs with high attention weights but overlook the impact of attention redistribution caused by token removal, as well as the spatial-temporal dynamics in KV selection. In this paper, we propose ReST-KV, a robust KV eviction method that combines layer-wise output Reconstruction and Spatial-Temporal smoothing to provide a more comprehensive perspective for the KV cache eviction task. Specifically, ReST-KV formulates KV cache eviction as an optimization problem that minimizes output discrepancies through efficient layer-wise reconstruction. By directly modeling how each token's removal affects the model output, our method naturally captures attention redistribution effects, going beyond simplistic reliance on raw attention weights. To further enhance robustness, we design exponential moving average smoothing to handle temporal variations and an adaptive window-based mechanism to capture spatial patterns. Our method, ReST-KV, significantly advances performance on long-context benchmarks. It surpasses state-of-the-art baselines by 2.58% on LongBench and 15.2% on RULER. Additionally, ReST-KV consistently outperforms existing methods on Needle-in-a-Haystack and InfiniteBench, all while achieving a remarkable 10.61$\times$ reduction in decoding latency at 128k context length. The code is publicly available at https://github.com/an-yongqi/rest-kv to facilitate reproducibility and further research.

</details>


### [100] [XPERT: Expert Knowledge Transfer for Effective Training of Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08842)
*Chang Liu,Boyu Shi,Xu Yang,Xin Geng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出XPERT框架，从预训练的MoE大语言模型中提取、整合并复用跨领域专家知识，以支持不同规模语言模型的高效训练，实验表明复用专家知识可提升模型性能并加速收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 观察到MoE模型中部分专家在不同知识领域中被一致激活，这些跨领域专家编码了与模型泛化密切相关的通用知识，因此探索如何从预训练的MoE LLMs中识别并复用此类结构化专家知识。

Method: 提出XPERT框架：(1) 通过仅推理分析识别跨领域专家；(2) 利用张量分解精炼专家表示；(3) 将提取的知识适配到下游模型中进行复用。

Result: 在语言理解和对话生成基准上，复用专家知识的模型相比强基线在性能和收敛速度上均取得一致提升。

Conclusion: MoE大语言模型可作为结构化的可复用知识源，专家层面的知识复用在提升模型训练效果方面具有重要价值。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) language models organize knowledge into explicitly routed expert modules, making expert-level representations traceable and analyzable. By analyzing expert activation patterns in MoE large language models (LLMs), we find that a subset of experts is consistently activated across diverse knowledge domains. These common experts encode cross-domain, generalizable knowledge that is closely related to model generalization, naturally raising the question of how such identifiable expert knowledge can be practically reused. Motivated by this observation, we propose XPERT, a framework that extracts, consolidates, and reuses expert knowledge from pre-trained MoE LLMs to support more effective training of language models across different model scales. XPERT identifies cross-domain experts via inference-only analysis, refines their representations through tensor decomposition, and adapts the extracted knowledge to reuse in downstream models. Experiments on language understanding and dialogue generation benchmarks show that models benefiting from reused expert knowledge achieve consistently stronger performance and faster convergence compared to strong baselines. These results highlight MoE LLMs as structured and reusable knowledge sources, and demonstrate the value of expert-level knowledge reuse for improving model training.

</details>


### [101] [EmoS: A High-Fidelity Multimodal Benchmark for Fine-grained Streaming Emotional Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08847)
*Pengze Guo,Jingxi Liang,Zhiwen Xie,Qifeng Wang,Derek F. Wong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: EmoS是一个高保真双语基准数据集，通过静态切片与动态独白子集结合、双层人工标注管道，解决了现有情绪数据集生态效度低、噪声大、细粒度标注不可靠的问题。微调多模态大语言模型在该数据集上显著优于零样本基线，为未来情绪识别与共情模型的训练与评估奠定基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前高压老龄化社会对大规模情感模型的需求日益迫切，但现有基准无法同时实现生态效度、信号清晰度和可靠的细粒度标注，亟需一个高质量的情感数据集来支撑情绪识别与共情模型的训练与评估。

Method: 构建EmoS双语基准数据集，采用严格筛选的静态切片与动态Streaming Monologue子集相结合的方式，并配备双层人工标注管道以获得可信的细粒度连续情绪演化标注。

Result: 在EmoS上微调多模态大语言模型，相比零样本基线取得了显著的性能提升。

Conclusion: EmoS能够有效解决现有数据集的生态效度和噪声问题，为未来情绪识别模型和共情模型的训练与评估提供了可靠的基础，数据集与代码已开源。

Abstract: In the context of today's high-pressure, aging society, the demand for large-scale emotional models capable of providing empathetic support is more critical than ever. However, existing benchmarks fail to simultaneously achieve ecological validity, signal clarity, and reliable fine-grained labeling. We introduce EmoS, a high-fidelity bilingual benchmark designed to resolve the limitations of ecological validity and noise in existing datasets by combining strictly filtered static slices with a dynamic Streaming Monologue subset. Supported by a rigorous dual-layer human annotation pipeline, EmoS provides trusted ground truth that captures continuous emotional evolution. Empirical results show that fine-tuning MLLMs (multimodal large language models) on EmoS yields significant gains over zero-shot baselines, laying the foundation for the training and evaluation of future emotion recognition models and empathy models. The dataset and code are publicly available at https://github.com/NLP2CT/EmoS.

</details>


### [102] [Max-pooling Network Revisited: Analyzing the Role of Semantic Probability in Multiple Instance Learning for Hallucination Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08863)
*Shota Fujikawa,Issei Sato*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需语义一致性计算的高效幻觉检测方法，通过最大池化聚合token级特征并利用轻量级MLP直接估计句子分数，在保持与最先进方法竞争性能的同时大幅提升效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的混合幻觉检测方法（如HaMI）虽性能优异，但存在重复采样和语义相似性计算带来的巨大计算开销，亟需在保证检测性能的前提下降低计算成本。

Method: 首先对HaMI进行理论分析，揭示其通过缩放内部状态与语义一致性来扩大决策边界的机制；受此启发，从边际扩大的角度重新审视经典句子分类模型，通过最大池化聚合token级特征，再使用轻量级MLP直接估计句子分数，完全避免了语义一致性计算。

Result: 在无需语义一致性计算的情况下，该方法实现了显著的效率提升，同时保持了与最先进基线方法具有竞争力的检测性能。

Conclusion: 通过自适应聚合内部特征表示，可以在不依赖语义一致性计算的情况下实现高效且有效的幻觉检测。

Abstract: Hallucination detection has become increasingly important for improving the reliability of large language models (LLMs). Recently, hybrid approaches such as HaMI, which combine semantic consistency with internal model states via Multiple Instance Learning (MIL), have achieved state-of-the-art performance. However, these methods incur substantial computational overhead due to repeated sampling and costly semantic similarity computations. In this work, we first provide a theoretical analysis of HaMI in terms of decision margins, revealing that scaling internal states with semantic consistency leads to an enlarged decision margin. Motivated by this insight, we revisit classical sentence classification models from a margin enlargement perspective, aggregating token-level features via max pooling and directly estimating sentence scores using a lightweight MLP. Without requiring semantic consistency computations, our approach achieves substantial efficiency improvements while maintaining competitive performance with state-of-the-art baselines through adaptive aggregation of internal feature representations.

</details>


### [103] [DocScope: Benchmarking Verifiable Reasoning for Trustworthy Long-Document Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08888)
*Xiang Feng,Jiawei Zhou,Zhangfeng Huang,Kewei Wang,Shanshan Ye,Jinxin Hu,Zulong Chen,Yong Luo,Jing Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: DocScope是一个评估多模态大语言模型在长文档QA中推理轨迹正确性的基准，包含1124个问题/273篇文档，采用四阶段解耦评估协议。实验发现：正确答案中完整证据链仅29%，区域定位是最弱环节，跨距离证据聚合是主要难点，激活参数量比总规模更重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估仅关注最终答案准确性，无法衡量多模态大语言模型在处理长视觉丰富文档时是否产生可信、可验证的推理过程，需要更细粒度的评估方法。

Method: 设计四阶段评估协议：页面定位、区域定位、事实提取、答案验证，通过阶段间解耦独立审计每个层级；构建包含1124个问题/273篇文档的基准，所有层次证据由人工标注；通过人类对齐研究选择和校准评判者。

Result: 正确答案中完整证据链最高仅29%；区域定位是最弱环节；主要难点来自跨长距离和多文档簇的证据聚合；Oracle研究表明可信感知和事实提取是主导能力瓶颈；激活参数量比总规模更重要。

Conclusion: 答案准确性不能替代轨迹级评估，需要更细粒度的推理过程审计来真正衡量模型在长文档理解中的可信推理能力。

Abstract: Evaluating whether Multimodal Large Language Models can produce trustworthy, verifiable reasoning over long, visually rich documents requires evaluation beyond end-to-end answer accuracy. We introduce DocScope, a benchmark that formulates long-document QA as a structured reasoning trajectory prediction problem: given a complete PDF document and a question, the model outputs evidence pages, supporting evidence regions, relevant factual statements, and a final answer. We design a four-stage evaluation protocol -- Page Localization, Region Grounding, Fact Extraction, and Answer Verification -- that audits each level of the trajectory independently through inter-stage decoupling, with all judges selected and calibrated via human alignment studies. DocScope comprises 1,124 questions derived from 273 documents, with all hierarchical evidence annotations completed by human annotators. We benchmark 6 proprietary models, 12 open-weight models, and several domain-specific systems. Our experiments reveal that answer accuracy cannot substitute for trajectory-level evaluation: even among correct answers, the highest observed rate of complete evidence chains is only 29\%. Across all models, region grounding remains the weakest trajectory stage. Furthermore, the primary difficulty stems from aggregating evidence dispersed across long distances and multiple document clusters, while an oracle study identifies faithful perception and fact extraction as the dominant capability bottleneck. Cross-architecture comparisons further suggest that activated parameter count matters more than total scale. The benchmark and code will be publicly released at https://github.com/MiliLab/DocScope.

</details>


### [104] [FragileFlow: Spectral Control of Correct-but-Fragile Predictions for Foundation Model Robustness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08896)
*Zhuoyun Li,Boxuan Wang,Jinwei Hu,Xiaowei Huang,Yi Dong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出FragileFlow正则化方法，通过校准边缘缓冲区识别正确但脆弱的预测，并利用类间脆弱风险矩阵组织概率质量，首次给出该误差流的PAC-Bayes上界，在多选LLM基准和少样本CLIP适应中提升了最差类鲁棒性并保持干净准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有鲁棒性评估依赖平均准确率或平均一致性，这掩盖了一种结构化失效模式：预测看似正确，但概率质量已从真实类流向决策边界附近的系统性错误竞争者。需要一种能捕捉这种边缘感知误差流的新方法。

Method: 提出FragileFlow，一种即插即用正则化器，利用校准边缘缓冲区识别正确但脆弱的预测，将离类概率质量组织成类级脆弱风险矩阵。理论上首次给出该边缘感知误差流对象的PAC-Bayes上界，证明在稳定性条件下经验谱控制能提供确定性最差类鲁棒性的保守路径。

Result: 在多选LLM基准和少样本CLIP适应实验中，FragileFlow一致提升了理论面向的风险度量，在大多数设置中提高了扰动下最差类准确率，并保持了干净准确率。

Conclusion: FragileFlow能有效捕捉和缓解平均指标掩盖的结构化失败模式，在不牺牲干净性能的前提下提升最差类鲁棒性，理论分析与实验结果一致。

Abstract: Robust adaptation of LLMs and VLMs is often evaluated by average accuracy or average consistency under perturbations. However, these averages can hide a structured failure mode: a prediction may remain correct while probability mass already flows from particular true classes toward systematic wrong competitors near the decision boundary. In this paper, we formalize this phenomenon as margin-aware error flow and introduce FragileFlow, a plug-in regularizer that uses a calibrated margin buffer to identify correct-but-fragile predictions and organize their off-class probability mass into a class-wise vulnerable-risk matrix. Theoretically, we provide the first PAC-Bayes upper bound for this margin-aware error-flow object, showing how empirical spectral control yields a conservative route to deterministic worst-class robustness under a stability condition. Experiments on multiple-choice LLM benchmarks and few-shot CLIP adaptation show that FragileFlow consistently improves the proposed theory-facing risk measures over matched baselines, yields perturbed worst-class accuracy gains in most settings, and preserves clean accuracy across comparisons.

</details>


### [105] [LLM-Agnostic Semantic Representation Attack](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08898)
*Jiawei Lian,Jianhong Pan,Lefan Wang,Yi Wang,Tairan Huang,Shaohui Mei,Lap-Pui Chau*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于语义表示的攻击方法（SRA），通过将对抗目标从精确文本匹配转变为恶意语义表示，实现了对LLM跨模型的高成功率攻击（平均99.71%），并具有强迁移性和隐蔽性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于token级优化的对抗攻击方法存在收敛性差、提示自然度低、跨模型泛化能力弱等瓶颈，亟需一种从语义层面实现攻击的新范式。

Method: 提出语义表示攻击（SRA）框架，包含理论上的语义连贯-收敛关系及跨模型语义泛化界，技术上通过语义表示启发式搜索（SRHS）算法，在保持可解释性和结构连贯性的前提下，逐步扩展离散token块来实现攻击。

Result: 在26个开源LLM上平均攻击成功率达到99.71%，具备强迁移性和隐蔽性。

Conclusion: SRA通过转向语义级别的对抗目标，有效克服了传统token级优化方法的固有限制，在攻击成功率、跨模型迁移性和隐蔽性方面均显著优于现有方法。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) increasingly employ alignment techniques to prevent harmful outputs. Despite these safeguards, attackers can circumvent them by crafting adversarial prompts. Predominant token-level optimization methods primarily rely on optimizing for exact affirmative templates (e.g., ``\textit{Sure, here is...}''). However, these paradigms frequently encounter bottlenecks such as suboptimal convergence, compromised prompt naturalness, and poor cross-model generalization. To address these limitations, we propose Semantic Representation Attack (SRA), a novel LLM-agnostic paradigm that fundamentally reconceptualizes adversarial objectives from exact textual targeting to malicious semantic representations. Theoretically, we establish the semantic Coherence-Convergence Relationship and derive a Cross-Model Semantic Generalization bound, proving that maintaining semantic coherence guarantees both white-box semantic convergence and black-box transferability. Technically, we operationalize this framework via the Semantic Representation Heuristic Search (SRHS) algorithm, which preserves interpretability and structural coherence of the adversarial prompts during incremental discrete token chunk expansion. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that our framework achieves a 99.71% average attack success rate across 26 open-source LLMs, with strong transferability and stealth.

</details>


### [106] [Decomposing and Steering Functional Metacognition in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08942)
*Yanshi Li,Xueru Bai,Shuman Liu,Haibo Zhang,Anxiang Zeng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出大语言模型内部存在可分解的功能性元认知状态空间（包括评估意识、自我能力评估、风险感知、计算资源分配、受众适应、意图性等），通过残差流分析证明这些状态可从内部激活中线性解码且具有分层特征，并通过引导激活因果调节推理行为。研究认为基准测试不仅反映任务能力，还反映了元认知状态的激活状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究发现LLM表现出评估上下文意识并扭曲性能测量，但尚不清楚这种现象是单一行为伪影还是模型内部更深层次的结构。作者希望通过揭示模型内部可分解的元认知状态空间来回答这一问题。

Method: 1) 提出功能性元认知状态空间概念，定义评估意识、自我能力评估、风险感知、计算资源分配、受众适应、意图性等内部变量；2) 通过残差流分析多个推理模型，证明这些状态可从内部激活中线性解码并具有分层特征；3) 沿探针导出方向引导模型激活，验证每个元认知状态因果性地区分调节推理行为（影响冗长度、准确性和安全性响应）。

Result: 元认知状态可从内部激活中线性解码，具有不同的层分布特征；通过因果干预可分别调节模型的行为表现，包括冗长度、准确性和安全相关响应；证明基准性能不仅受任务能力影响，还受特定元认知状态激活程度的影响。

Conclusion: 理解和控制这些内部功能性元认知状态对于可靠评估和部署推理模型至关重要；本文为研究人工系统中的功能性元认知提供了机械论框架。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) increasingly exhibit behaviors suggesting awareness of their evaluation context, often adapting their reasoning strategies in benchmark settings. Prior work has shown that such evaluation awareness can distort performance measurements; however, it remains unclear whether this phenomenon reflects a single behavioral artifact or a deeper internal structure within the model.
  We propose that LLMs maintain a decomposable space of functional metacognitive states: internal variables encoding factors such as evaluation awareness, self-assessed capability, perceived risk, computational effort allocation, audience expertise adaptation, and intentionality. Through residual stream analysis across multiple reasoning models, we demonstrate that these states are linearly decodable from internal activations and exhibit distinct layer-wise profiles. Moreover, by steering model activations along probe-derived directions, we show that each functional metacognitive state causally modulates reasoning behavior in dissociable ways, affecting verbosity, accuracy, and safety-related responses across tasks.
  Our findings suggest that benchmark performance reflects not only task competence but also the activation of specific functional metacognitive states. We argue that understandi ng and controlling these internal states is essential for reliable evaluation and deployment of reasoning models, and we provide a mechanistic framework for studying functional m etacognition in artificial systems. Our code and data are publicly available at https://github.com/xlands/meta-cognition.

</details>


### [107] [Improving Lexical Difficulty Prediction with Context-Aligned Contrastive Learning and Ridge Ensembling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08950)
*Wicaksono Leksono Muhamad,Joanito Agili Lopo,Tsamarah Rana Nugraha,Ahmad Cahyono Adi,Muhammad Oriza Nurfajri*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Context-Aligned Contrastive Regression方法，融合岭回归集成与两种对比学习目标（跨视角上下文对比与序数软对比学习），解决词汇难度预测中跨语言对齐和序数结构问题，在三个L1数据集上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有词汇难度预测方法仅依赖标量监督的回归训练，未能显式构建表征空间结构，限制了模型捕捉跨语言对齐和序数难度的能力。

Method: 提出Context-Aligned Contrastive Regression，包含三个核心组件：(i) Ridge回归集成，(ii) Cross-View Context对比学习，(iii) Ordinal Soft Contrastive Learning（序数软对比学习）。

Result: 在三个L1数据集上实验结果表明：(i)对比学习目标改善了跨语言表征对齐并保留语言特定细节，(ii)所学表征捕捉了词汇难度的序数结构，(iii)集成方法有效缓解了单个模型的系统偏差，在不同难度级别上表现更稳定。

Conclusion: 该研究通过引入对比学习与集成策略，克服了传统回归方法的表征空间结构缺失问题，验证了对比目标对跨语言对齐和序数难度建模的有效性。

Abstract: Lexical difficulty prediction is a fundamental problem in language learning and readability assessment, requiring models to estimate word difficulty across different first-language (L1) backgrounds. However, existing approaches rely on regression-only training with scalar supervision, which does not explicitly structure the representation space, limiting their ability to capture cross-lingual alignment and ordinal difficulty. To mitigate these issues, we propose Context-Aligned Contrastive Regression, which integrates Ridge regression ensemble with two complementary objectives, i.e., Cross-View Context and Ordinal Soft Contrastive Learning. Experiments on three L1 datasets show that (i) contrastive objectives improve cross-lingual representation alignment while preserving language-specific nuances, (ii) the learned representations capture the ordinal structure of lexical difficulty, and (iii) the ensemble effectively mitigates systematic biases of individual models, leading to more stable performance across difficulty levels.

</details>


### [108] [Dolphin-CN-Dialect: Where Chinese Dialects Matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08961)
*Yangyang Meng,Huihang Zhong,Guodong Lin,Guanbo Wang,Hu Du,Zhiming Shao,Yukai Huang,Ke Li,Wei-Qiang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Dolphin-CN-Dialect 是一个面向中文及方言场景的流式 ASR 模型，通过温度采样策略平衡普通话与低资源方言数据、重新设计分词器（中文字符级+英文子词级+可扩展方言token），在方言识别和CER上优于前代Dolphin，并与近期SOTA开源模型性能相当，同时模型体积更小，支持流式/非流式推理、热词定制和硬件优化部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决中文方言场景下ASR面临的方言数据高度不平衡问题，以及前代模型在方言识别上的不足，同时兼顾流式推理的低延迟与高精度需求。

Method: 1) 温度采样策略：基于温度参数调整方言数据的采样概率，平衡标准普通话与低资源方言；2) 分词器重新设计：中文采用字符级建模，英文采用子词建模，引入可扩展方言token以更好对齐语言特性；3) 数据预处理、训练稳定性及数据采样策略上的全面改进。

Result: 方言识别准确率提升，CER相比前代Dolphin降低；与近期SOTA开源ASR模型相比达到竞争性性能，同时保持显著更小的模型体积。

Conclusion: Dolphin-CN-Dialect 通过数据采样、分词器设计等改进，在保持小模型体积和流式能力的同时，实现了中文方言ASR领域的强竞争力表现，为实际多方言ASR应用提供了实用且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: We present Dolphin-CN-Dialect, a streaming-capable ASR model with a focus on Chinese and dialect-rich scenarios. Compared to the previous version, Dolphin-CN-Dialect introduces substantial improvements in data processing, tokenization, training stability, and data sampling strategies. To address the challenges of highly imbalanced dialect data, we propose a temperature-based sampling strategy that effectively balances standard Mandarin and low-resource dialects, leading to significant gains in dialect recognition performance. In addition, we redesign the tokenizer to better align with linguistic characteristics, adopting character-level modeling for Chinese and subword modeling for English, while introducing extensible dialect tokens. Experimental results show that Dolphin-CN-Dialect achieves improvement in dialect recognition accuracy and CER reduction compared to Dolphin. Furthermore, Dolphin-CN-Dialect reaches competitive performance with recent SOTA open-source ASR models, while maintaining a significantly smaller model size. Dolphin-CN-Dialect supports both streaming and non-streaming inference, enabling a practical balance between latency and accuracy. It also provides flexible customization through hotword support and efficient deployment optimized for specialized hardware. These improvements make Dolphin-CN-Dialect a strong and practical solution for real-world multi-dialect ASR applications.

</details>


### [109] [LLiMba: Sardinian on a Single GPU -- Adapting a 3B Language Model to a Vanishing Romance Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09015)
*Luca Ballore*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LLiMba是一个基于Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct在单张24GB消费级GPU上通过持续预训练和监督微调得到的3B参数撒丁语模型。在11.5M撒丁语token上CPT后困惑度达6.76，rsLoRA r256在英译撒丁语方向上达28.5 BLEU。研究发现适配器容量比LoRA变体选择更重要，高秩配置优于低秩，但所有方法在训练集外内容上均存在幻觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 撒丁语作为约百万使用者的罗曼语族语言，在现代NLP中几乎不存在，商业服务不支持，现有语言模型无法可靠生成。为解决这一低资源语言的语言模型缺失问题，作者尝试在消费级硬件上高效适配现有模型。

Method: 基于Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct，使用11.5M撒丁语token（涵盖LSC、Logudorese、Campidanese三种方言）和2.4M相关罗曼语文本进行CPT（防寄存器模糊），然后比较五种SFT配置：全参数微调、LoRA r64、rsLoRA r128、rsLoRA r256和DoRA r256。

Result: CPT后困惑度6.76，在所有FLORES-200方向超越基座。rsLoRA r256在英译撒丁语方向达28.5 BLEU（全微调21.0，CPT后17.3）。r128性能介于LoRA r64和rsLoRA r256之间但出现跨脚本泄露。LoRA r64事实保留最差且产生更自信的幻觉。DoRA r256训练-评估差距最小但事实准确率最差。

Conclusion: 适配器容量比LoRA变体选择更重要；更强正则化并非普遍有益；BLEU指标平滑排序了定性行为存在类别差异的配置；跨脚本困惑度比较需考虑字节回退token化对非拉丁脚本的度量低估。

Abstract: Sardinian, a Romance language with roughly one million speakers, has minimal presence in modern NLP. Commercial services do not support it, and current language models do not produce it reliably. We present LLiMba, a 3B parameter Sardinian-ready model adapted from Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct through continued pretraining (CPT) and supervised fine-tuning (SFT) on a single 24 GB consumer GPU. The corpus contains 11.5 million tokens of Sardinian spanning LSC, Logudorese, and Campidanese, augmented with 2.4 million tokens of related Romance text as replay against register blurring. After CPT the model reaches a perplexity of 6.76 on held out Sardinian and outperforms the base across all six FLORES-200 directions. We compare five SFT configurations under matched conditions: full fine-tuning, LoRA r64, rsLoRA r128, rsLoRA r256, and DoRA r256. rsLoRA r256 wins on every direction into Sardinian, reaching 28.5 BLEU from English against 17.3 after CPT and 21.0 with full fine-tuning. The rank ablation places r128 between LoRA r64 and rsLoRA r256 on BLEU but reveals failure modes invisible to the metric, including leakage across scripts no other variant produces. LoRA r64 retains less factual content from SFT than configurations at higher rank and produces more confident fabrications, though all methods fabricate on content absent from training. DoRA r256 yields the smallest gap between training and evaluation but the worst factual accuracy. The findings indicate that adapter capacity matters more than the choice among LoRA variants for adapting a Romance pretrained base to a low resource Romance target, that stronger regularization is not uniformly beneficial, and that translation metrics smoothly order configurations whose qualitative behavior differs categorically. Perplexity comparisons across scripts must account for byte fallback tokenization, which deflates the metric for scripts other than Latin.

</details>


### [110] [GAMBIT: A Three-Mode Benchmark for Adversarial Robustness in Multi-Agent LLM Collectives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09027)
*Alexandre Le Mercier,Chris Develder,Thomas Demeester*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: GAMBIT是一个多智能体系统中针对自适应欺骗性智能体的基准测试框架，包含27,804个标注样本、三种评估模式和两种独立评分，用于评估冒牌货检测器在零样本和少样本适应场景下的性能。研究发现零样本评估对自适应对抗具有误导性，而元学习变体在少样本适应上比零样本得分相同的检测器快20倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MAS对抗研究仅针对浅层任务且未考虑自适应对抗者（能进化策略以规避检测器），缺乏评估冒牌货检测器在真实场景下对抗自适应欺骗智能体的基准。

Method: 以国际象棋作为深度推理问题基底，使用Gemini 3.1 Pro作为智能体，构建包含240种共进化冒牌货策略的27,804个标注样本基准。引入基于高效进化框架的自适应冒牌货智能体，设计三种评估模式（两种零样本检测、一种基于20个标注样本的重新校准模式）和两个独立评分。

Result: 自适应冒牌货智能体可使集体任务性能崩溃，同时保持几乎不可检测（Gemini检测器F1仅50.5%）。两个零样本得分几乎相同的检测器在少样本适应上相差8倍，元学习变体收敛速度快20倍，该差距仅在校准模式中可见。

Conclusion: GAMBIT是首个对抗攻击与防御共进化的多智能体基准，其冒牌货框架可泛化至国际象棋以外场景，并提供快速重新校准技术以应对快速进化的对抗系统。零样本评估对自适应对抗具有高度误导性，需要校准模式进行全面评估。

Abstract: In multi-agent systems (MAS), a single deceptive agent can nullify all gains of an agentic AI collective and evade deployed defenses. However, existing adversarial studies on MAS target only shallow tasks and do not consider adaptive adversaries, which evolve their strategies to evade the very detectors trained to catch them. To address that gap, we introduce GAMBIT, a benchmark with three evaluation modes and two independent scores for evaluating imposter detectors: the first two modes measure zero-shot detection under increasing distribution shift, and a third recalibration mode measures how quickly a detector adapts to novel attacks from just 20 labeled examples. The benchmark comes with a dataset of 27,804 labeled instances spanning 240 co-evolved imposter strategies. Our contributions are threefold: (1) Using chess as a substrate deep reasoning problem and Gemini 3.1 Pro for agents, we release GAMBIT and its dataset to evaluate imposter detectors under realistic constraints against a stealthy adaptive imposter; (2) We introduce an adaptive imposter agent based on an efficient evolutionary framework, generalizable beyond chess, that collapses collective task performance while remaining essentially undetectable (50.5% F1-score with a Gemini-based detector); (3) We show that zero-shot evaluation can be highly misleading for adaptive adversaries: two detectors with near-identical zero-shot scores differ by 8x on few-shot adaptation, while the meta-learned variant converges 20x faster, a gap only visible in the recalibration mode. Altogether, GAMBIT provides the first multi-agent benchmark where adversarial attacks and defenses co-evolve, with an imposter framework generalizable beyond our use case, and promising techniques for fast recalibration in a rapidly evolving adversarial system. Code and data: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/gambit.

</details>


### [111] [BiAxisAudit: A Novel Framework to Evaluate LLM Bias Across Prompt Sensitivity and Response-Layer Divergence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09041)
*Jialing Gan,Junhao Dong,Songze Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 现有的大语言模型偏见审计基准存在两个关键缺陷：(1)跨提示的格式变化会导致偏见评分波动超过0.7；(2)响应的选择层与阐述层可能立场相反，导致聚合评分掩盖内部不一致性（"抵消陷阱"）。本文提出BiAxisAudit协议，在跨提示和响应内两个正交轴上分别报告偏见分数及可靠性估计，揭示了任务格式、提示维度交互等对偏见评分的显著影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有偏见审计基准在欧盟AI法案等治理框架下已成为安全关切，但当前基准将偏见坍缩为单一标量，忽略了两种无需修改模型权重即可利用的失败模式：跨提示的格式变化和响应内的立场对立。

Method: 提出BiAxisAudit协议：(1)跨提示轴：在任务格式、视角、角色和情感的因子网格下评估每条陈述，将偏见视为分布而非点估计；(2)响应内轴：使用拆分编码（Split Coding）分离选择层和阐述层信号，通过不一致率（Inconsistency Rate）和分歧净不平衡（Divergence Net Imbalance）测量。

Result: 在8个LLM上（每个80200条编码响应）：任务格式解释的方差与模型选择相当；63.6%的偏见信号仅出现在一个编码层；提示维度交互效应超过主效应；仅选择层与仅阐述层的排名几乎不相关（Spearman ρ=0.238, p=0.570）；某些提示配置可同时降低BER和IR，而另一些仅抑制选择层偏见。

Conclusion: 偏见审计必须超越单一标量报告，采用多维可靠评估体系，以区分真实的偏见减少与跨层重新分布导致的表面降低。

Abstract: Bias audits of large language models now operate within governance frameworks such as the EU AI Act, making benchmark reliability a security concern in its own right. Many current benchmarks, however, collapse bias into a single scalar from one prompt format and one surface label. This design misses two failure modes that can be exploited without changing model weights. Across prompts, meaning-preserving format changes shift bias endorsement by more than $0.7$ on a fixed statement pool. Within a response, the discrete Selection and free-text Elaboration can take opposing stances, so an apparently clean aggregate may hide substantial internal inconsistency (a ``cancellation trap''). Selection-only and elaboration-only rankings are therefore nearly uncorrelated across eight LLMs (Spearman $ρ= 0.238$, $p = 0.570$): LLaMA3-70B ranks in the middle under selection-only scoring but highest under elaboration-only scoring on the same responses. We introduce \textsc{BiAxisAudit}, a protocol that reports each bias score together with a reliability estimate on two orthogonal axes. The across-prompt axis evaluates each statement under a factorial grid of task format, perspective, role, and sentiment, treating bias as a distribution rather than a point estimate. The within-response axis uses Split Coding to recover Selection and Elaboration as separate signals, measured by the Inconsistency Rate and Divergence Net Imbalance. Across eight LLMs with $80{,}200$ coded responses each, task format alone explains as much variance as model choice; $63.6\%$ of pooled bias signals (up to $85.2\%$ per model) appear in only one coding layer, and prompt-dimension interactions exceed main effects. The instrument also separates real bias reductions from apparent reductions caused by cross-layer redistribution: some prompt configurations reduce both BER and IR, whereas others suppress only selection-layer bias.

</details>


### [112] [Evaluating Pragmatic Reasoning in Large Language Models: Evidence from Scalar Diversity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09042)
*Ye-eun Cho*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究通过标量多样性（scalar diversity）作为分级诊断工具，比较了直接概率测量和元语言提示两种评估方法对LLM语用推理能力的影响，发现评估方法、模型家族和任务结构之间存在复杂交互作用，语用推理并非单一稳定能力，而是内部概率表征与任务诱导行为相互作用的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM语用推理评估中，基于提示的判断与模型内部概率分布可能存在分歧，使得观测到的表现究竟是底层能力还是任务诱导行为难以区分。本研究旨在系统探究评估方法对LLM语用推理表现的影响。

Method: 采用标量多样性作为分级诊断指标，借鉴Hu & Levy (2023)的方法，在多个模型家族和实验设置中，对比直接概率测量与元语言提示两种评估范式下的语用推理表现。

Result: 两种评估方法并无一致性优劣；语用行为在模型家族、提示策略和任务结构之间差异显著；标量多样性梯度仅在特定的模型-条件组合中出现。

Conclusion: LLM的语用推理是内部概率表征与任务诱导提示行为相互作用的结果，而非单一评估范式所能捕捉的稳定能力；评估设计在解读LLM语用能力中起核心作用。

Abstract: Evaluating pragmatic reasoning in large language models (LLMs) remains challenging because model behavior can vary depending on evaluation methods. Previous studies suggest that prompt-based judgments may diverge from models' internal probability distributions, raising questions about whether observed performance reflects underlying competence or task-induced behavior. This study examines this issue using scalar diversity as a graded diagnostic for pragmatic inference. Following Hu & Levy (2023), this study compares direct probability measurement and metalinguistic prompting across multiple models and experimental settings. The results show that neither evaluation method consistently outperforms the other and that pragmatic behavior varies substantially across model families, prompting strategies, and task structures. Moreover, scalar diversity gradients emerge only in specific model-condition combinations, suggesting that pragmatic reasoning in LLMs reflects an interaction between internal probabilistic representations and task-induced prompting behavior rather than a stable competence captured by a single evaluation paradigm. These findings highlight the central role of evaluation design in interpreting pragmatic abilities in LLMs.

</details>


### [113] [Phase Transitions in Affective Meaning Divergence: The Hidden Drift Before the Break](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09043)
*Napassorn Litchiowong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出情感意义分歧（AMD）概念，将其形式化为对话者锚定条件情感分布之间的总变差距离。基于言语行为理论、共同知识积累和熵正则化博弈论，推导出对数最优反应映射，该映射在βα>4时发生鞍结分岔，导致修复协调的突然迟滞坍塌。在Conversations Gone Awry数据集上，偏离对话表现出多层级临界减速信号，AMD提供独特的 temporal 特征，在分岔点方差达到峰值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对话中同一词语可能被不同参与者赋予截然不同的情感含义（如"Fine"可理解为"解决"或"投降"），现有研究缺乏对这种情感意义分歧的形式化建模及其对对话协调崩溃影响的理论解释。

Method: 将AMD定义为锚定条件情感分布的总变差距离；基于言语行为理论、共同知识积累和熵正则化博弈论推导对数最优反应映射；通过鞍结分岔分析揭示对话修复协调的突变机制；在CGA-Wiki和CGA-CMV数据集上验证临界减速信号。

Result: 在CGA-Wiki（N=652）上，偏离对话表现出显著的临界减速信号：词汇分歧方差（p<0.001, d=0.36）、AMD方差（p=0.001, d=0.26）和对话行为修复方差（p=0.016, d=0.20）。AMD的分岔点方差峰值晚于毒性方差峰值，且是唯一基于理论框架的指标。CGA-CMV（N=1,169）的边界条件分析结果混合但方向一致。

Conclusion: AMD是对话协调崩溃的前兆信号，其理论驱动特性优于传统的情感极性和毒性基线，为理解对话破裂提供了形式化的理论框架和可操作的量化指标。

Abstract: One partner says "Fine" meaning <i>resolution</i>; the other hears <i>surrender</i>. The word is shared; the affective uptake is not. We formalize this as <b>affective meaning divergence (AMD)</b>, the total-variation distance between interlocutors' anchor-conditioned affect distributions. Building on speech-act theory, common-ground accumulation, and entropy-regularized game theory, we derive a logit best-response map whose dynamics undergo a saddle-node bifurcation: when $βα> 4$, a monotone increase in AMD-driven load produces an abrupt, hysteretic collapse of repair coordination. On Conversations Gone Awry (CGA-Wiki; $N=652$), derailing conversations exhibit critical-slowing-down (CSD) signatures across multiple levels: lexical divergence variance ($p<0.001$, $d=0.36$), AMD variance ($p=0.001$, $d=0.26$), and dialog-act repair variance ($p=0.016$, $d=0.20$), all significant after correction and stronger than toxicity and sentiment baselines. AMD provides a distinct temporal signature, with retrospectively measured variance peaking at the bifurcation point while toxicity variance peaks earlier, and is the only indicator grounded in the theoretical framework. Boundary-condition analysis on CGA-CMV ($N=1{,}169$) yields mixed but directionally consistent evidence.

</details>


### [114] [Language-Conditioned Visual Grounding with CLIP Multilingual](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09060)
*J. de Curtò,Mauro Liz,I. de Zarzà*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 通过密集多语言CLIP探针实验，在固定视觉编码器、仅变化文本分支的条件下，发现低资源语言存在结构性性能差距，且该差距源自文本分支而非视觉编码器；扩大模型规模对性能差距的影响因语言类型而异，空间错位是主要失效模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多语言视觉-语言模型在不同语言间存在系统性性能差距，但其产生机制尚不明确——差距可能来自视觉编码器、文本分支或两者的交互。当前研究未能解耦这三方面的贡献，需要一种方法隔离变量以定位问题根源。

Method: 采用密集多语言CLIP探针设计，保持视觉编码器在13种语言间完全一致，仅变化XLM-RoBERTa文本分支。评估两种CLIP架构（XLM-R base + ViT-B/32, 约87M视觉参数；XLM-R large + ViT-H/14, 约632M），覆盖11个概念和210张图像，使用聚类掩码IoU、百分位IoU和Spearman秩相关系数量化跨语言一致性。

Result: 低资源语言（阿拉伯语、巴斯克语、卢森堡语）在两种骨干规模下均存在结构性惩罚（Wilcoxon HR>LR p<10^-300；聚类掩码IoU差距base为+0.114，large为+0.143）；扩大7倍编码器对结构性失效案例（巴斯克语Δ=-0.056，卢森堡语Δ=-0.076）扩大了差距，但改善了阿拉伯语（Δ=+0.033）；峰值相似性保持率高（large规模下均值比0.94），但聚类掩码IoU急剧下降，表明空间错位是主要失效模式。

Conclusion: 多语言视觉-语言模型的性能差距明确来自文本分支；空间错位而非信号崩溃是主导失效模式；模型缩放对差距的影响因语言的语料覆盖与分词器生育率问题而异；密集CLIP方法在能耗方面具有竞争力（每千次查询3.4-3.9 Wh），适合能效感知的多语言部署。

Abstract: Multilingual vision-language models exhibit systematic performance gaps across languages, but the mechanism remains ambiguous: cross-language divergence could arise from the visual encoder, the text branch, or their interaction. We resolve this ambiguity through a dense multilingual CLIP probe in which the visual encoder is held identical across thirteen typologically diverse languages and only the XLM-RoBERTa text branch varies. We evaluate two CLIP architectures spanning a 7x visual-encoder scale gap (XLM-R base + ViT-B/32, ~87M visual parameters; XLM-R large + ViT-H/14, ~632M) on 11 concepts and 210 images, and quantify cross-language agreement via cluster-mask IoU, top-percentile IoU, and Spearman rank correlation against an English reference (n=2,310 paired observations per language). Three findings emerge. First, low-resource languages (Arabic, Basque, Luxembourgish) incur a structural penalty at both backbone scales (Wilcoxon HR>LR p<10^-300; cluster-mask IoU gap +0.114 at base, +0.143 at large), isolating the deficit to the text branch. Second, scaling the encoder 7x widens the gap for structural failure cases (Basque Δ=-0.056, Luxembourgish Δ=-0.076) while improving Arabic (Δ=+0.033), separating corpus-coverage from tokeniser-fertility failures. Third, peak similarity is preserved across languages (mean ratio 0.94 at large scale) while cluster-mask IoU drops sharply, identifying spatial misalignment, not signal collapse, as the dominant failure mode. At 3.4-3.9 Wh per 1,000 queries, dense-CLIP grounding is competitive with high-throughput inference budgets, positioning it as a practical substrate for energy-aware multilingual deployment.

</details>


### [115] [Soohak: A Mathematician-Curated Benchmark for Evaluating Research-level Math Capabilities of LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09063)
*Guijin Son,Seungone Kim,Catherine Arnett,Hyunwoo Ko,Hyein Lee,Hyeonah Kang,Jiang Longxi,Jin Yun,JungYup Lee,Kyungmin Lee,Sam Yoosuk Kim,Sang Park,Seunghyeok Hong,SeungJae Lee,Seungyeop Yi,Shinae Shin,SunHye Bok,Sunyoung Shin,Yonghoon Ji,Youngtaek Kim,Hanearl Jung,Akari Asai,Graham Neubig,Sean Welleck,Youngjae Yu,Akshelin R,Alexander B. Ivanov,Boboev Muhammadjon,Chaeyoung Han,Christian Stump,Dmitrii Karp,Dohyun Kwon,DoYong Kwon,Duk-Soon Oh,Giovanni Resta,Greta Panova,Huiyun Noh,Hyungryul Baik,Hyungsun Bae,Inomov Mashrafdzhon,Jeewon Kim,Ji Eun Lee,Jiaqi Liu,Jieui Kang,Jimin Kim,Jon-Lark Kim,Junseo Yoon,Junwoo Jo,Kibeom Kim,Kiwoon Kwon,Mario Kummer,Max Mercer,Minjun Kim,Nahyun Lee,Ng Ze-An,Rafał Marcin Łochowski,Raphaël Lachièze-Rey,Ruichen Zhang,Sejin Park,Seonguk Seo,Shin Jaehoon,Sunatullo,Taewoong Eom,Yeachan Park,Yongseok Jang,Youchan Oh,Zhaoyang Wang,Zoltán Kovács*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Soohak是一个由64位数学家从头编写的439题研究级数学基准测试集，包含Challenge和Refusal两个子集。前沿模型在其Challenge子集上表现较低（最高30.4%），而在Refusal子集上所有模型均未超过50%，表明拒绝回答（识别不适定问题）能力成为当前模型新的优化目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 奥林匹克级数学问题仅衡量逐步推理能力，而研究级问题要求将推理用于推进数学知识前沿本身。然而研究级数学基准稀缺（如Riemann Bench仅25题、FrontierMath-Tier 4仅50题），亟需更大规模、可靠的研究级数学推理评测基准。

Method: 由64位数学家从头编写439道研究级数学问题，构建Soohak基准。该基准分为两个子集：Challenge子集（标准问题求解）和Refusal子集（识别不适定问题并拒绝回答）。数据集将于2026年末公开，期间可申请获取模型评估结果以防止数据污染。

Result: 在Challenge子集上，Gemini-3-Pro得分为30.4%、GPT-5为26.4%、Claude-Opus-4.5为10.4%，开源模型如Qwen3-235B低于15%。在Refusal子集上，所有模型均未超过50%。

Conclusion: Soohak为下一代前沿模型提供了具有充足提升空间的研究级数学评测基准，并首次提出"拒绝回答"（识别不适定问题）作为当前模型未直接优化的新能力目标。

Abstract: Following the recent achievement of gold-medal performance on the IMO by frontier LLMs, the community is searching for the next meaningful and challenging target for measuring LLM reasoning. Whereas olympiad-style problems measure step-by-step reasoning alone, research-level problems use such reasoning to advance the frontier of mathematical knowledge itself, emerging as a compelling alternative. Yet research-level math benchmarks remain scarce because such problems are difficult to source (e.g., Riemann Bench and FrontierMath-Tier 4 contain 25 and 50 problems, respectively). To support reliable evaluation of next-generation frontier models, we introduce Soohak, a 439-problem benchmark newly authored from scratch by 64 mathematicians. Soohak comprises two subsets. On the Challenge subset, frontier models including Gemini-3-Pro, GPT-5, and Claude-Opus-4.5 reach 30.4%, 26.4%, and 10.4% respectively, leaving substantial headroom, while leading open-weight models such as Qwen3-235B, GPT-OSS-120B, and Kimi-2.5 remain below 15%. Notably, beyond standard problem solving, Soohak introduces a refusal subset that probes a capability intrinsic to research mathematics: recognizing ill-posed problems and pausing rather than producing confident but unjustified answers. On this subset, no model exceeds 50%, identifying refusal as a new optimization target that current models do not directly address. To prevent contamination, the dataset will be publicly released in late 2026, with model evaluations available upon request in the interim.

</details>


### [116] [Character-Level Transformer for Tajik-Persian Transliteration with a Parallel Lexical Corpus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09092)
*Mullosharaf K. Arabov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文构建了塔吉克语（西里尔字母）到波斯语（波斯-阿拉伯字母）的最大公开词级平行语料库（52,152词），并训练字符级序列到序列Transformer模型进行自动转写，在CER和准确率上优于规则方法和RNN基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 塔吉克语（西里尔字母）与波斯语（波斯-阿拉伯字母）之间存在书写系统差异，缺乏大规模、高质量、经词典验证的平行语料库，且现有转写方法性能有限，亟需构建标准化数据集并探索基于深度学习的转写方案。

Method: 构建52,152词的塔吉克-波斯平行语料库（来源：印刷词典、百科全书、人工验证在线资源）；采用字符级序列到序列Transformer模型；使用CER和精确匹配准确率评估；采用波束搜索（k=3）优化；进行词性标注分析。

Result: Transformer模型CER为0.3216，精确匹配准确率0.3133，优于基于词典的规则方法和RNN基线；波束搜索（k=3）进一步将CER降至0.3182，准确率提升至0.3215。

Conclusion: 所提方法和语料库有效支持塔吉克-波斯跨脚本转写任务，释放了完整预处理脚本、数据划分和训练配置以促进可重复性和后续研究，适用于字符级转写、跨脚本NLP及词典学应用。

Abstract: This study addresses automatic transliteration from Tajik (Cyrillic script) to Persian (Perso-Arabic script). We present a curated, lexicographically verified parallel corpus of 52,152 Tajik--Persian words and short phrases, compiled from printed dictionaries, encyclopedic sources, and manually verified online resources. To the best of our knowledge, this is one of the largest publicly available word-level corpora for Tajik--Persian transliteration.
  Using this corpus, we train a character-level sequence-to-sequence Transformer model and evaluate it using Character Error Rate (CER) and exact-match accuracy. The Transformer achieves a CER of 0.3216 and an exact-match accuracy of 0.3133, outperforming both dictionary-based rule-based and recurrent neural baselines. With beam search (k=3), performance further improves to CER 0.3182 and accuracy 0.3215. We describe the data collection and preprocessing pipeline, model architecture, and experimental protocol, and report a part-of-speech analysis showing performance differences across lexical categories.
  All preprocessing scripts, deterministic splits into training, validation, and test sets, and training configurations are released to support reproducibility and further research on Tajik and related Persian dialects. The corpus supports research in character-level transliteration, cross-script NLP, and lexicographic applications.

</details>


### [117] [Dynamic Meta-Metrics: Source-Sentence Conditioned Weighting for MT Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09098)
*Luke Zhang,Justin Vasselli,Aditya Khan,York Hay Ng,En-Shiun Annie Lee*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出动态元度量（DMM）框架，根据源句特征动态组合现有翻译评估指标，软条件化扩展优于线性模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器翻译评估指标的组合通常采用静态集成或语言特定的固定权重，无法根据源句的语义和结构特性自适应调整，限制了评估的准确性。

Method: 提出DMM框架，包含硬条件化（按聚类分配可解释的组合器）和软条件化扩展（基于源句聚类责任连续调整权重），采用MLP、线性回归和高斯过程等组合策略。

Result: 在WMT指标共享任务的多个语言对上，基于MLP的组合在系统和句子级别的配对一致性上优于线性和高斯过程集成，软条件化在部分设置下进一步提升性能。

Conclusion: DMM通过源句条件化的动态指标组合能更灵活地适应不同源句特征，软条件化扩展是提升评估性能的有效方向。

Abstract: We propose Dynamic Meta-Metrics (DMM), a framework for machine translation evaluation that learns source-sentence conditioned combinations of existing metrics. Rather than relying on a single static ensemble or language-specific weighting, DMM adapts the metric combination based on properties of the source segment.
