We study hard conditioning, which fits an interpretable combiner per cluster, and an exploratory soft-conditioned extension whose weights vary continuously with source-cluster responsibilities. We evaluate DMM on the WMT Metrics Shared Task data across multiple language pairs using pairwise agreement measures at the system and segment levels. Across settings, MLP-based combinations outperform linear and Gaussian process-based ensembles, and introducing soft conditioning yields gains over linear models.

</details>


### [118] [GRC: Unifying Reasoning-Driven Generation, Retrieval and Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09100)
*Zhongtao Miao,Qiyu Wu,Yoshimasa Tsuruoka*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出名为GRC的统一训练框架，通过元潜表征和统一的生成、表征与压缩微调方法，使大语言模型能够在前向传播中同时完成推理驱动生成、推理增强文本表征和上下文压缩三项任务，实现单次前向传播完成多目标、模块化灵活推理，显著降低RAG部署成本，训练数据利用率提升3倍，并提出自推理潜表征和潜记忆增强生成等新范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前文本嵌入和生成任务基于LLM分开训练，导致训练成本高、部署负担大；同时上下文压缩是推理驱动生成和长上下文场景的关键挑战，亟需一种能统一处理生成、表征和压缩任务的框架以减少部署开销并提升效率。

Method: 提出GRC训练框架，通过引入元潜表征（meta latent tokens）和统一生成、表征与压缩的微调方法，将推理驱动生成、推理增强文本嵌入和上下文压缩三任务在单次前向传播中统一；提出混合分页注意力（hybrid paged attention）加速推理。

Result: 在推理密集型检索基准、生成任务、文档压缩、延迟评估和RAG场景下均验证了有效性；实现了O(1)长度的压缩KV缓存作为可更新记忆，训练数据利用率提升3倍，推理效率显著提高。

Conclusion: GRC框架为真正统一的模型提供了新方向，该模型能无缝处理推理驱动生成、文本嵌入和上下文压缩任务，并启用了自推理潜表征和潜记忆增强生成等新范式。

Abstract: Text embedding and generative tasks are usually trained separately based on large language models (LLMs) nowadays. This causes a large amount of training cost and deployment effort. Context compression is also a challenging and pressing task, which is vital to reasoning-driven generation, and agentic tasks requiring long context and continual learning. In this paper, we explore how to unify reasoning-driven generation, reasoning-enhanced text representation and context compression tasks in one forward pass for LLMs. Through meta latent tokens and a unified generative, representative and compressive tuning approach, we propose a training framework named GRC that bridges the three tasks. The trained models can accomplish three objectives in a single forward pass while maintaining modular, LEGO-style flexibility during inference. This design greatly reduces the deployment effort for retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) and achieves efficient inference and three times data utilization during training. Furthermore, this framework design enables a new paradigm for text embedding: self-reason-latent embeds, and a new generation paradigm, latent memory-augmented generation, where compressed and internalized KV cache with O(1) length is used as the updatable memory. We also propose hybrid paged attention to speed up the inference of our models. Extensive experiments on reasoning-intensive retrieval benchmarks, generative tasks, document compression, latency evaluation, and RAG settings demonstrate the effectiveness of our method and may shed light on the truly unified model that can handle reasoning-driven generation, embedding and compression tasks seamlessly.

</details>


### [119] [Fin-Bias: Comprehensive Evaluation for LLM Decision-Making under human bias in Finance Domain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09106)
*Xiaoyu Hu,Jinman Zhao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Fin-Bias基准，用于评估LLM在金融环境下受分析师评级（真实/虚假）影响而产生的从众行为，并开发了一种检测人类偏见的提示方法以增强LLM独立决策能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有金融基准多局限于小样本股票交易评估，未能展现LLM在含有潜在人类偏见的长文本金融上下文中的脆弱性，因此需要构建更全面的评估基准。

Method: 构建包含8868份长篇幅分析师报告的Fin-Bias基准，涵盖多行业，通过向LLM提供含/不含分析师评级及虚假评级的报告，测试其投资评级输出，同时开发检测人类偏见的提示方法。

Result: LLM倾向于跟随上下文中的显式偏见过度依赖分析师评级（从众效应）；所提出的偏见检测方法能促使LLM独立思考，部分模型在预测未来股票收益方面超越人类表现。

Conclusion: LLM在金融决策中存在显著的从众偏差，但通过适当的提示设计可缓解该问题，提升模型独立判断能力。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in financial contexts, raising critical concerns about reliability, alignment, and susceptibility to adversarial manipulation. While prior finance-related benchmarks assess LLMs' capabilities in stock trading, they are often restricted to small sample and fail to demonstrate LLM susceptibility to context with potential human bias. We introduce Fin-Bias (financial herding under long and uncertain financial context), a benchmark for evaluating LLM investment decision-making when faced with uncertainty and possible human-biased opinions. Fin-Bias includes 8868 long firm-specific analyst reports, including firm aspects summarized and analyzed by sophisticated analysts with investment ratings (Bullish/Neutral/Bearish) spanning from various industries. We present large language models with firm analyst reports with/without analyst investment ratings and even with 'fake' rating, to get investment ratings generated by LLMs. Our results reveal that LLMs tend to herd the explicit bias in context. We also develop a method to detect potential human opinions, which can encourage LLMs to think independently, some models even exceed human performance in predicting future stock return.

</details>


### [120] [From Traditional Taggers to LLMs: A Comparative Study of POS Tagging for Medieval Romance Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09147)
*Matthias Schöffel,Esteban Garces Arias*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了LLM在中世纪罗曼语（奥克语、加泰罗尼亚语、法语）词性标注中的表现，发现微调与跨语言迁移学习显著优于传统方法，其中跨语言迁移对低资源变体尤为有效，双语训练在特定目标语言上可超越多语配置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中世纪罗曼语的词性标注因正字法变异、形态复杂性和标注资源匮乏而长期面临挑战，亟需探索现代神经方法（尤其是LLM）在该领域的适用性。

Method: 对比传统（基于规则、统计）标注器与开源LLM在零样本提示、少样本提示、单语微调、跨语言迁移学习四种设置下的性能，使用基于历史语料的标注数据集进行评估。

Result: LLM方法全面超越传统标注器；微调和多语训练带来最大提升；跨语言迁移学习显著惠及低资源变体；针对特定目标语言的双语训练有时优于更广泛的多语配置。

Conclusion: 语言距离和数据集特征对迁移策略设计至关重要；LLM-based POS标注管线可有效应用于数字人文研究；全部代码、模型和数据集已开源。

Abstract: Part-of-speech (POS) tagging for Medieval Romance languages remains challenging due to orthographic variation, morphological complexity, and limited annotated resources. This paper presents a systematic empirical evaluation of large language models (LLMs) for POS tagging across three medieval varieties: Medieval Occitan, Medieval Catalan, and Medieval French. We compare traditional rule-based and statistical taggers with modern open-source LLMs under zero-shot prompting, few-shot prompting, monolingual fine-tuning, and cross-lingual transfer learning settings.
  Experiments on historically grounded datasets show that LLM-based approaches consistently outperform traditional taggers, with fine-tuning and multilingual training yielding the largest improvements. In particular, cross-lingual transfer learning substantially benefits under-resourced varieties, while targeted bilingual training can outperform broader multilingual configurations for specific target languages. The results highlight the importance of linguistic proximity and dataset characteristics when designing transfer strategies for historical NLP.
  These findings provide empirical insights into the applicability of modern neural methods to medieval text processing and provide practical guidance for deploying LLM-based POS tagging pipelines in digital humanities research. All code, models, and processed datasets are released for reproducibility.

</details>


### [121] [Lost in Translation? Exploring the Shift in Grammatical Gender from Latin to Occitan](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09156)
*Ahan Chatterjee,Matthias Schöffel,Matthias Aßenmacher,Esteban Garces Arias*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一个可解释的深度学习框架，研究拉丁语到罗曼语演化过程中语法性别系统从三分类（阴阳性+中性）到二分类（阴阳性）的重组，从词汇和语境两个层面分析性别信息的分布，并改进了低资源历史语言场景下的分词策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拉丁语到罗曼语的演化涉及语法性别系统的结构性重组（三分类→二分类），但现有方法在低资源历史语言场景下不够鲁棒，缺乏从词汇和语境层面系统量化性别信息分布的可解释分析框架。

Method: 提出可解释深度学习框架，包含：(1) 针对低资源历史语言场景优化的分词器；(2) 词汇层面评估形态特征对性别预测的贡献；(3) 语境层面量化不同词性类别对语法性别预测的贡献，从而刻画词元与其句法语境之间性别信息的分布特征。

Result: 常规分词策略在此低资源历史场景下不够鲁棒，提出的分词器优于基线方法。词汇和语境层面分别量化了形态特征和词性类别对性别预测的贡献度，揭示性别信息在词元和句子语境之间的分布模式。

Conclusion: 可解释深度学习框架能够有效分析历史语言演化中语法性别系统的重组过程，分词优化对低资源历史语言处理至关重要，词汇与语境层面的联合分析为理解性别信息分布提供了新的视角。

Abstract: The diachronic evolution from Latin to the Romance languages involved a restructuring of the grammatical gender system from a tripartite configuration (masculine, feminine, neuter) to a bipartite one (masculine, feminine). In this work, we introduce an interpretable deep learning framework to investigate this phenomenon at both lexical and contextual levels. First, we show that conventional tokenization strategies are insufficiently robust for this low-resource historical setting, and that our proposed tokenizer improves performance over these baselines. At the lexical level, we evaluate the contribution of morphological features to gender prediction. At the contextual level, we quantify the contributions of different part-of-speech categories to grammatical gender prediction. Together, these analyses characterize the distribution of gender information between the lemma and its sentential context. We make our codebase, datasets, and results publicly available.

</details>


### [122] [WorldSpeech: A Multilingual Speech Corpus from Around the World](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09167)
*Antonis Asonitis,Luca A. Lanzendörfer,Frédéric Berdoz,Roger Wattenhofer*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: WorldSpeech是一个包含76种语言、65k小时、24kHz采样率的多语言语音语料库，通过对现有ASR模型进行微调，在11种语言上实现了平均63.5%的相对词错误率降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高资源语言ASR性能良好，但大多数语言因缺乏公开可用的配对音频-文本数据而导致准确率严重下降，亟需构建大规模多语言语音数据集。

Method: 从议会记录、国际广播、公共领域有声书等多个公开来源收集了涵盖76种语言、65k小时的对齐音频-文本数据，构建了24kHz采样率的WorldSpeech语料库。

Result: 对37种语言提供超过200小时的对齐语音，28种超过500小时，24种超过1k小时；在11种类型多样的语言上微调现有ASR模型，平均相对词错误率降低63.5%。

Conclusion: WorldSpeech能够有效缓解多语言ASR中的数据稀缺问题，显著提升低资源语言的语音识别性能。

Abstract: Automatic speech recognition (ASR) performs well for high-resource languages with abundant paired audio-transcript data, but its accuracy degrades sharply for most languages due to limited publicly available aligned data. To this end, we introduce WorldSpeech, a 24 kHz multilingual speech corpus comprising 65k hours of aligned audio-transcript data across 76 languages, collected from diverse public sources including parliamentary proceedings, international broadcasts, and public-domain audiobooks. For 37 languages, WorldSpeech provides more than 200 hours of aligned speech, with 28 exceeding 500 hours and 24 surpassing 1k hours. Fine-tuning existing ASR models on WorldSpeech results in an average relative Word-Error-Rate reduction of 63.5% across 11 typologically diverse languages.

</details>


### [123] [Two Ways to De-Bias an LLM-as-a-Judge: A Continuous-Score Comparison of Hierarchical Bayesian Calibration and Neural-ODE Score Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09227)
*Andrea Morandi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文对比了两种LLM评分校准方法：参数化贝叶斯线性校正和非参数神经ODE流校正。结果表明方法选择取决于数据预算：100个锚点时线性校正更优，1500个锚点时流校正全面胜出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM作为自动评分器存在偏差（宽严不一、中间压缩、偏好冗长回答），常见补救方式是后验校准，但两种对立建模思路（参数化vs非参数化）的优劣尚不明确。

Method: 对比两种校正器：1）参数化小锚点贝叶斯线性校正（带逐评分不确定性）；2）非参数化神经ODE（FFJORD）评分传输流。在UltraFeedback细粒度评分上以1700个配对样本（200个保留）进行头对头比较，评估总体均值恢复、逐项准确率和分布形态匹配三个维度。

Result: 100个锚点时，线性校正KL散度（0.031 vs 0.058）优于流校正约2倍，MAE持平；1500个锚点时，流校正全面胜出（MAE 0.320 vs 0.359, Pearson 0.922 vs 0.896, KL 0.026 vs 0.037）。线性校正在1500锚点以下饱和，无法拟合残差tanh形非线性；流校正随标注量增加持续改进。

Conclusion: 两种方法的选择本质是数据预算问题：小样本场景用线性校正，大样本场景用流校正。两者均能将原始+0.71的均值偏差缩小至±0.08以内。

Abstract: [Abridged] Using a Large Language Model (LLM) as an automatic rater (LLM-as-a-judge) is cheap but potentially biased: some judges run lenient, others strict, the middle of the scale gets compressed, and verbose answers may be over-rewarded. A common remedy is post-hoc calibration: leave the cheap judge in place and, on a modest set of paired anchors, fit a transformation from raw judge scores to an estimate of the human rating. We compare two correctors that take opposing views on how this mapping should be modeled: a parametric, small-anchor hierarchical Bayesian linear correction with per-score uncertainty, and a non-parametric Neural-ODE (FFJORD) score-transport flow. Both are run head-to-head on UltraFeedback fine-grained_score (1700 paired examples, 200 held out), with calibration split into three operational sub-questions: population-mean recovery, per-item accuracy, and distributional-shape match.
  The headline result is that the choice between methods is primarily a data-budget question. Both correctors close the raw $+0.71$-point mean offset to within $\pm 0.08$ of the GPT-4 reference, at 100 and at 1500 anchors. Past that, the methods swap roles. With 100 anchors, the linear corrector reconstructs the human-score distribution roughly twice as well by KL divergence (0.031 vs. 0.058) and ties the flow on MAE. With 1500 anchors the flow wins on every metric (MAE 0.320 vs. 0.359, Pearson 0.922 vs. 0.896, KL 0.026 vs. 0.037). The Bayesian linear corrector saturates well below 1500 anchors: residual $\tanh$-shaped non-linearity is, by construction, structure a linear correction cannot fit. The flow keeps improving as labels grow. We translate these findings into an explicit decision rule for production deployments.

</details>


### [124] [Matching Meaning at Scale: Evaluating Semantic Search for 18th-Century Intellectual History through the Case of Locke](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09236)
*Yu Wu,Ananth Mahadevan,Filip Ginter,Michael Mathioudakis,Mikko Tolonen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文评估了语义搜索在18世纪思想史研究中的应用，通过分析约翰·洛克著作的接受情况，发现语义搜索比词汇方法能检索到更多隐含的思想接收，但仍存在“词汇门控”效应，即检索结果部分受限于表层词汇重叠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前研究多依赖词汇文本复用检测来研究思想传播，但该方法无法捕捉转述和复杂的隐含引用关系，亟需探索语义搜索在历史语料库中的有效性。

Method: 基于专家标注的语义分类体系，评估现成的语义搜索流水线在18世纪思想史中检索约翰·洛克思想接收情况的表现，并与词汇基线方法进行对比。

Result: 语义搜索能检索到比词汇基线方法更多的隐含思想接收案例，但语言诊断揭示了“词汇门控”效应，即检索仍部分受限于表层词汇重叠。

Conclusion: 语义搜索在分析大规模历史语料库中的思想传播方面具有潜力，但也存在局限性，不能完全摆脱词汇层面的约束。

Abstract: While digitized corpora have transformed the study of intellectual transmission, current methods rely heavily on lexical text reuse detection, capturing verbatim quotations but fundamentally missing paraphrases and complex implicit engagement. This paper evaluates semantic search in 18th-century intellectual history through the reception of John Locke's foundational work. Using expert annotation grounded in a semantic taxonomy, we examine whether an off-the-shelf semantic search pipeline can surface meaning-level correspondences overlooked by lexical methods. Our results demonstrate that semantic search retrieves substantially more implicit receptions than lexical baselines. However, linguistic diagnostics also reveal a "lexical gatekeeping" effect, where retrieval remains partially constrained by surface vocabulary overlap. These findings highlight both the potential and the limitations of semantic retrieval for analyzing the circulation of ideas in large historical corpora. The data is available at https://github.com/COMHIS/locke-sim-data.

</details>


### [125] [Cornerstones or Stumbling Blocks? Deciphering the Rock Tokens in On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09253)
*Yuxuan Jiang,Runchao Li,Shubhashis Roy Dipta,Dawei Li,Zhao Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发现On-Policy Distillation中存在一类"Rock Tokens"（占生成token的18%），尽管其梯度贡献极大，但既不收敛、对推理性能也无实质作用，通过策略性绕过这些token可大幅简化对齐过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR领域已发现关键token驱动推理增益，但OPD中token级别的理解尚属空白；现有理论认为高损失token应随训练收敛而减少，但实证发现相反，存在持续高损失的token。

Method: 通过实证分析OPD中每token KL散度下的高损失token行为，结合因果干预实验评估其对推理性能的功能贡献。

Result: Rock Tokens占比高达18%；提供梯度范数比例过大但自身不收敛；因果干预显示其对推理性能贡献可忽略。

Conclusion: 大量优化带宽浪费在结构和话语残留上，策略性绕过Rock Tokens可实现更高效的蒸馏对齐，挑战了统一token加权的必要性。

Abstract: While recent work in Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) has shown that a small subset of critical tokens disproportionately drives reasoning gains, an analogous token-level understanding of On-Policy Distillation (OPD) remains largely unexplored. In this work, we investigate high-loss tokens, a token type that--as the most direct signal of student-teacher mismatch under OPD's per-token KL objective--should progressively diminish as training converges according to existing studies; however, our empirical analysis shows otherwise. Even after OPD training reaches apparent saturation, a substantial subset of tokens continues to exhibit persistently high loss; these tokens, which we term Rock Tokens, can account for up to 18\% of the tokens in generated outputs. Our investigation reveals two startling paradoxes. First, despite their high occurrence frequency providing a disproportionately large share of total gradient norms, Rock Tokens themselves remain stagnant throughout training, resisting teacher-driven corrections. Second, through causal intervention, we find that these tokens provide negligible functional contribution to the model's actual reasoning performance. These findings suggest that a vast amount of optimization bandwidth is spent on structural and discourse residuals that the student model cannot or need not internalize. By deconstructing these dynamics, we demonstrate that strategically bypassing these ``stumbling blocks'' can significantly streamline the alignment process, challenging the necessity of uniform token weighting and offering a more efficient paradigm for large-scale model distillation.

</details>


### [126] [Beyond Continuity: Challenges of Context Switching in Multi-Turn Dialogue with LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09268)
*Aditya Sinha,Harald Steck,Vito Ostuni,Matteo Rinaldi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了多轮对话中LLM对用户话题转变（pivot）与需求细化（refine）的检测能力，以及从历史轮次中筛选相关上下文的能力。基于真实数据集构建合成基准测试，评估了10个LLM的零样本性能，发现仅部分推理型和强指令型模型能准确检测话题转变，开源模型表现较差，所有模型均存在位置偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用户在多轮对话中经常改变话题或细化请求，但LLM往往忽略这些话题转变，携带无关历史上下文导致回答不准确。现有研究缺乏对LLM多轮理解能力，特别是对pivot和refine检测的系统性评估。

Method: 定义了两个子任务：(1) 检测用户当前轮次是pivot（话题转变）还是refine（需求细化）；(2) 从历史轮次中筛选相关上下文。基于真实世界多领域数据集构建合成基准，模拟不同难度级别的话题转变。对10个LLM（包括开源、闭源和推理模型）进行零样本性能评估。

Result: 仅部分推理型和强指令遵循的LLM能准确检测话题转变；开源LLM在此任务上表现不佳，即使有明确线索也常携带过时上下文；所有模型均存在位置偏差。

Conclusion: 针对提升LLM多轮对话长期鲁棒性提出了关键建议。

Abstract: Users interacting with Large Language Models (LLMs) in a multi-turn conversation routinely refine their requests or pivot to new topics. LLMs, however, often miss these topic shifts and carry over irrelevant context from previous turns, leading to inaccurate responses. In this paper, we stress-test the multi-turn understanding of LLMs and study the following two sub-tasks: (1) detecting whether the user pivots or refines in the current turn, and (2) shortlisting relevant context from previous turns. To this end, we construct synthetic benchmarks based on real-world datasets from varied domains, as to simulate context shifts of different levels of difficulty. We then evaluate the zero-shot performance of ten LLMs (open-weight, closed-source and reasoning), and demonstrate that only some reasoning and strongly instructed LLMs are accurate in detecting pivots; open-weight LLMs struggle with the task and frequently carry stale context even with explicit cues; and all models suffer from a position bias. Based on the results, we discuss key takeaways for improving long-term robustness in multi-turn capabilities for LLMs.

</details>


### [127] [DeltaRubric: Generative Multimodal Reward Modeling via Joint Planning and Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09269)
*Rui Liu,Dian Yu,Zhenwen Liang,Yucheng Shi,Tong Zheng,Runpeng Dai,Haitao Mi,Pratap Tokekar,Leoweiliang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: DeltaRubric将多模态偏好评估重构为"规划-执行"两阶段过程：先生成实例特定的验证清单，再执行检查，通过多角色强化学习联合优化，显著提升MLLM奖励模型的可靠性（VL-RewardBench上4B模型+22.6%，8B模型+18.8%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM奖励模型存在"懒惰判断"问题，即利用语言先验而非细粒度视觉验证；基于清单的评估虽在纯文本中有效，但扩展到多模态任务时受限于视觉推理的复杂性，响应间的关键差异往往依赖实例特定的视觉细节。

Method: 提出DeltaRubric框架，将多模态偏好评估解耦为两个角色：(1) 分歧规划器(Disagreement Planner)生成中立、实例特定的验证清单；(2) 清单验证器(Checklist Verifier)对照图像和问题执行自生成的检查以给出理由充分的判断。该过程建模为多角色强化学习问题，联合优化规划与验证能力。

Result: 在Qwen3-VL 4B和8B Instruct模型上，DeltaRubric在VL-RewardBench上分别提升整体准确率+22.6（4B）和+18.8（8B）个百分点，大幅超越无清单基线。

Conclusion: 将评估分解为结构化、可验证的步骤能够实现更可靠、更具泛化能力的多模态奖励建模。

Abstract: Aligning Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) requires reliable reward models, yet existing single-step evaluators can suffer from lazy judging, exploiting language priors over fine-grained visual verification. While rubric-based evaluation mitigates these biases in text-only settings, extending it to multimodal tasks is bottlenecked by the complexity of visual reasoning. The critical differences between responses often depend on instance-specific visual details. Robust evaluation requires dynamically synthesizing rubrics that isolate spatial and factual discrepancies. To address this, we introduce $\textbf{DeltaRubric}$, an approach that reformulates multimodal preference evaluation as a plan-and-execute process within a single MLLM. DeltaRubric operates in two steps: acting first as a $\textit{Disagreement Planner}$, the model generates a neutral, instance-specific verification checklist. Transitioning into a $\textit{Checklist Verifier}$, it executes these self-generated checks against the image and question to produce the final grounded judgment. We formulate DeltaRubric as a multi-role reinforcement learning problem, jointly optimizing planning and verification capabilities. Validated on Qwen3-VL 4B and 8B Instruct models, DeltaRubric achieves solid empirical gains. For instance, On VL-RewardBench, it improves base model overall accuracy by $\textbf{+22.6}$ (4B) and $\textbf{+18.8}$ (8B) points, largely outperforming standard no-rubric baselines. The results demonstrate that decomposing evaluation into structured, verifiable steps leads to more reliable and generalizable multimodal reward modeling.

</details>


### [128] [BetaEdit: Null-Space Constrained Sequential Model Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09285)
*Bingqing Liu,Wei Liu,Yuhua Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文揭示了空空间方法在模型编辑中存在的知识泄漏问题，并分析了历史感知更新策略能提升顺序编辑性能的原因，提出了BetaEdit框架，在三个大语言模型上取得了优于先前方法的大规模顺序编辑性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有空空间方法依赖近似空空间导致知识泄漏，且在顺序编辑中性能严重下降；历史感知策略虽能缓解但原因不明。本文旨在揭示问题根源并设计更优的编辑框架。

Method: 首先分析了空空间方法中知识泄漏的机理以及历史感知更新为何有效；在此基础上提出BetaEdit框架，有效控制知识泄漏并将历史感知更新融入空空间范式。

Result: 在三个大语言模型和两个标准基准上的大量实验表明，BetaEdit在大规模顺序编辑这一挑战性场景中持续优于先前方法。

Conclusion: BetaEdit通过控制知识泄漏和引入历史感知更新，显著提升了空空间方法在顺序编辑任务中的表现，为大规模模型编辑提供了更可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: Null-space-based methods have garnered considerable attention in model editing by constraining updates to the null space of the pre-existing knowledge representation, thereby preserving the model's original behavior. However, in practice these methods rely on an approximate null space--leading to knowledge leakage--and further suffer from severe performance degradation during sequential editing. Recent work shows that history-aware editing strategies can empirically mitigate this decline, yet the underlying reason remains unclear. In this paper, we first expose the knowledge leakage inherent in existing null-space approaches and then analyze why history-aware updates effectively preserve both editing performance and general capabilities during long-horizon editing. Building on these insights, we propose BetaEdit, a refined framework that effectively controls the knowledge leakage and integrates history-aware updates into the null-space paradigm. Extensive experiments on three large language models across two standard benchmarks show that BetaEdit consistently outperforms prior methods in the challenging regime of massive-scale sequential editing. Code is available at: https://github.com/lbq8942/BetaEdit.

</details>


### [129] [Test-Time Speculation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09329)
*Avinash Kumar,Sujay Sanghavi,Poulami Das*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出测试时投机推理（Test-Time Speculation, TTS），一种在线蒸馏方法，在推理过程中持续适配草稿模型，解决现有投机解码器在生成长序列时接受长度退化的问题，将接受长度平均提升41%，最高72%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有投机解码器（如DFlash、EAGLE-3、PARD）的接受长度随着生成序列长度增加而显著退化，在几千个token内即降至接近1（无加速效果），根本原因是这些模型在短序列上离线训练，而推理时被迫匹配远超训练分布的长序列输出，导致投机解码器对长响应任务失效。

Method: 提出Test-Time Speculation (TTS)，一种在线蒸馏方法：利用投机解码中目标模型验证每个草稿token时已产生的训练信号（无需额外成本），将草稿模型视为学生、目标模型视为教师，在多个投机轮次中持续调整草稿模型参数，随着生成进行不断提高草稿模型的准确性。

Result: 在Qwen-3、Qwen-3.5和Llama3.1等多个模型上的实验表明，TTS相比现有最优投机解码器，接受长度平均提升41%，最高提升72%，且收益随生成长度增加而扩大。

Conclusion: TTS通过测试时在线蒸馏有效解决了投机解码器在接受长度上的分布偏移问题，使得投机解码能够适用于长序列生成任务，且改进幅度随序列长度增加而增大。

Abstract: Speculative decoding accelerates LLM inference by using a fast draft model to generate tokens and a more accurate target model to verify them. Its performance depends on the $\textit{acceptance length}$, or number of draft tokens accepted by the target. Our studies show that the acceptance length of even state-of-the-art speculators, like DFlash, EAGLE-3 and PARD degrade with generation length, reaching values close to 1 (i.e. no speedup) within just a few thousand output tokens, making speculators ineffective for long-response tasks. Acceptance lengths decline because most speculators are trained offline on short sequences, but are forced to match the target model on much longer outputs at inference, well beyond their training distribution.
  To address this issue, we propose $\textit{Test-Time Speculation (TTS)}$, an online distillation approach that continuously adapts the speculator at test-time. TTS leverages the key insight that the token verification step already invokes the target model for each draft token, providing the training signal needed to adapt the draft at no additional cost. Treating the draft as the student and the target as a teacher, TTS adjusts the draft over several speculation rounds, with each update improving the draft's accuracy as generation proceeds. Our results across multiple models from the Qwen-3, Qwen-3.5, and Llama3.1 families show that TTS improves acceptance lengths over state-of-the-art speculators by up to $72\%$ and $41\%$ on average, with the benefits scaling with increased generation lengths.

</details>


### [130] [RuPLaR : Efficient Latent Compression of LLM Reasoning Chains with Rule-Based Priors From Multi-Step to One-Step](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09346)
*Xiaocheng Luo,Kang Wang,Zaifu Zhan,Yuechi Zhou,Xiangyu Duan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为RuPLaR（基于规则先验的潜在推理压缩框架），通过单模型单步训练范式，让LLM在连续潜在空间中自主生成推理token，显著提升了潜在CoT方法的准确率（提升11.1%）并降低了token开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有潜在CoT方法存在多步/多模型范式带来的错误传播和协调开销问题，而传统CoT则受限于自然语言的低效性和表达限制。

Method: 提出One-Model One-Step压缩框架，包含三个训练目标：交叉熵保证答案一致性、KL散度对齐软token与规则先验（Soft Thinking约束）、表示空间中的问题-推理语义对齐约束。单阶段训练让模型自主生成潜在推理token。

Result: 相比现有潜在CoT方法准确率提升11.1%，同时使用最少的token数量。

Conclusion: RuPLaR压缩框架有效解决了潜在CoT的结构复杂性问题，在提升推理能力的同时保持了高效性，具有良好的可扩展性。

Abstract: The Chain-of-Thought (CoT) paradigm, while enhancing the interpretability of Large Language Models (LLMs), is constrained by the inefficiencies and expressive limits of natural language. Latent Chain-of-Thought (latent CoT) reasoning, which operates in a continuous latent space, offers a promising alternative but faces challenges from structural complexities in existing multi-step or multi-model paradigms, such as error propagation and coordination overhead. In this paper, we introduce One-Model One-Step, a novel compression framework for Latent Reasoning with Rule-Based Priors(RuPLaR) to address this challenge. Our method trains an LLM to autonomously generate latent reasoning tokens in a single training stage, guided by rule-based prior probability distributions, thereby eliminating cascaded processes and inter-model dependencies. To ensure reasoning quality, we design a joint training objective that enforces answer consistency via cross-entropy, aligns soft tokens with rule-based priors via KL divergence (the Soft Thinking constraint), and adds a problem-thought semantic alignment constraint in the representation space. Extensive experiments show that our compression framework not only improves accuracy by 11.1% over existing latent CoT methods but also achieves this with minimal token usage, underscoring its effectiveness and extensibility. Code: https://github.com/xiaocen-luo/RuPLaR.

</details>


### [131] [HOME-KGQA: A Benchmark Dataset for Multimodal Knowledge Graph Question Answering on Household Daily Activities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09348)
*Shusaku Egami,Aoi Ohta,Tomoki Tsujimura,Masaki Asada,Tatsuya Ishigaki,Ken Fukuda,Masahiro Hamasaki,Hiroya Takamura*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了HOME-KGQA，一个基于日常家庭活动多模态知识图谱的KGQA基准数据集。该数据集包含涉及多级时空推理、多模态对齐和聚合函数的复杂多跳问题。实验表明，现有LLM-based KGQA方法在该数据集上性能显著下降，揭示了真实场景部署中的挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有KGQA基准数据集存在三个局限性：(1)偏向百科知识，(2)局限于单一模态，(3)缺乏细粒度时空数据，这限制了其在具身AI真实场景中的应用。为填补这一空缺，需要构建更贴近真实世界的KGQA基准。

Method: 构建基于日常家庭活动多模态知识图谱的HOME-KGQA数据集，包含复杂多跳自然语言问题及对应的图数据库查询语言，涵盖多级时空推理、多模态对齐和聚合函数等挑战性任务。

Result: LLM-based KGQA方法在HOME-KGQA上的表现显著低于其在现有数据集上的性能，表明现有方法无法有效处理该数据集带来的挑战。

Conclusion: HOME-KGQA揭示了现有KGQA方法在真实世界部署中的重大挑战，为未来研究提供了新的方向和基准。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) provide flexible natural language processing capabilities, while knowledge graphs (KGs) offer explicit and structured knowledge. Integrating these two in a complementary manner enables the development of reliable and verifiable AI systems. In particular, knowledge graph question answering (KGQA) has attracted attention as a means to reduce LLM hallucinations and to leverage knowledge beyond the training data. However, existing KGQA benchmark datasets are biased toward encyclopedic knowledge, limited to a single modality, and lack fine-grained spatiotemporal data, which limits their applicability to real-world scenarios targeted by Embodied AI. We introduce HOME-KGQA, a novel KGQA benchmark dataset built on a multimodal KG of daily household activities. HOME-KGQA consists of complex, multi-hop natural language questions paired with graph database query languages. Compared to existing benchmarks, it includes more challenging questions that involve multi-level spatiotemporal reasoning, multimodal grounding, and aggregate functions. Experimental results show that the LLM-based KGQA methods fail to achieve performance comparable to that on existing datasets when evaluated on HOME-KGQA. This highlights significant challenges that should be addressed for the real-world deployment of KGQA systems. Our dataset is available at https://github.com/aistairc/home-kgqa

</details>


### [132] [Cross-Cultural Transfer of Emoji Semantics and Sentiment in Financial Social Media](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09414)
*Ahmed Mahrous,Roberto Di Pietro*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究探究了表情符号在金融沟通中的跨语言、跨平台和跨资产迁移能力。基于Twitter和StockTwits四种语言的大规模语料，发现表情符号在语义和情感极性上具有稳定性，尽管其使用频率存在跨社区差异。包含表情符号的模型相比纯文本模型能有效减少迁移差距，表明金融沟通中存在部分共享的"表情符号代码"，能提供简洁、与语言无关的情感线索，提升模型跨市场泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线金融沟通中表情符号被广泛使用，但其能否在不同语言、平台和资产社区间提供可迁移的情感信号尚不明确。

Method: 使用四种语言的Twitter和StockTwits大规模语料，测量跨社区分歧，评估在纯表情、纯文本和文本+表情输入下训练的情感模型。

Result: 表情符号频率跨社区存在差异（尤以跨语言为甚），但语义和情感极性基本稳定。跨资产迁移性能下降最小，跨语言迁移最具挑战性。包含表情符号一致地减少了与纯文本模型相比的迁移差距。

Conclusion: 金融沟通中存在部分共享的"表情符号代码"，表情符号提供了简洁、与语言无关的情感线索，能改善模型跨市场和平台的泛化能力。

Abstract: Emojis are widely used in online financial communication, but it is unclear whether they provide transferable sentiment signals across languages, platforms, and asset communities. This study examines the extent to which emoji usage, semantics, and sentiment polarity remain stable across financial communities, and how these layers influence zero-shot sentiment transfer. Using large corpora of Twitter and StockTwits posts in four languages, we measure cross-community divergence and evaluate sentiment models trained under emoji-only, text-only, and text+emoji inputs.
  We find that emoji frequencies differ across communities, especially across languages, but their semantics and sentiment polarity are largely stable. Cross-asset transferability shows minimal degradation, while cross-language transfer remains the most challenging. Including emojis consistently reduces transfer gaps relative to text-only models. These results indicate that financial communication exhibits a partially shared ``emoji code,'' and that emojis provide compact, language-independent sentiment cues that improve model generalization across markets and platforms.

</details>


### [133] [Perception Without Engagement: Dissecting the Causal Discovery Deficit in LMMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09422)
*Jiafeng Liang,Zhihao Zhu,Zihan Zhang,Baoqi Ren,Shixin Jiang,Runxuan Liu,Tao Ren,Ming Liu,See-Kiong Ng,Bing Qin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出ProCauEval评估协议和ADPO强化学习方法，系统揭示了LMM在视频因果发现中过度依赖文本先验而忽视视觉证据的问题，并通过对抗蒸馏策略有效提升模型的视觉推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LMM在视频理解中表现出对文本先验捷径的依赖，但仅通过响应准确率无法揭示缺陷的根源与程度，缺乏从机制层面诊断模型因果发现能力的评估方法。

Method: 提出ProCauEval扰动评估协议，通过五种受控配置系统操纵视觉和文本模态以分解贡献；提出ADPO强化学习框架，通过最大化原始与视觉损坏输入下的策略分布散度，迫使模型基于视觉证据而非文本捷径进行推理。

Result: 17个主流LMM的评估表明：模型能正确感知视频内容但系统性地未充分利用视觉信息；更强的后训练反而加剧文本先验依赖；更高基线性能伴随更大的扰动脆弱性。ADPO有效提升视觉参与度且不损害基础理解能力。

Conclusion: ProCauEval从结果评估转向机制诊断，揭示了LMM因果发现中的文本先验捷径缺陷；ADPO通过负教师对齐的强化学习为缓解这一缺陷提供了初步可行方案。

Abstract: Although Large Multimodal Models (LMMs) have achieved strong performance on general video understanding, their susceptibility to textual prior shortcuts during causal discovery has been recognized as a critical deficit. The underlying mechanisms of this phenomenon remain incompletely understood, as existing benchmarks only measure response accuracy without revealing the sources and extent of the deficit. We introduce ProCauEval, a perturbation-based evaluation protocol that shifts from outcome assessment to mechanism diagnosis, probing causal discovery through five controlled configurations that systematically manipulate visual and textual modalities to decompose their respective contributions to model behavior and dissect the failure modes. Evaluating 17 mainstream LMMs, we find that models faithfully perceive video content yet systematically underexploit it during causal reasoning. We further observe that stronger post-training amplifies rather than mitigates textual prior reliance, and that higher baseline performance correlates with greater fragility under perturbation. To address these, we propose Anti-Distillation Policy Optimization (ADPO), a reinforcement learning framework built on negative teacher alignment, which augments GRPO by explicitly pushing the policy away from a prior-only counterfactual teacher induced by visual corruption. Specifically, ADPO maximizes the divergence between the policy distributions conditioned on the original and visually corrupted inputs, thereby forcing the model to ground its reasoning in visual evidence rather than textual shortcuts. Extensive experiments show that ADPO improves visual engagement without sacrificing fundamental comprehension, thus offering a preliminary step toward reliable causal discovery.

</details>


### [134] [PumpSense: Real-Time Detection and Target Extraction of Crypto Pump-and-Dumps on Telegram](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09431)
*Ahmed Mahrous,Roberto Di Pietro*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文构建了包含28万条Telegram帖子的加密货pump-and-dump协调消息数据集，提出基于LightGBM和BGE-M3的实时泵检测方法（F1分别达0.79和0.83，延迟低至50ms/样本），并首次建立操纵币种及交易所提取基准，证明LLM方法（准确率0.91）优于传统规则方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有针对加密货币pump-and-dump方案的研究未能同时实现可靠结果与快速响应，主要原因在于缺乏公开的消息级标注数据以及设计选择上的局限。

Method: 1) 构建了超过28万条Telegram帖子的人工标注语料库，识别出2,246条泵公告及其目标加密货币和交易所。2) 定义两个任务：实时泵公告检测和目标加密货币/交易所提取。3) 检测任务对比了轻量级树模型LightGBM（F1=0.79, 延迟9.4s/样本）与基于Transformer的BGE-M3（F1=0.83, 延迟50ms/样本）。4) 提取任务对比传统规则方法与LLM方法。

Result: BGE-M3模型在泵检测任务上达到F1=0.83且延迟仅50ms/样本，实现了在单个Telegram消息窗口级别近即时的泵检测。在目标提取任务中，LLM方法取得0.91的准确率，显著优于传统规则方法。

Conclusion: 基于协调消息的分析可在毫秒级实现泵检测——远快于依赖市场数据的方法（需数秒等待异常交易出现），且首次建立了操纵币种和交易所提取的基准，证明了LLM在解决ticker歧义问题上的有效性。

Abstract: Cryptocurrency pump-and-dump schemes coordinated via Telegram threaten market integrity. However, existing research addressing this specific threat has not yet produced solutions that combine reliable results with fast response. This is in part due to the absence of publicly available, message-level labeled data, as well as design choices. In this paper, we address both issues. In particular, we introduce a corpus of over 280,000 Telegram posts from 39 pump-organizing groups, all manually reviewed to identify 2,246 pump announcements and their targeted cryptocurrency and exchange. Leveraging this dataset, we define two tasks: real-time pump-announcement detection and target cryptocurrency/exchange extraction. For detection, we compare two machine-learning models: a lightweight tree-based LightGBM classifier (F1=0.79, latency=9.4 s/sample) and a transformer-based BGE-M3 (F1=0.83, latency=50 ms/sample). With our proposed approach, we show that message analysis can achieve near-instant pump detection at the level of individual Telegram message windows. Unlike prior work that relies purely on market data and typically detects pumps tens of seconds after abnormal trading activity is observed, our method operates directly on the coordination messages themselves and can be evaluated in microseconds per window on commodity hardware. To our knowledge, we also establish the first benchmark for manipulated coin and exchange extraction. We demonstrate that traditional rule-based extraction methods, widely relied upon in prior literature, are ineffective due to ticker ambiguity. In contrast, LLMs achieve the highest accuracy with a score of 0.91.

</details>


### [135] [Key Coverage Matters: Semi-Structured Extraction of OCR Clinical Reports](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09440)
*Yu Wang,Yingyun Li,Ying Qin,Haiyang Qian*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于规范键条件抽取式问答的方法，用于从OCR处理的临床报告中提取关键字段。通过迭代式键挖掘、归一化、聚类和轻量人工验证维护规范键清单，并引入键覆盖率作为评估指标。在20多家医院真实报告上的实验表明，键覆盖率是影响端到端性能的主导因素，Top-90键覆盖下模型在精确匹配和边界容忍匹配上分别达到0.839和0.893的F1分数，优于微调的Qwen3-0.6B基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床报告因隐私法规和数据孤岛而碎片化，患者跨院就医时通常携带纸质或扫描报告，阻碍了EHR集成、纵向回顾及下游应用（如患者管理、随访、真实世界研究和临床试验匹配）。尽管OCR可以数字化这些报告，但临床文档异质性高、OCR文本噪声大且需低成本本地部署，使得可靠提取仍然困难。

Method: 将问题形式化为基于规范键条件（canonical key-conditioned）的抽取式问答。核心方法包括：(1) 通过迭代式键挖掘、归一化、聚类和轻量人工验证来维护开放规范键清单；(2) 引入键覆盖率（key coverage）作为量化清单完整性的指标；(3) 使用0.2B参数的BERT模型进行问答提取。

Result: 在20多家医院真实报告上，模型性能随键覆盖率单调提升。Top-90规范键覆盖下，精确匹配F1为0.839，边界容忍匹配F1为0.893，均优于微调的Qwen3-0.6B基线。

Conclusion: 键覆盖率是影响端到端性能的主导因素。该方法依赖半结构化临床报告中与语言无关的键值组织形式，虽标注语料为中文，但可适配其他语言，只需构建相应的规范键清单和别名映射。

Abstract: Clinical reports are often fragmented across healthcare institutions because privacy regulations and data silos limit direct information sharing. When patients seek care at a different hospital, they often carry paper or scanned reports from prior visits. This hinders EHR integration and longitudinal review, and downstream applications that depend on more complete patient records, such as patient management, follow-up care, real-world studies, and clinical-trial matching. Although OCR can digitize such reports, reliable extraction remains challenging because clinical documents are heterogeneous, OCR text is noisy, and many healthcare settings require low-cost on-premise deployment. We formulate this problem as canonical key-conditioned extractive question answering over OCR-derived clinical reports. Because the key fields are neither fixed nor known in advance, the key space is open. We maintain a canonical key inventory through iterative key mining, normalization, clustering, and lightweight human verification, and introduce key coverage as a metric to quantify inventory completeness. Using a 0.2B BERT-based model, experiments on real-world reports from more than 20 hospitals show performance improves monotonically with key coverage. The model achieves F1 scores of 0.839 and 0.893 under exact match and boundary-tolerant matching, respectively, once the Top-90 canonical keys are covered. These results show that key coverage is a dominant factor for end-to-end performance. At Top-90 coverage, our model outperforms a fine-tuned Qwen3-0.6B baseline under exact match. Although our annotated corpus is Chinese, the method relies on the language-agnostic key-value organization of semi-structured clinical reports and can be adapted to other settings given an appropriate canonical key inventory and alias mapping.

</details>


### [136] [Beyond Position Bias: Shifting Context Compression from Position-Driven to Semantic-Driven](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09463)
*Jiwei Tang,Zhijing Huang,Xinyu Zhang,Chen Jason Zhang,Jianxing Yu,Libin Zheng,Rui Meng,Jian Yin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出SeCo方法，将上下文压缩从位置驱动转向语义驱动，通过选择查询相关令牌作为语义中心并聚合剩余令牌，消除了位置偏差，在14个基准测试中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有软提示压缩方法受位置偏差约束，主要依赖在固定位置插入可学习令牌或按物理布局分组，导致性能不稳定和语义碎片化。

Method: 提出语义一致性上下文压缩（SeCo），动态选择与查询相关的令牌作为语义中心，通过一致性加权合并聚合其余令牌，在语义空间中实现上下文压缩。

Result: 在14个基准测试和两个骨干模型上，SeCo在下游任务性能、推理延迟和域外鲁棒性方面均表现优异。

Conclusion: SeCo通过语义驱动的压缩策略有效消除了位置偏差，在保持语义一致性的同时提升了长上下文场景下的效率和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated exceptional performance across diverse tasks. However, their deployment in long-context scenarios faces high computational overhead and information redundancy. While soft prompt compression has emerged as a promising way to mitigate these costs by compressing sequences into compact embeddings, existing paradigms remain fundamentally constrained by position bias: they primarily rely on learnable tokens insertion at fixed positions or group tokens according to their physical token layout, thereby inducing performance instability and semantic fragmentation. To overcome this bottleneck, we propose Semantic Consistency Context Compression (SeCo), a method that shifts context compression from position-driven to semantic-driven. Rather than constraint by physical token layout, SeCo dynamically anchors compression directly in the semantic space by selecting query-relevant tokens as semantic centers and aggregating remaining tokens via consistency-weighted merging. This design inherently preserves semantic consistency while eliminating position bias. Extensive experiments on 14 benchmarks across two backbone models demonstrate that SeCo consistently shows superiority in downstream tasks, inference latency, and out-of-domain robustness. The code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/seco-EE5E.

</details>


### [137] [Align and Shine: Building High-Quality Sentence-Aligned Corpora for Multilingual Text Simplification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09476)
*Kenji Hilasaca,Nouran Khallaf,Serge Sharoff*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对英语以外语言缺乏大规模高质量文本简化数据集的问题，通过众包方式从可比语料库中收集并处理简化数据，构建了涵盖加泰罗尼亚语、英语、法语、意大利语和西班牙语的多语言句子级对齐语料库，并公开了该数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 英语以外的语言缺乏大规模、高质量的文本简化数据集，这限制了多语言文本简化模型的训练与评估。

Method: 从可比语料库（comparable corpora）中通过众包方式收集简化数据；设计文档级数据中的句子级对齐机制。

Result: 构建了包含加泰罗尼亚语、英语、法语、意大利语和西班牙语五种语言的对齐句子对数据集，并已公开。

Conclusion: 该多语言数据集可用于文本简化系统的训练与测试，弥补了英语以外语言资源的不足。

Abstract: Text simplification plays a crucial role in improving the accessibility and comprehensibility of written information for diverse audiences, including language learners and readers with limited literacy. Despite its importance, large-scale, high-quality datasets for training and evaluating text simplification models remain scarce for languages other than English. This paper reports an experimental study on the collection and processing of crowd-sourced simplification data from comparable corpora to construct a corpus suitable for both training and testing text simplification systems across multiple languages (Catalan, English, French, Italian and Spanish). We report mechanisms for sentence-level alignment from document-level data. The resulting dataset of the aligned sentence pairs is publicly available.

</details>


### [138] [A Cognitively Grounded Bayesian Framework for Misinformation Susceptibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09483)
*Pranava Madhyastha*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为BPL（有界务实听者）的认知驱动贝叶斯框架，用于建模信息失序的易感性。BPL扩展了理性言语行为理论，引入了三种认知边界：递归深度边界（工作记忆限制）、先验压缩参数（信息瓶颈）和可用性样本量（显著性加权的重要性采样）。该框架能预测错误信息易感性、标注者分歧以及对误、伪、恶意信息的差异化脆弱性。在LIAR和MultiFC基准上验证了竞争性的真实性分类能力和深度不匹配悖论的实验支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有信息失序研究缺乏解释人类认知局限如何影响信息真实性判断的机制性模型。作者希望通过引入认知边界来弥补Rational Speech Act理论在建模信息失序易感性方面的不足。

Method: 在Rational Speech Act理论上扩展三层认知边界：递归深度边界（工作记忆限制）、先验压缩参数（信息瓶颈）、可用性样本量（显著性加权的importance sampling），构建BPL贝叶斯框架。

Result: 在LIAR和MultiFC数据集上验证了竞争性的真实性分类性能，并提供了对深度不匹配悖论的实验支持。

Conclusion: BPL框架能够有效建模信息失序中的认知机制，包括错误信息易感性、标注者分歧以及不同类型信息的差异化影响，为理解人类如何受信息失序影响提供了认知计算视角。

Abstract: In this (work in progress) paper, we present Bounded Pragmatic Listener (or BPL), a cognitively grounded Bayesian framework for modelling susceptibility to information disorder. BPL extends Rational Speech Act theory with three cognitively motivated bounds derived from the bounded rationality literature with a) a recursion depth bound (that emphasises working memory limits);b) a prior compression parameter (which is oriented at capturing information bottleneck); and c) an availability sample size (that operationalises importance sampling with saliency-weighted proposals). This allows us to test predictions about misinformation susceptibility, annotator disagreement, and the differential vulnerability to mis-, dis-, and mal-information as defined in the Information Disorder framework. We validate BPL on the LIAR and MultiFC benchmarks showcasing competitive veracity classification and experimental support for the depth-mismatch paradox.

</details>


### [139] [APCD: Adaptive Path-Contrastive Decoding for Reliable Large Language Model Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09492)
*Tianyu Zheng,Hong Wu,Jiaji Zhong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出自适应路径对比解码（APCD），一种多路径解码框架，通过熵驱动路径扩展和散度感知路径对比，在保持解码效率的同时提升大语言模型的事实准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在自回归解码中因错误累积导致幻觉问题，早期次优token选择会误导后续生成。现有方法缺乏何时分支以及如何调节路径间交互的 principled 策略。

Method: APCD包含两个组件：(1) 熵驱动路径扩展：利用Shannon熵衡量预测不确定性，仅在存在多个合理延续时进行分支；(2) 散度感知路径对比：鼓励多样化推理轨迹，同时随预测分布发散动态衰减路径间影响。

Result: 在八个基准测试上实现更优的事实准确性，同时保持解码效率。

Conclusion: APCD通过自适应探索和受控路径交互有效提升了输出可靠性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) often suffer from hallucinations due to error accumulation in autoregressive decoding, where suboptimal early token choices misguide subsequent generation. Although multi-path decoding can improve robustness by exploring alternative trajectories, existing methods lack principled strategies for determining when to branch and how to regulate inter-path interactions. We propose Adaptive Path-Contrastive Decoding (APCD), a multi-path decoding framework that improves output reliability through adaptive exploration and controlled path interaction. APCD consists of two components: (1) Entropy-Driven Path Expansion, which delays branching until predictive uncertainty - measured by Shannon entropy over top candidate tokens - indicates multiple plausible continuations; and (2) Divergence-Aware Path Contrast, which encourages diverse reasoning trajectories while dynamically attenuating inter-path influence as prediction distributions diverge. Experiments on eight benchmarks demonstrate improved factual accuracy while maintaining decoding efficiency. Our code is available at https://github.com/zty-king/APCD.

</details>


### [140] [Beyond Language: Format-Agnostic Reasoning Subspaces in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09496)
*Aojie Yuan,Zhiyuan Su*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发现大语言模型在中间层存在一个与具体格式无关的通用推理子空间（FARS），仅用10维即可跨格式（英文散文、Python代码、数学符号）保留90-96%的模型输出，并揭示了声明式与程序式表征之间的不对称性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究大语言模型在处理不同符号系统（自然语言、代码、数学符号）中的相同推理时，是否共享一个共同的内部表征基底，即验证跨格式推理的神经表征同构性。

Method: 构建TriForm基准（18概念×6格式×3实例=324刺激），使用排列校正RSA、跨格式探针分析和激活修补三种方法，对五个LLM（1.6B-8B）跨三种架构家族进行研究。

Result: 发现中间层存在格式无关推理子空间（FARS），概念中心PCA提取的10维子空间可将概念结构放大3倍同时抑制格式信息；交叉格式修补中仅替换这10维即可保留90-96%输出（远超完整替换的44-56%和方差最大PCA的60-74%）；消融则导致目标性破坏。FARS可泛化到未见概念，跨架构收敛（CCA>0.79）。发现声明式与程序式表征不对称：散文与数学的兼容性远高于二者与代码的兼容性。

Conclusion: 为柏拉图表征假说提供了模态内的证据，表明不同符号系统间存在共享的推理子空间，且格式间差异的关键维度是声明式vs程序式，而非语言vs形式化。

Abstract: Large language models represent the same reasoning in vastly different surface forms -- English prose, Python code, mathematical notation -- yet whether they share a common internal substrate across these symbolic systems remains unknown. We introduce the TriForm Benchmark (18 concepts x 6 forms x 3 instances = 324 stimuli) and study five LLMs (1.6B-8B) across three architecture families. Using permutation-corrected RSA, cross-form probing, and activation patching, we find converging evidence for a Format-Agnostic Reasoning Subspace (FARS) in middle layers. We make FARS concrete: concept-centroid PCA extracts a 10-dimensional subspace that amplifies concept structure 3x while suppressing form information to near zero. Replacing only these 10 dimensions during cross-form patching preserves 90-96% of model output -- far exceeding both full activation replacement (44-56%) and variance-maximizing PCA (60-74%) -- while ablating them causes targeted disruption. FARS generalizes to held-out concepts and converges across architectures (CCA > 0.79 for all model pairs), providing within-modality evidence for the Platonic Representation Hypothesis. We further discover a declarative-procedural asymmetry: representations are far more compatible between prose and mathematics than between either and code, suggesting that the critical axis of divergence is not linguistic vs. formal but declarative vs. procedural.

</details>


### [141] [Hidden Error Awareness in Chain-of-Thought Reasoning: The Signal Is Diagnostic, Not Causal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09502)
*Aojie Yuan,Zhiyuan Julian Su,Haiyue Zhang,Yi Nian,Yue Zhao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文发现，思维链(CoT)推理中，模型内部隐藏状态能够高精度(0.95 AUROC)检测自身推理错误，但从输出文本中无法识别这一差异(文本分类器仅0.59)。尽管模型"知道"自己错了，但所有利用这一信号纠正错误的干预尝试均告失败，表明该信号是诊断性而非因果性的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 质疑CoT推理中"生成的推理过程反映模型内部计算"这一基本假设，探究模型是否在内部层面意识到自身的推理错误，以及这种意识能否用于纠正错误。

Method: 使用线性探针从模型隐藏状态预测推理轨迹正确性(AUROC 0.95)，对比口头表达的置信度(错误轨迹4.55/5，正确轨迹4.87/5)和基于文本表面的分类器(0.59 AUROC)。在Qwen、Llama、Phi三个模型家族(1.5B-72B)及DeepSeek-R1上进行验证。尝试四种干预：激活引导、探针引导的best-of-N、自我纠正、激活修补。

Result: 线性探针从第一步推理就能预测正确性(0.79 AUROC)，口头置信度无法区分正确与错误轨迹(4.87 vs 4.55)，文本表面分类器仅0.59 AUROC。所有四种利用该信号纠正错误的干预均失败，激活修补甚至完全破坏输出连贯性。

Conclusion: 模型内部确实存在对自身推理错误的隐式感知，该信号是诊断性的（反映计算质量）而非因果性的（无法用于重定向计算），揭示了可解释性干预的边界：错误表征与事实知识表征本质不同，前者无法通过编辑手段修正。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) prompting assumes that generated reasoning reflects a model's internal computation. We show this assumption is wrong in a specific, measurable way: models internally detect their own reasoning errors but outwardly express confidence in them. A linear probe on hidden states predicts trace correctness with 0.95 AUROC -- from the very first reasoning step (0.79) -- while verbalized confidence for wrong traces is 4.55/5, nearly identical to correct ones (4.87/5). A text-surface classifier achieves only 0.59 on the same data, confirming a 0.20-point gap invisible in the generated text. This hidden error awareness holds across three model families (Qwen, Llama, Phi), 1.5B-72B parameters, and RL-trained reasoning models (DeepSeek-R1, 0.852 AUROC). The natural question is whether this signal can fix the errors it detects. It cannot. Four interventions -- activation steering, probe-guided best-of-N, self-correction, and activation patching -- all fail; patching destroys output coherence entirely. The signal is diagnostic, not causal: a readout of computation quality, not a lever to redirect it. This delineates a boundary for mechanistic interpretability: error representations during reasoning are fundamentally different from the factual knowledge representations that prior work has successfully edited.

</details>


### [142] [Assessment of RAG and Fine-Tuning for Industrial Question-Answering-Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09533)
*Jakob Sturm,Josef Pichlmeier,Christian Bernhard,Maka Karalashvili,Johannes Klepsch,Georg Groh,Andre Luckow*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究比较了RAG和微调两种方法在企业QA系统中适配领域知识的效果，发现RAG在成本-准确性权衡上更优，能使开源模型达到接近闭源模型的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在企业QA系统中，LLMs需要适配领域特定知识，RAG和微调是两种主流方法，但从成本-准确性权衡角度，尚不清楚哪种方法更适合工业场景。

Method: 在汽车行业两个封闭数据集上，评估RAG和微调对答案质量和运营成本的影响，并扩展Cost-of-Pass框架以联合评估输出质量、生成成本和用户交互成本。

Result: 闭源模型开箱即用性能最佳，但开源模型结合RAG可达到可比质量；总体而言，RAG是对闭源和开源模型最有效且成本效率最高的适配方法。

Conclusion: RAG在成本-准确性权衡上优于微调，是更优的领域适配方案。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly employed in enterprise question-answering (QA) systems, requiring adaptation to domain-specific knowledge. Among the most prevalent methods for incorporating such knowledge are Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) and fine-tuning (FT). Yet, from a cost-accuracy trade-off perspective, it remains unclear which approach best suits industry scenarios. This study examines the impact of RAG and FT on two closed datasets specific to the automotive industry, assessing answer quality and operational costs. We extend the Cost-of-Pass framework proposed by Erol et al. (arXiv:2504.13359) to jointly assess output quality, generation cost, and user interaction cost. Our findings reveal that while premium models perform best out of the box, open-source models can achieve comparable quality when enhanced with RAG. Overall, RAG emerges as the most effective and cost-efficient adaptation method for both closed- and open-source models.

</details>


### [143] [TAD: Temporal-Aware Trajectory Self-Distillation for Fast and Accurate Diffusion LLM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09536)
*Haoyang Zhou,Li Kong,Shijie Ren,Xiting Wang,Shuang Liang,Guowei Wang,Zhenxuan Pan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出TAD（时间感知轨迹自蒸馏框架），通过区分近端和远端掩码令牌并分别采用硬交叉熵损失和软KL散度损失训练，有效解决扩散大语言模型在并行文本生成中准确率与并行度之间的权衡问题，在LLaDA上质量模型准确率从46.2%提升至51.6%，速度模型AUP从46.2提升至257.1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散大语言模型（dLLMs）在并行文本生成中面临准确率与并行度（TPF）之间的权衡问题，现有的加速方法往往以牺牲准确率为代价提升速度，缺乏有效的解决方案。

Method: 提出TAD框架：1）数据构建阶段：基于提示和真实响应，让教师模型生成解码轨迹并记录中间掩码状态；2）根据剩余解码步数将掩码位置分为近端和远端子集；3）近端令牌使用硬交叉熵损失（以教师轨迹令牌为标签）训练学生模型；4）远端令牌使用软KL散度损失训练，提供更柔和的监督并保留未来规划知识；5）由此衍生出两种部署配置：优先准确率的质量模型和偏好激进加速的速度模型。

Result: 在LLaDA模型上，质量模型将平均准确率从46.2%提升至51.6%；速度模型将平均AUP从46.2提升至257.1，显著改善了准确率-并行度权衡。

Conclusion: TAD通过时间感知的轨迹自蒸馏，有效解决了dLLMs中准确率与并行度的矛盾，同时支持在不同部署场景下灵活切换质量优先或速度优先的配置。

Abstract: Diffusion large language models (dLLMs) offer a promising paradigm for parallel text generation, but in practice they face an accuracy-parallelism trade-off, where increasing tokens per forward (TPF) often degrades generation quality. Existing acceleration methods often gain speed at the cost of accuracy. To address this limitation, we propose TAD, a Temporal-Aware trajectory self-Distillation framework. During data construction, we condition a teacher model on both the prompt and the ground-truth response to generate decoding trajectories, recording the intermediate masked states throughout the process. Based on how many decoding steps remain before each masked token is revealed, we partition masked positions into near and distant subsets. For near tokens, we train the student with a hard cross-entropy loss using the teacher trajectory tokens as labels, encouraging confident predictions for tokens that are about to be decoded. For distant tokens, we apply a soft KL divergence loss between the teacher and student token distributions, providing softer supervision and preserving future planning knowledge. This temporal-aware partition naturally gives rise to two deployment configurations: a Quality model that prioritizes accuracy and a Speed model that favors more aggressive acceleration. Experiments show that TAD consistently improves the accuracy-parallelism trade-off. On LLaDA, it raises average accuracy from 46.2\% to 51.6\% with the Quality model and average AUP from 46.2 to 257.1 with the Speed model. Our code is available at: https://github.com/BHmingyang/TAD

</details>


### [144] [TacoMAS: Test-Time Co-Evolution of Topology and Capability in LLM-based Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09539)
*Chen Xu,Yicheng Hu,Ruizi Wang,Xinyu Lin,Wenjie Wang,Dongrui Liu,Fuli Feng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: TacoMAS是一个测试时协同进化框架，通过快慢双循环机制（快速能力更新+慢速拓扑演化）动态优化多智能体系统，在四个基准测试中平均提升13.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有测试时自适应方法要么保持拓扑固定不变，要么仅单独调整拓扑或能力，缺乏协同进化机制。理论和实验表明，有效测试时演化需要联合调整能力和拓扑两个维度，且需在不同时间尺度上进行。

Method: 将MAS推理建模为在线图自适应问题：快速能力循环使用轨迹级反馈更新智能体角色特定能力；慢速元LLM驱动拓扑循环执行边缘编辑、智能体添加/删除等出生-死亡操作。快-慢双循环设计推动MAS向任务条件稳定均衡演化。

Result: 在四个基准测试中击败近20个多智能体基线方法，平均改进13.3%。

Conclusion: 证明了测试时协同进化能力和拓扑的必要性，快慢双循环设计能有效平衡任务适应性和协调稳定性。

Abstract: Multi-agent systems (MAS) have emerged as a promising paradigm for solving complex tasks. Recent work has explored self-evolving MAS that automatically optimize agent capabilities or communication topologies. However, existing methods either learn a topology that remains fixed at inference time or adapt only the topology or capability during inference. We empirically and theoretically show that effective test-time evolution requires jointly adapting both axes, but on different time scales: capabilities should update rapidly to handle emerging subtasks, while the topology should evolve more slowly to preserve coordination stability. We then introduce TacoMAS, a test-time co-evolution framework for dynamic MAS. TacoMAS formulates MAS inference as a task of online graph adaptation, where nodes represent agents with role-specific capabilities and edges define their communication topology. During inference, a fast capability loop updates agent expertise using trajectory-level feedback, while a slow meta-LLM-driven topology loop performs agents' birth-death operations on MAS, including edge edit, agent addition, and agent removal. We further show that this fast-slow design drives MAS evolution toward a task-conditioned stable equilibrium. Experiments on four benchmarks demonstrate that TacoMAS outperforms nearly 20 multi-agent baselines, achieving an average improvement of 13.3% over the strongest baseline. The codes are released at https://github.com/chenxu2-gif/TacoMAS-MultiAgent.

</details>


<div id='cs.GR'></div>

# cs.GR [[Back]](#toc)

### [145] [Explicit Inversion of Planar NURBS Curves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08087)
*Michelangelo Marsala,Pablo Mazón*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本文证明了平面NURBS曲线参数化存在有理样条表示的逆映射，并构造了相应的有理样条函数族及显式计算公c，通过实例验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NURBS曲线在CAD/CAGD中广泛应用，但其逆映射（从曲线几何到参数域）通常缺乏有理样条的精确表示，现有方法多为近似或迭代求解。

Method: 在曲线上构造一族有理样条函数，给出显式计算公c，并证明逆参数化可表示为这些函数的线性组合。

Result: 证明了平面NURBS曲线参数化的逆映射存在有理样条表示，并提供了多个数值示例验证方法有效性。

Conclusion: 平面NURBS曲线的逆参数化可以精确地用有理样条表示，为曲线几何处理提供了新的理论工具。

Abstract: We prove that a general planar NURBS curve parametrization $φ: [u_0,u_m] \xrightarrow{} C \subset \mathbb{R}^2$ admits an inverse map $φ^{-1}: C \xrightarrow{} [u_0,u_m]$ defined by rational splines. More specifically, we construct a family of rational spline functions on the curve $C$, present explicit formulas for their computation, and prove that the inverse parametrization admits a representation as a linear combination of these functions. Several examples are provided to illustrate the effectiveness of the proposed approach.

</details>


### [146] [Alice v1: Distillation-Enhanced Video Generation Surpassing Closed-Source Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08115)
*Wang Xiaoyu,Phong Nguyen,Chen Zhao*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: Alice v1是一个140亿参数的开源视频生成模型，通过一致性蒸馏与分数正则化（rCM）实现了超越教师模型（Wan2.2）的质量（VBench从84.0提升至91.2），在4步去噪（约8秒）下生成5秒720p视频，同时超越闭源系统Veo3和Sora2。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统蒸馏方法以牺牲质量换取速度，作者希望证明基于rCM的蒸馏可以超越教师模型质量，同时实现大幅加速。

Method: (1) 一致性蒸馏配合分数正则化（rCM），作为模式-seeking目标集中概率质量于高质量输出；(2) 针对失败模式（物理、手、面部）的合成数据管线与硬示例挖掘；(3) 一致性强制作为隐式正则化，消除对特定噪声样本的"幸运路径"依赖。

Result: Alice v1生成5秒720p 24fps视频仅需4步去噪（~8秒/H100），相比教师模型50步实现7倍加速；VBench得分从84.0提升至91.2，超越Veo3（~90）和Sora2（~88），在人类偏好研究中具有竞争力。

Conclusion: rCM蒸馏可以超越教师模型质量，开放全部模型权重、代码、数据管线和评估脚本以推动开源视频生成研究。

Abstract: Wepresent Alice v1, a 14-billion parameter open-source video generation model that achieves state-of-the-art quality through consistency distillation with score regularization (rCM). Contrary to conventional distillation-which trades quality for speed-we demonstrate that rCM-based distillation can exceed teacher model quality. We attribute this to three mechanisms: (1) the score regularization term acts as a mode-seeking objective that concentrates probability mass on high-quality outputs rather than covering the full teacher distribution, (2) our targeted synthetic data pipeline with hard example mining provides training signal specifically for failure modes (physics, hands, faces) that the teacher handles inconsistently, and (3) consistency enforcement acts as implicit regularization, eliminating "lucky path" dependence on specific noise samples. Alice v1 generates 5-second 720p videos at 24fps in 4 denoising steps (~8 seconds on H100), a 7x speedup over the 50-step teacher while improving VBench score from 84.0 (Wan2.2) to 91.2. This surpasses both the teacher and closed-source systems including Veo3 (~90) and Sora2 (~88) on automated benchmarks, with competitive results in human preference studies. We release all model weights, training code, synthetic data pipelines, and evaluation scripts to advance open research in video generation.

</details>


### [147] [MeshFIM: Local Low-Poly Mesh Editing via Fill-in-the-Middle Autoregressive Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08744)
*Dingdong Yang,Jian Liu,Biwen Lei,Haohan Weng,Zhuo Chen,Song Guo,Hao Richard Zhang,Ali Mahdavi Amiri,Chunchao Guo*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: MeshFIM是一个面向低多边形网格的Fill-in-the-Middle框架，能够基于周围上下文条件仅重新生成网格中的不满意局部区域，而无需整体重新生成。通过五个关键设计（边界顶点标记、上下文位置编码、扩展上下文宽度、上下文增强和基于门控减法机制的低多边形几何编码器），解决了精确边界附着、拓扑顺序保持和区域溢出控制三大挑战。实验证明其在网格细化、修复和整体生成+拼接方案上优于多种基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归模型生成低多边形网格时采用"全有或全无"的方式：局部区域不满意就必须整体重新生成，浪费计算资源并破坏其他区域已满意的网格结构。MeshFIM旨在实现局部网格的精确再生成，避免全局重做。

Method: 提出Fill-in-the-Middle框架MeshFIM，包含五项设计：1）边界顶点标记确保精确附着；2）上下文位置编码保持拓扑顺序；3）扩展上下文宽度增强感知范围；4）上下文增强提升鲁棒性；5）低多边形几何编码器通过门控减法机制利用参考表面与现有网格的差异聚焦于缺失区域生成。

Result: 在网格细化、网格修复和整体生成+拼接方案上均优于多个基线方法。消融实验验证了每个组件的有效性。展示了交互式笔刷编辑和自动缺陷修复两个应用。

Conclusion: MeshFIM成功实现了低多边形网格的局部条件式重新生成，解决了边界附着、拓扑有序和区域溢出等网格特有挑战，为交互式编辑和自动修复提供了有效工具。

Abstract: Autoregressive (AR) models can generate high-quality low-poly meshes from point clouds, but they still operate in an all-or-nothing manner: when a local region is unsatisfactory, the entire mesh must be regenerated, wasting computation and destroying satisfactory mesh structure elsewhere. We introduce MeshFIM, a Fill-in-the-Middle (FIM) framework that regenerates a target region of a low-poly mesh conditioned on the surrounding context. MeshFIM addresses three mesh-specific challenges: enforcing exact attachment along the exposed boundary, preserving topological order in the context, and suppressing overflow beyond the intended region. It does so with five complementary design choices: boundary vertex markers, context positional embeddings, expanded context width, context augmentation, and a low-poly geometry encoder whose gated subtraction mechanism focuses generation on the missing region by leveraging the difference between the reference surface and the existing mesh. Detailed ablation studies are presented to show the effectiveness of every introduced component. Based on MeshFIM, we demonstrate two applications: interactive brush-based editing and automatic defect repair on low-poly mesh (see Figure 1). Last but not least, experiments show that MeshFIM outperforms a range of baselines in mesh refinement, mesh repair and whole mesh generation plus stitch-back scheme.

</details>


### [148] [HairGPT: Strand-as-Language Autoregressive Modeling for Realistic 3D Hairstyle Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08824)
*Haimin Luo,Min Ouyang,Lan Xu,Jingyi Yu*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: HairGPT提出了一种以发丝为中心的三维发型生成框架，通过双重解耦自回归序列建模，将发型生成从连续的扩散场转变为结构化、语义可控的编辑过程，支持在真实和风格化域中生成高质量发型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有三维发型生成方法依赖连续的扩散场，混淆了全局拓扑与局部纹理，缺乏对发型语义和结构组织的显式建模，难以支持语义可控的编辑和复杂发型合成。

Method: 采用发丝作为生成基元，提出双重解耦自回归序列建模：空间上按语义头皮区域解耦，结构上沿层次化发丝表示从全局布局到细粒度风格解耦；引入几何分词器和区域感知语义标注引导发丝级生成。

Result: 支持组合编辑、稀有复杂发型合成、风格化域适配，在真实和风格化域均实现高保真结果和强语义可控性。

Conclusion: 将发型生成从不透明纹理合成转变为结构化、语义可控的创作流程，与数字造型工作流对齐。

Abstract: Hair is a rich medium of visual and cultural expression, yet its digital modeling remains challenging due to the duality of fluidity and structure. Many existing generative approaches rely primarily on continuous diffusion fields, which entangle global topology with local texture and obscure the semantic and structural organization of hairstyles. To address this, we propose HairGPT, a strand-centric framework that treats strands as generative primitives and formulates realistic 3D hairstyle synthesis as a dual-decoupled autoregressive sequence modeling problem. Our method applies spatial decoupling across semantic scalp regions and structural decoupling along a hierarchical strand representation, progressing from global layout to fine-grained style. We further introduce a geometric tokenizer and region-aware semantic annotations to guide strand-level generation, enabling compositional editing, synthesis of rare and complex hairstyles, and adaptation to stylized domains. By aligning generative modeling with the workflow of digital grooming, HairGPT turns hair generation from opaque texture synthesis into a structured and semantically controllable authoring process, supporting robust semantic conditioning and high-fidelity results across realistic and stylized domains. Project Page: https://haiminluo.github.io/hairgpt/

</details>


### [149] [CAGS: Color-Adaptive Volumetric Video Streaming with Dynamic 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09279)
*Daheng Yin,Yili Jin,Jianxin Shi,Isaac Ding,Miao Zhang,Fangxin Wang,Zhaowu Huang,Cong Zhang,Jiangchuan Liu,Fang Dong*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 针对3D高斯泼溅在体积视频流传输中带宽消耗大、现有LoD方法不适用的问题，提出基于矢量量化的颜色自适应方案CAGS，通过低分辨率参考图像进行颜色校正，在波动带宽下PSNR提升5~20dB。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 体积视频流传输需要实时、逼真的远程3D场景呈现，但3D高斯泼溅面临高带宽消耗问题；现有基于密度的LoD方法不适用于高斯表示，导致视觉质量下降；属性压缩虽可减少带宽但引起颜色失真。

Method: 提出Color-Adaptive方案，使用矢量量化建立LoD；利用低分辨率参考图像校正颜色失真；构建CAGS系统，服务端渲染参考图像，客户端进行颜色恢复。

Result: 在波动带宽下PSNR比现有自适应流系统高5~20dB；速度显著快于现有可扩展高斯压缩方法；可泛化到不同高斯表示。

Conclusion: CAGS通过颜色自适应方案有效解决了3DGS在体积视频流中的带宽与质量问题，兼顾了实时性、视觉质量和带宽适应性。

Abstract: Volumetric video (VV) streaming enables real-time, immersive access to remote 3D environments, powering telepresence, ecological monitoring, and robotic teleoperation. These applications turn VV streaming into a real-time interface to remote physical environments, imposing new system-level demands for photorealistic scene representation, low-latency interaction, and robust performance under heterogeneous networks. 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has been widely used for real-time photorealistic rendering, offering superior visual quality and rendering performance, but it faces challenges due to bandwidth consumption. Furthermore, as the foundation of adaptive VV streaming, existing Levels of Detail (LoD) methods based on density are not well-suited to Gaussian representations, leading to visible gaps and severe quality degradation. Recent studies have also explored attribute compression techniques to reduce bandwidth consumption. Our preliminary studies reveal that aggressive attribute compression primarily causes color distortion, which can be effectively corrected in the rendered image using a reference image. Motivated by these findings, we propose a novel Color-Adaptive scheme for adaptive VV streaming that uses vector quantization (VQ) to establish LoDs and correct color distortions with low-resolution reference images. We further present CAGS, an adaptive VV streaming system compatible with diverse Gaussian representations, which integrates the Color-Adaptive scheme by rendering reference images on the streaming server and performing color restoration on the client. Extensive experiments on our prototype system demonstrate that CAGS outperforms the existing adaptive streaming systems in PSNR by 5$\sim$20 dB under fluctuating bandwidth, operates significantly faster than existing scalable Gaussian compression methods, and generalizes across different Gaussian representations.

</details>


### [150] [LagrangianSplats: Divergence-Free Transport of Gaussian Primitives for Fluid Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09299)
*Ningxiao Tao,Baoquan Chen,Mengyu Chu*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 提出了一种从稀疏2D视频重构3D流体速度场的框架，通过散度无核参数化保证不可压缩性，结合拉格朗日3D高斯泼溅表示实现长程传输一致性，并引入滑动窗口机制进行高效优化，在合成和真实数据集上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从稀疏2D视频观测中重构3D流体速度场是一个高度病态的反问题，现有方法通过软惩罚施加物理约束，往往导致精度不足和收敛问题。

Method: 1) 使用连续散度无核(DFK)表示参数化重构速度场，从结构上保证流体的不可压缩性；2) 速度场驱动拉格朗日3D高斯泼溅(3DGS)表示的对流，实现长程传输一致性；3) 提出滑动窗口(Sliding Window)方案，在合理时间范围内传播梯度，保持可管理的训练成本。

Result: 在合成和真实数据集上，该方法在传输一致性和物理精度方面均优于现有基线，实现了高质量的重模拟和流分析应用。

Conclusion: 通过结构性强约束（而非软惩罚）来保证流体物理定律和传输一致性，结合高效的滑动窗口优化，显著提升了3D流体速度场重构的质量和可靠性。

Abstract: Reconstructing 3D fluid velocity fields from sparse 2D video observations is a highly ill-posed inverse problem, demanding both transport consistency with observed motion and physical validity under fluid laws. Existing methods typically impose these constraints through soft penalties, often leading to compromised accuracy and convergence issues. We introduce a reconstruction framework that structurally enforces both constraints. Specifically, we parameterize the reconstructed velocity using a continuous Divergence-Free Kernel representation, driving the advection of a Lagrangian 3D Gaussian Splatting representation. This formulation intrinsically guarantees both flow incompressibility and long-range transport coherence by construction. To enable the efficient optimization of such a constrained system, we introduce a novel Sliding Window scheme that propagates gradients over meaningful temporal horizons while maintaining tractable training costs. Experiments on synthetic and real-world datasets demonstrate that our method outperforms state-of-the-art baselines in both transport consistency and physical accuracy, enabling applications such as high-quality re-simulation and flow analysis.

</details>


### [151] [FrameTwin: Curve-Anchored Gaussian Alignment from Sparse Views for Adaptive Wireframe 3D Printing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09362)
*Wenting Wang,Zhuo Huang,Kun Qian,Neelotpal Dutta,Yuhu Guo,Yingjun Tian,Yeung Yam,Charlie C. L. Wang*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: FrameTwin提出了一种基于曲线锚定高斯对齐的框架，利用稀疏视角图像闭环控制自适应线框3D打印，通过参数化曲线上的高斯核捕捉薄壁结构变形，并利用可微渲染生成神经变形场，实现数字孪生与打印轨迹自适应更新。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有线框3D打印过程中薄壁结构易发生变形，缺乏有效的闭环控制手段；传统高斯溅射方法在稀疏视角下对薄结构存在歧义性问题，难以精确捕捉拓扑变形。

Method: 将高斯核锚定到参数化曲线上，形成紧凑且几何感知的编码以显式捕捉支柱拓扑；通过可微渲染管线估计神经变形场，对齐部分打印的模型与制造中观测到的变形结构；利用变形场融合已变形打印几何与未打印几何，实现自适应更新打印轨迹。

Result: 能够鲁棒地捕捉并补偿机器人3D打印系统中线框模型的变形，在稀疏视角下有效减少歧义性，实现全局支柱一致性对齐。

Conclusion: 曲线锚定的高斯表示有效解决了稀疏视角下薄结构感知的歧义问题，闭环控制策略可显著提升线框3D打印的自适应性与打印质量。

Abstract: We present FrameTwin, a curve-anchored Gaussian alignment framework that uses sparse-view images to close the control loop for adaptive wireframe 3D printing. Our key idea is to capture the deformation of thin wireframe structures from sparse-view images using Gaussian kernels anchored to parametric curves, yielding a compact and geometry-aware encoding that explicitly captures strut topology. Driven by a differentiable rendering pipeline, FrameTwin estimates a neural deformation field that aligns the partially printed target model with the deformed structure observed during fabrication, where the optimized curve-Gaussian representation serves as a digital twin of the evolving wireframe. Unlike general Gaussian-splatting approaches, our formulation constrains kernel placement along parametric curves, substantially reducing the ambiguity inherent in sparse-view observations of thin structures. The resultant deformation-field alignment enforces global consistency across all struts. By using the estimated deformation field to blend the distorted printed geometry with the remaining unprinted geometry, FrameTwin enables adaptive updates to future printing trajectories. We demonstrate that FrameTwin can robustly capture and compensate for deformation in wireframe models fabricated using a robotized 3D printing system.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [152] [Where Reliability Lives in Vision-Language Models: A Mechanistic Study of Attention, Hidden States, and Causal Circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08200)
*Logan Mann,Ajit Saravanan,Ishan Dave,Shikhar Shiromani,Saadullah Ismail,Yi Xia,Emily Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出VLM可靠性探针（VRP）框架，系统检验了"注意力越尖锐，VLM越可信"这一普遍直觉。通过对LLaVA-1.5、PaliGemma、Qwen2-VL三个开源VLM家族（3-7B参数）的机制分析发现：注意力结构对正确性预测能力极弱（R_pb≈0.001），而隐藏状态几何、层间边界形成和稀疏后层电路才是更可靠的正确性预测指标。晚融合架构（LLaVA）将可靠性集中在脆弱的后层瓶颈，而早融合架构（PaliGemma、Qwen2-VL）将其广泛分布，具备更强鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型（VLM）的可靠性评估是一个关键问题。研究者注意到一个普遍直觉——当VLM的注意力图看起来"尖锐"（即注意力集中在查询区域）时，模型输出应当更可信、更校准。然而这一"注意力-置信度假设"从未被直接验证过。本文旨在严格检验该假设是否成立，并寻找更可靠的VLM正确性预测信号。

Method: 研究构建了统一的机制分析流水线——VLM可靠性探针（VLM Reliability Probe, VRP），对三种开源VLM家族（LLaVA-1.5、PaliGemma、Qwen2-VL，3-7B参数）进行系统比较。方法包括：1）注意力结构与正确性的相关性分析；2）隐藏状态线性探针预测正确性；3）自一致性分析（K=10）；4）因果性神经元消融实验，对比晚融合（LLaVA）与早融合（PaliGemma、Qwen2-VL）架构的鲁棒性差异。

Result: 三项核心结果：（i）注意力结构对正确性的预测能力接近于零（R_pb(C_k,y)=0.001, 95%CI[-0.034,0.036]），尽管注意力在特征提取中仍因果必要（top-30%补丁掩码导致准确率下降8.2-11.3pp, p<0.001）；（ii）正确性在计算后期变得可读：单个隐藏状态线性探针在POPE上AUROC>0.95（两个VLM家族），自一致性（K=10）是最强的行为预测信号（R_pb=0.43）；（iii）因果消融暴露架构差异：晚融合LLaVA的可靠性集中在脆弱的后层瓶颈（消融top-5探针神经元使物体识别准确率下降8.3pp），而早融合PaliGemma和Qwen2-VL广泛分布可靠性，消融~50%峰值层隐藏维度后性能下降≤1pp。

Conclusion: 结论明确但有限：在3-7B规模的VLM中，模型的可靠性更可靠地从隐藏状态几何、层间边界形成和稀疏后层电路中读出，而非从注意力图尖锐度中读出。这一发现对VLM的监控设计具有直接启示：注意力锐度不适合作为可信度指标，应关注隐藏状态空间的几何特征和后层稀疏电路。

Abstract: A pervasive intuition holds that vision-language models (VLMs) are most trustworthy when their attention maps look sharp: concentrated attention on the queried region should imply a confident, calibrated answer. We test this Attention-Confidence Assumption directly. We instrument three open-weight VLM families (LLaVA-1.5, PaliGemma, Qwen2-VL; 3-7B parameters) with a unified mechanistic pipeline -- the VLM Reliability Probe (VRP) -- that compares attention structure, generation dynamics, and hidden-state geometry against a single correctness label. Three results emerge. (i) Attention structure is a near-zero predictor of correctness (R_pb(C_k,y)=0.001, 95% CI [-0.034,0.036]; R_pb(H_s,y)=-0.012, [-0.047,0.024] on a pooled n=3,090 split), even though attention remains causally necessary for feature extraction (top-30% patch masking drops accuracy by 8.2-11.3 pp, p<0.001). (ii) Reliability becomes legible later in the computation: a single hidden-state linear probe reaches AUROC>0.95 on POPE for two of three families, and self-consistency at K=10 is the strongest behavioral predictor we measure at 10x inference cost (R_pb=0.43). (iii) Causal neuron-level ablations expose a sharp architectural split with direct monitor-design implications: late-fusion LLaVA concentrates reliability in a fragile late bottleneck (-8.3 pp object-identification accuracy after top-5 probe-neuron ablation), whereas early-fusion PaliGemma and Qwen2-VL distribute it widely and absorb destruction of ~50% of their peak-layer hidden dimension with <=1 pp degradation. The takeaway is narrow but consequential: in 3-7B VLMs, reliability is read more reliably off hidden-state geometry, layer-wise margin formation, and sparse late-layer circuits than off attention-map sharpness.

</details>


### [153] [Auto-Rubric as Reward: From Implicit Preferences to Explicit Multimodal Generative Criteria](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08354)
*Juanxi Tian,Fengyuan Liu,Jiaming Han,Yilei Jiang,Yongliang Wu,Yesheng Liu,Haodong Li,Furong Xu,Wanhua Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Auto-Rubric as Reward (ARR)框架，将隐式偏好知识外化为显式评分标准，并搭配Rubric Policy Optimization (RPO)策略优化，在文本到图像生成和图像编辑任务上超越成对奖励模型和VLM评判器，实现更可靠、数据高效的多模态对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RLHF方法将人类偏好的多维结构简化为标量或成对标签，易受奖励破解攻击；而Rubrics-as-Reward方法在生成可靠、可扩展且数据高效的显式标准方面仍存在挑战。

Method: ARR框架通过外化VLM的内隐偏好知识为提示特定的评分标准，将整体意图转化为可独立验证的质量维度；RPO则将ARR的结构化多维评估蒸馏为鲁棒的二元奖励，替代不透明的标量回归。

Result: ARR-RPO在文本到图像生成和图像编辑基准上优于成对奖励模型和VLM评判器，显著抑制了位置偏差等评估偏见，支持零样本部署和少样本微调。

Conclusion: 多模态对齐的瓶颈不在于偏好知识的缺失，而在于缺乏将隐式知识分解为结构化接口的机制；显式外化偏好知识为结构化评分标准能够实现更可靠、数据高效的对齐。

Abstract: Aligning multimodal generative models with human preferences demands reward signals that respect the compositional, multi-dimensional structure of human judgment. Prevailing RLHF approaches reduce this structure to scalar or pairwise labels, collapsing nuanced preferences into opaque parametric proxies and exposing vulnerabilities to reward hacking. While recent Rubrics-as-Reward (RaR) methods attempt to recover this structure through explicit criteria, generating rubrics that are simultaneously reliable, scalable, and data-efficient remains an open problem. We introduce Auto-Rubric as Reward (ARR), a framework that reframes reward modeling from implicit weight optimization to explicit, criteria-based decomposition. Before any pairwise comparison, ARR externalizes a VLM's internalized preference knowledge as prompt-specific rubrics, translating holistic intent into independently verifiable quality dimensions. This conversion of implicit preference structure into inspectable, interpretable constraints substantially suppresses evaluation biases including positional bias, enabling both zero-shot deployment and few-shot conditioning on minimal supervision. To extend these gains into generative training, we propose Rubric Policy Optimization (RPO), which distills ARR's structured multi-dimensional evaluation into a robust binary reward, replacing opaque scalar regression with rubric-conditioned preference decisions that stabilize policy gradients. On text-to-image generation and image editing benchmarks, ARR-RPO outperforms pairwise reward models and VLM judges, demonstrating that explicitly externalizing implicit preference knowledge into structured rubrics achieves more reliable, data-efficient multimodal alignment, revealing that the bottleneck is the absence of a factorized interface, not a deficit of knowledge.

</details>


### [154] [Embeddings for Preferences, Not Semantics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08360)
*Carter Blair,Ariel D. Procaccia,Milind Tambe*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于文本嵌入的集体决策方法，针对标准嵌入仅捕捉语义相似性而无法反映偏好相似性的问题，通过合成训练数据打破语义干扰与偏好信号之间的相关性，从而显著提升偏好预测效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在集体决策中，参与者以自由文本而非固定选项表达观点，希望利用设施选址和公平聚类等文献中的方法。然而标准文本嵌入衡量的是语义相似性，而决策需要的是偏好相似性——参与者与文本的距离应反比于其同意程度。现有嵌入虽通过语义与偏好的相关性继承了粗糙的偏好信号，但相关性一旦断裂便失效。

Method: 形式化定义了偏好相似性问题的不变性挑战：文本嵌入同时编码偏好相关信号（立场与价值观）和语义干扰（风格与措辞），两者在观测上存在相关性。设计合成训练数据打破这种相关性，使优化评分器从受干扰主导的余弦相似度转向偏好主导的度量。

Result: 在11个在线审议数据集上，该方法显著提升了偏好预测性能，验证了打破语义干扰与偏好信号相关性的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过合成数据解耦偏好信号与语义干扰，能够有效改进文本嵌入在集体决策中的偏好预测能力，为基于自由文本的群体决策提供了新的方法论支持。

Abstract: Modern AI is opening the door to collective decision-making in which participants express their views as free-form text rather than voting on a fixed set of candidates. A natural idea is to embed these opinions in a vector space so that the substantial literature on facility location problems and fair clustering can be brought to bear. But standard text embeddings measure semantic similarity, whereas distances in facility location problems and fair clustering require what we call \textit{preferential similarity}: a participant's agreement with a piece of text should be inversely related to their distance from it. Off-the-shelf embeddings inherit a coarse preference signal through a correlation between semantic and preferential similarity, but fail to capture preferences when the correlation breaks.
