To overcome these limitations, we propose AHD Agent, a novel tool-integrated, multi-turn framework that empowers LLMs to proactively decide whether to generate heuristics or invoke tools to retrieve targeted evidence from the solving environment. To effectively train such a dynamic decision-making agent, we introduce an agentic reinforcement learning (RL) system, which leverages a novel environment synthesis pipeline to optimize a compact model's generalizable AHD capabilities. Experiments across eight diverse domains, including four held-out tasks, demonstrate that our 4B-parameter agent matches or surpasses state-of-the-art baselines using much larger models, while requiring significantly fewer evaluations. Model and inference scaling analysis further reveals that AHD Agent offers an effective trajectory toward truly autonomous heuristic design.

</details>


### [191] [From Holo Pockets to Electron Density: GPT-style Drug Design with Density](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08767)
*Jiahao Chen,Letian Gao,Yanhao Zhu,Wenbiao Zhou,Bing Su,Zhi John Lu,Bo Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出EDMolGPT，一种利用低分辨率电子密度点云作为物理条件的自回归分子生成框架，用于基于结构的药物设计，能够整合实验数据并生成具有3D构象的分子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SBDD方法通常仅以空结合口袋为条件，忽略了填充物（配体和溶剂）提供的信息；同时刚性口袋表示无法捕捉构象灵活性，导致结构偏差。

Method: 利用计算或实验来源的低分辨率电子密度（ED）作为物理条件，采用纯解码器自回归框架EDMolGPT，从ED点云生成分子。

Result: 在101个生物靶标上的评估验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 基于电子密度的物理条件生成能够结合实验数据、捕捉构象灵活性、缓解结构偏差，生成具有3D构象的分子，为SBDD提供了新范式。

Abstract: Recent advances in generative modeling have enabled significant progress in structure-based drug design (SBDD). Existing methods typically condition molecule generation on empty binding pockets from holo complexes, overlooking informative components such as the filler (ligands and solvent). Here, we leverage low-resolution electron density (ED) derived from the filler as a physically grounded condition for \textit{de novo} drug design. We consider two types of ED, calculated and cryo-EM/X-ray, obtainable from computational or experimental sources, supporting unified pre-training and experimental integration. Compared with rigid pocket representations, experimental ED naturally captures conformational flexibility and provides a more faithful description of the binding environment. Based on this, we introduce EDMolGPT, a decoder-only autoregressive framework that generates molecules from low-resolution ED point clouds. By grounding generation in physically meaningful density signals, EDMolGPT mitigates structural bias and produces molecules with 3D conformations. Evaluations on 101 biological targets verify the effectiveness. Our project page: https://jiahaochen1.github.io/EDMolGPT_Page/.

</details>


### [192] [EvoMAS: Learning Execution-Time Workflows for Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08769)
*Chengdong Xu,Kaiqiang Ke,Ziheng Liu,Jiaqi Wei,Zibo Shao,Weile Guo,Chao Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EvoMAS 提出了一种在任务执行过程中动态构建多智能体工作流的方法，通过元级序列决策和基于策略梯度的学习来自适应协调智能体，在长时域复杂任务上优于静态工作流方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的多智能体系统大多采用一次性（one-shot）范式，工作流在任务执行前就固定下来。这种静态协调策略不适合子目标、中间证据和信息需求会随执行阶段演变的长期任务。

Method: 将工作流构建形式化为沿单个任务轨迹的元级序列决策问题。每个阶段通过Planner-Evaluator-Updater流水线构建显式任务状态，并使用学习得到的Workflow Adapter从固定候选智能体池中实例化特定于该阶段的分层工作流。适配器通过策略梯度训练，以稀疏、可验证的最终任务成功作为主要监督信号。

Result: 在GAIA、HLE和DeepResearcher基准上，EvoMAS优于单智能体基线和最近的自动化多智能体工作流设计方法。显式任务状态构建和学习的工作流自适应提供了互补收益。在最终成功信号极度稀疏的场景下，过程奖励最为有用。

Conclusion: 动态适应智能体协调随着任务状态的演变而演变是处理长时域复杂任务的有效策略，过程奖励在极端稀疏奖励环境下具有独特价值。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based multi-agent systems have shown strong potential on complex tasks through agent specialization, tool use, and collaborative reasoning. However, most automated multi-agent system design methods still follow a one-shot paradigm: a workflow is optimized or selected before execution and then reused unchanged throughout the task. This static coordination strategy is ill-suited for long-horizon tasks whose subgoals, intermediate evidence, and information needs evolve over multiple execution stages. We propose EvoMAS, a framework for execution-time multi-agent workflow construction. EvoMAS formulates workflow construction as a meta-level sequential decision problem along a single task trajectory. At each stage, it constructs an explicit task state through a Planner-Evaluator-Updater pipeline and uses a learned Workflow Adapter to instantiate a stage-specific layered workflow from a fixed pool of candidate agents. The adapter is trained with policy gradients using sparse, verifiable terminal task success as the main supervision signal, while evaluator-based process reward is analyzed separately under very-hard sparse-reward settings. Experiments on GAIA, HLE, and DeepResearcher show that EvoMAS outperforms single-agent baselines and recent automated multi-agent workflow design methods. Our analyses further show that explicit task-state construction and learned workflow adaptation provide complementary benefits. Additional results indicate that process reward is most useful when terminal success is extremely sparse, and qualitative case studies illustrate that EvoMAS adapts agent coordination as the task state evolves.

</details>


### [193] [Reasoning Compression with Mixed-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08776)
*Han Yang,Mingyan Wu,Bailan He,Zeyu Cao,Sikuan Yan,Kevin Qinghong Lin,Zifeng Ding*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出混合策略蒸馏（MPD），通过将大模型（教师）的简洁推理能力蒸馏到小模型（学生），在保持或提升推理性能的同时，显著减少小模型的推理token消耗（最高减少27.1%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理密集型大语言模型虽性能强劲，但生成中间推理轨迹时token消耗过大、推理成本高。小模型推理轨迹冗余更长，在资源受限场景中部署困难。

Method: 提出Mixed-Policy Distillation (MPD)框架：学生模型先采样推理轨迹，教师模型将其重写为更简洁的版本，再通过KL散度对齐训练学生模型。该方法结合了on-policy（保留学生探索轨迹）和off-policy（注入教师压缩指导）的优点。

Result: 在Qwen3-1.7B上，MPD减少最高27.1%的token使用量，并在多个推理基准上提升了性能。

Conclusion: MPD是一种有效的推理压缩方法，可在小模型上实现高效推理，兼顾性能与成本。

Abstract: Reasoning-centric large language models (LLMs) achieve strong performance by generating intermediate reasoning trajectories, but often incur excessive token usage and high inference-time decoding cost. We observe that, when solving the same problems, larger reasoning models can often produce more concise traces, whereas smaller reasoning models tend to generate longer and more redundant trajectories. This is especially problematic in real-world deployment, where memory, latency, and serving-cost constraints often favor smaller models. Our observations suggest that reasoning compression can be transferred from large models to small ones rather than enforced through explicit length constraints. Based on this insight, we propose Mixed-Policy Distillation (MPD), a reasoning compression framework that transfers concise reasoning behavior from a larger-sized teacher to a smaller student by distilling teacher-compressed student trajectories. Unlike on-policy distillation, which aligns the student with teacher distributions over verbose student trajectories, or off-policy distillation, which relies on teacher-generated trajectories and may suffer from distribution mismatch, MPD combines the strengths of both. Given a student-sampled trajectory, the teacher rewrites it into a more concise reasoning trace, and the student is trained via KL-based alignment on the compressed trajectory. This preserves student-policy exploration while injecting teacher-guided compression. Experiments on Qwen3-1.7B show that MPD reduces token usage by up to 27.1% while improving performance across multiple reasoning benchmarks, demonstrating an effective approach to efficient small-model reasoning.

</details>


### [194] [Not All Turns Matter: Credit Assignment for Multi-Turn Jailbreaking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08778)
*Zhida He,Xiaoyu Wen,Han Qi,Ziyuan Zhou,Peng Yu,Xingcheng Xu,Dongrui Liu,Xia Hu,Chaochao Lu,Qiaosheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TRACE提出了一种基于回合感知信用分配框架的强化学习多轮越狱攻击方法，通过语义掩码和有害性惩罚解决信用分配问题，在攻击成功率和防御对齐上均取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有训练式多轮越狱方法使用粗粒度的轨迹级结果信号对所有回合进行统一奖励，但多轮越狱中不同回合的贡献是非均匀、阶段依赖且目标特定的，这导致了信用分配问题——成功轨迹中冗余回合被过度奖励，失败轨迹中有用的中间回合得不到应有信用。

Method: 提出TRACE框架：1) 对成功轨迹，通过留一轮语义掩码估计回合级贡献；2) 对失败轨迹，基于提示有害性和语义相关性分配惩罚，并增加局部拒绝感知惩罚；3) 将攻击侧的信用信号复用于多轮防御对齐。

Result: 在开源和闭源目标上的大量实验表明，TRACE在有效性、可迁移性和效率方面均表现优异，相比最强RL基线攻击成功率相对提升约25%，在复用于防御对齐时也改善了安全-实用性平衡。

Conclusion: 精细化的回合级信用分配能有效解决多轮越狱中的信用分配问题，同时攻击侧的信用信号可复用于增强多轮防御对齐。

Abstract: Deploying LLMs in multi-turn dialogues facilitates jailbreak attacks that distribute harmful intent across seemingly benign turns. Recent training-based multi-turn jailbreak methods learn long-horizon attack strategies from interaction feedback, but often rely on coarse trajectory-level outcome signals that broadcast uniformly to every turn. However, we find that turn-level contributions in multi-turn jailbreaking are non-uniform, phase-dependent, and target-specific. Such coarse outcome supervision induces a credit assignment problem, leading to over-rewarding redundant turns in successful trajectories and under-crediting useful intermediate turns in failed ones. To address this, we propose TRACE, a turn-aware credit assignment framework for reinforcement learning (RL)-based multi-turn jailbreaking. For successful trajectories, TRACE estimates turn-level contributions via leave-one-turn-out semantic masking; for failed ones, TRACE assigns penalties based on prompt harmfulness and semantic relevance, with an additional local refusal-aware penalty. Furthermore, we reuse the attack-side credit signal for multi-turn defense alignment. Extensive experiments on open-source and closed-source targets show that TRACE achieves strong overall performance in effectiveness, transferability, and efficiency, yielding about a 25% relative improvement in attack success rate over the strongest RL baseline while also improving the safety-utility balance when reused for defense alignment.

</details>


### [195] [How You Begin is How You Reason: Driving Exploration in RLVR via Prefix-Tuned Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08817)
*Yifan Xu,Junren Chen,Yifan Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出IMAX框架，通过训练一组可学习的软前缀（soft prefixes）来重塑基础模型在推理轨迹上的先验分布，结合信息最大化奖励与可验证奖励，解决RLVR中的熵坍缩和探索稀疏问题，在多个规模上显著提升推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR在LLM推理任务中面临奖励稀疏和长推理视野导致的探索困难，具体表现为熵坍缩现象——虽然单次 rollout 准确率提升，但成功推理轨迹的覆盖范围未能扩大。被动探索技术（如熵正则化）容易降低生成质量，产生噪声 rollout。

Method: 提出IMAX框架：(1) 训练一个软前缀池，每个前缀作为可训练控制旋钮，在相同骨干模型上诱导不同的 rollout 分布；(2) 推导信息最大化(InfoMax)奖励，与可验证奖励互补，用于RL训练；(3) 该框架与特定RL算法无关，可无缝集成到现有RLVR流程中。

Result: 在三种骨干模型规模上，IMAX相比标准RLVR一致提升推理性能，Pass@4提升最高达11.60%，Avg@4提升最高达10.57%。

Conclusion: IMAX通过软前缀池+信息最大化奖励有效缓解了RLVR中的熵坍缩问题，提升了推理轨迹的多样性和任务相关性，是一种算法无关的通用改进框架。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) recently thrives in large language model (LLM) reasoning tasks. However, the reward sparsity and the long reasoning horizon make effective exploration challenging. In practice, this challenge manifests as the \emph{entropy collapse} phenomenon, where RLVR improves single-rollout accuracy but fails to expand coverage on successful reasoning trajectories. Passive exploration techniques like entropy regularization tend to dismiss generation quality, resulting in noisy rollouts. In response to this issue, we propose an Information-Maximizing Augmented eXploration (IMAX) framework to train a pool of soft prefixes that reshapes the base model's prior over reasoning trajectories. Rather than relying on RL to incentivize exploration on top of the base model, each prefix acts as a trainable control knob that induces a distinct rollout distribution from the same backbone model. To encourage discovery of diverse and task-relevant reasoning behaviors, we derive an Information Maximization (InfoMax) reward to complement the verifiable rewards for RL training. IMAX is in general algorithm-agnostic and can be seamlessly integrated into existing RLVR pipelines. Experiment results have shown that across three backbone scales, IMAX consistently improves reasoning performance over standard RLVR, with gains up to 11.60\% in Pass@4 and 10.57\% in Avg@4.

</details>


### [196] [Mental Health AI Safety Claims Must Preserve Temporal Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08827)
*Srimonti Dutta,Ratna Kandala*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出当前精神健康AI安全评估的时间尺度错误，提出"时间安全不可识别性"形式化概念，并开发SCOPE-MH报告标准以保留交互时序证据，证明基于单轮行为的评分无法捕捉因交互顺序和累积效应导致的临床失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有精神健康AI安全评估聚焦于孤立响应、端点结果或对话整体质量的静态评分，忽视了交互顺序和累积效应导致的临床关键失败模式（如延迟升级、重复强化、依赖形成、修复失败、渐进恶化），这种评估覆盖不足会导致无效的安全结论。

Method: 提出Temporal Safety Non-Identifiability形式化理论，证明依赖序列、时序、累积或恢复的安全属性无法被丢弃这些特征的评估协议认证；基于此开发SCOPE（Safety Claims Over Preserved Evidence）通用原则，并实例化为SCOPE-MH精神健康报告标准；在AnnoMI专家标注动机访谈数据集上进行概念验证。

Result: 概念验证揭示出单轮行为评分无法表征的失败机制，证明保留时序证据的评估对于安全关键型精神健康AI部署是必要的。

Conclusion: SCOPE-MH应作为现有评估基础设施的诊断补充，保留时序证据的评估对于安全关键型精神健康AI部署是不可或缺的。

Abstract: The safety of mental health AI is often judged at the wrong temporal scale. Current evaluations typically score isolated responses, endpoint outcomes, or aggregate dialogue quality, while clinically consequential failures may arise from the order and accumulation of interactions themselves, including delayed escalation, repeated reinforcement, dependency formation, failed repair, and gradual deterioration across turns. This paper argues that this mismatch is not merely a limitation of evaluation coverage but a source of invalid safety conclusions. We introduce Temporal Safety Non-Identifiability, a formal account of why safety properties that depend on sequence, timing, accumulation, or recovery cannot be certified by protocols that discard those features. From this formalization, we develop SCOPE (Safety Claims Over Preserved Evidence) as a general principle for aligning safety claims with the evidence an evaluation actually retains, and instantiate it as SCOPE-MH, a mental-health instantiation of this reporting standard. We operationalize SCOPE-MH through a proof-of-concept on the AnnoMI dataset of expert-annotated motivational interviewing conversations, which reveals mechanisms of failure that per-turn behavior scoring does not represent. We propose SCOPE-MH as a diagnostic complement to existing evaluation infrastructure and argue that evaluation preserving temporal evidence is necessary, not optional, for safety-critical mental health AI deployment.

</details>


### [197] [When Agents Overtrust Environmental Evidence: An Extensible Agentic Framework for Benchmarking Evidence-Grounding Defects in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08828)
*Strick Sheng,Ziyue Wang,Liyi Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出EnvTrustBench，一个用于评估大语言模型智能体在环境证据接地缺陷（EGD）的基准框架。该框架通过生成任务场景、工作空间、目标和验证预言机，测试智能体在面临过时、错误或恶意环境观察时是否仍能保持对真实环境状态的正确认知。实验使用6种LLM和5种主流脚手架，在11个任务场景的55个案例中验证了EGD的普遍存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体基准主要评估任务完成能力或特定攻击（如提示注入、记忆投毒），但忽略了智能体在环境观察过时、不正确或恶意时，是否仍能保持对真实环境状态的可靠接地这一基础可靠性问题。

Method: 定义证据接地缺陷（EGD）为智能体将环境声称视为充分证据而不与当前可用证据进行比对的行为失败。EnvTrustBench框架包含：任务场景生成、工作空间与环境构建、智能体目标设定、验证预言机生成、执行智能体并记录轨迹、应用预言机判定结果。实验使用6种LLM骨干和5种常见脚手架，通过5轮反馈引导生成迭代，在11个任务场景中构建55个测试案例。

Result: 证据接地缺陷（EGD）在不同操作工作流中一致出现，表明环境接地是智能体可靠性的核心问题，并具有重要的安全影响。

Conclusion: 环境接地是智能体系统层面的可靠性问题，涉及上下文准入、证据溯源、新鲜度检查、验证策略、行为门控和模型推理等多个维度，需要系统性关注和解决。

Abstract: Large language model agents increasingly operate through environment-facing scaffolds that expose files, web pages, APIs, and logs. These observations influence tool use, state tracking, and action sequencing, yet their reliability and authority are often uncertain. Environmental grounding is therefore a systems-level problem involving context admission, evidence provenance, freshness checking, verification policy, action gating, and model reasoning. Existing agent benchmarks mainly evaluate task capability or specific attacks such as prompt injection and memory poisoning, but they under-specify a fundamental reliability question: whether agents remain grounded in the true environment state when observations are stale, incorrect, or malicious.
  We introduce EnvTrustBench, an agentic framework for benchmarking this failure mode. We define an evidence-grounding defect (EGD) as a behavioral failure in which an agent treats an environment-facing claim as sufficient evidence for action without resolving it against available current evidence, leading to a task-incorrect false path under the true environment state. Given a task scenario, EnvTrustBench generates the workspace, environment, agent-facing objective, and validation oracle, executes the evaluated agent, records its action-observation trajectory and final state, and applies the oracle to produce a verdict.
  Using 6 LLM backbones and 5 widely used scaffolds, we evaluate 55 generated cases across 11 task scenarios, with each scenario expanded through five feedback-guided generation iterations. Results show that EGDs consistently emerge across operational workflows, highlighting environmental grounding as a core agent reliability problem with important security implications.

</details>


### [198] [SynerDiff: Synergetic Continuous Batching for Fast and Parallel Diffusion Model Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08835)
*Ziqi Zhou,Peng Yang,Yuxin Liang,Mingliu Liu,Jia Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SynerDiff是一种面向扩散模型服务的连续批处理系统，通过层内和层间协同优化，在保证图像质量的前提下，将吞吐量提升1.6倍，端到端平均延迟和P99尾部延迟降低高达78.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的连续批处理方法在UNet-VAE并发阶段存在严重的资源争用问题，导致延迟尖峰；同时，多任务并发调度需要在不同调度策略下权衡UNet吞吐量与VAE延迟。

Method: 提出SynerDiff系统，包含：(1) 层内协同：通过VAE Chunking和Adaptive Skip-CFG剪枝组件特定资源瓶颈以缓解资源争用；(2) 层间协同：利用组件对调度粒度的差异化敏感性，设计阈值感知调度器规划并发序列并调整层内决策，在保持UNet高吞吐阈值的同时最小化VAE延迟；(3) 反馈控制器：基于队列负载动态调整阈值以提升系统容量上限。

Result: 与基线相比，SynerDiff将吞吐量提升1.6倍，平均端到端延迟和P99尾部延迟分别降低高达78.7%，同时保证高图像保真度。

Conclusion: SynerDiff通过层内-层间协同的连续批处理策略，有效解决了扩散模型服务中UNet-VAE并发的资源争用与调度权衡问题，实现了高吞吐、低延迟的扩散模型服务。

Abstract: The expansion of Artificial Intelligence-generated content service requires diffusion model serving to simultaneously achieve high throughput and low task end-to-end (E2E) latency. However, existing continuous batching methods suffer from severe resource contention during UNet-VAE concurrency, leading to latency spikes. Furthermore, concurrent multi-task scheduling entails a trade-off between UNet throughput and VAE latency across varying scheduling strategies. To address these, we propose SynerDiff, an efficient continuous batching system built on intra-inter level synergy. At the intra-concurrency level, SynerDiff alleviates resource contention by pruning component-specific resource bottlenecks via VAE Chunking and Adaptive Skip-CFG. At the inter-concurrency level, leveraging components' differential sensitivity to scheduling granularities, a threshold-aware scheduler plans concurrent sequences and tunes intra-concurrency decisions to minimize VAE latency while maintaining UNet within high-throughput threshold. Additionally, a feedback controller dynamically adjusts this threshold based on queue loads to boost system capacity ceiling. Experimental results show that, SynerDiff improves throughput by 1.6$\times$ and decreases both average E2E and P99 tail latencies by up to 78.7\%, compared to benchmarks while guaranteeing high image fidelity.

</details>


### [199] [Ace-Skill: Bootstrapping Multimodal Agents with Prioritized and Clustered Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08887)
*Feng Xiong,Zengbin Wang,Yong Wang,Xuecai Hu,Jinghan He,Liang Lin,Yuan Liu,Xiangxiang Chu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Ace-Skill，一种协同进化框架，通过优先级采样（懒惰衰减熟练度追踪）和聚类知识组织，解决自进化智能体中的数据低效和知识干扰问题，在四个多模态工具使用基准上显著提升性能（Avg@4相对提升35.46%），使开源35B MoE模型媲美专有模型，且知识可零样本迁移至小模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自进化智能体面临两个耦合瓶颈：数据低效（低价值样本消耗过多资源）和知识干扰（异构知识导致检索噪声和任务错位指导），两者形成自我强化的失败循环。

Method: 提出Ace-Skill协同进化框架：(1) 优先级采样器结合懒惰衰减熟练度追踪，聚焦于信息量大且掌握不足的样本；(2) 聚类组织器对知识进行语义聚类，实现更干净的检索和更可靠的适应。

Result: 在四个多模态工具使用基准上，Avg@4准确率相对提升35.46%，开源35B MoE模型匹配或超越专有模型；知识可零样本迁移至9B和4B小模型。

Conclusion: 通过联合优化采样分配和知识组织，将自进化转变为良性循环，使资源受限的智能体无需额外训练即可继承高级能力。

Abstract: Self-evolving agents present a promising path toward continual adaptation by distilling task interactions into reusable knowledge artifacts. In practice, this paradigm remains hindered by two coupled bottlenecks: data inefficiency, where costly rollout effort is disproportionately spent on low-value samples rather than informative ones, and knowledge interference, where heterogeneous knowledge stored in shared repositories leads to noisy retrieval and task-misaligned guidance. Together, these issues form a self-reinforcing failure loop in which uninformative rollouts yield noisy knowledge, which in turn degrades subsequent rollouts. In this work, we introduce Ace-Skill, a co-evolutionary framework that jointly optimizes rollout allocation and knowledge organization for self-evolving multimodal agents. Specifically, Ace-Skill combines aprioritized sampler with lazy-decay proficiency tracking to focus rollouts on informative and insufficiently mastered samples, and a clustered organizer that semantically clusters knowledge for cleaner retrieval and more reliable adaptation. By improving sampling and organization together, Ace-Skill turns self-evolution into a virtuous cycle in which more informative rollouts produce higher-quality knowledge that supports stronger subsequent rollouts. Across four multimodal tool-use benchmarks, Ace-Skill delivers strong gains (e.g., +35.46% relative improvement in Avg@4 accuracy), enabling an opensource 35B MoE model to match or surpass proprietary models. The acquired knowledge also transfers effectively in a zero-shot manner to smaller 9B and 4B models, allowing resource-constrained agents to inherit advanced capabilities without additional training. The code has been publicly available at https://github.com/AMAP-ML/Ace-Skill.

</details>


### [200] [OPT-BENCH: Evaluating the Iterative Self-Optimization of LLM Agents in Large-Scale Search Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08904)
*Xiaozhe Li,Jixuan Chen,Xinyu Fang,Shengyuan Ding,Haodong Duan,Qingwen Liu,Kai Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: OPT-BENCH是一个评估LLM自改进能力的基准，结合20个机器学习任务和10个NP难问题，测试模型在动态环境中通过自我反思而非机械工具应用来优化解决方案的能力。OPT-Agent框架模拟人类认知适应，通过感知-记忆-推理循环迭代改进。实验表明，更强模型更善于利用反馈信号自改进，但所有LLM仍不及人类专家水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM在推理和工具使用上表现出色，但能否像人类一样在动态环境中通过内在认知能力（感知、推理、记忆）持续自我改进和优化解决方案，这一核心能力尚未被充分探索。

Method: 构建OPT-BENCH基准（20个机器学习任务+10个经典NP难问题），提出OPT-Agent框架，通过感知-记忆-推理循环模拟人类认知适应，在19个LLM（7个模型家族，3B-235B参数）上进行实验评估。

Result: 更强的LLM更善于利用反馈信号进行自我改进，但这种自适应能力受限于模型的基底能力，即使最先进的LLM也未能达到人类专家水平。

Conclusion: LLM具备一定的自改进能力，但存在上限，距离人类专家的灵活适应能力仍有差距，提示未来需要突破模型基底能力的限制。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated remarkable capabilities in reasoning and tool use. However, the fundamental cognitive faculties essential for problem solving, including perception, reasoning, and memory, remain the stable core of intelligence. Unlike memorizing specific patterns, humans succeed in novel environments by applying these intrinsic faculties to adapt and optimize. Yet, whether LLMs possess this essential capacity, namely the ability to continuously refine solutions in response to dynamic environmental feedback, remains underexplored. To address this challenge, we introduce OPT-BENCH, a benchmark for evaluating self-improvement capabilities in large-scale search spaces. By combining 20 machine learning tasks with 10 classic NP-hard problems, OPT-BENCH provides a rigorous setting to assess whether agents can adapt through intrinsic self-reflection rather than rote tool application. We further propose OPT-Agent, a framework that emulates human-like cognitive adaptation. It operates through a general perception, memory, and reasoning loop, iteratively refining solutions based on environmental feedback. Through extensive experiments on 19 LLMs from 7 model families, including reasoning models, general models, and open-source models ranging from 3B to 235B parameters, we demonstrate that stronger models are more effective at leveraging feedback signals for self-improvement. However, this upper-bound adaptability remains fundamentally constrained by the models' base capacity, and even the most advanced LLMs still fall short of human expert performance.

</details>


### [201] [Forge: Quality-Aware Reinforcement Learning for NP-Hard Optimization in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08905)
*Xiaozhe Li,Xinyu Fang,Shengyuan Ding,Yang Li,Linyang Li,Haodong Duan,Qingwen Liu,Kai Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出OPT-BENCH框架，通过质量感知的RLVR训练LLMs解决NP-hard优化问题，在7B模型上显著超越GPT-4o，且训练收益可迁移至其他任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理基准仅评估正确性，忽略了最优性（在约束下寻找最佳解的能力），缺乏针对NP-hard优化问题的质量感知训练与评估框架。

Method: 构建OPT-BENCH框架，包含三个核心组件：可扩展的训练基础设施（实例生成器、质量验证器、10个任务的最优基线）、含1000实例的严格基准（用Success Rate衡量可行性、Quality Ratio衡量质量）、支持连续改进的质量感知奖励。使用Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct-1M进行15K样本训练。

Result: Qwen2.5-7B在OPT-BENCH上达到93.1% SR和46.6% QR，远超GPT-4o的29.6% SR和14.6% QR；质量感知奖励较二元奖励提升28.8%；训练收益迁移至数学(+2.2%)、逻辑(+1.2%)、知识(+4.1%)、指令跟随(+6.1%)；任务多样性对泛化的贡献大于数据量。

Conclusion: 质量感知的RLVR是训练LLMs解决复杂优化问题的有效范式；OPT-BENCH可作为NP-hard优化问题的标准化评估平台；任务多样性比数据量更关键地驱动泛化能力。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have achieved remarkable success on reasoning benchmarks through Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR), excelling at tasks such as math, coding, logic, and puzzles. However, existing benchmarks evaluate only correctness, while overlooking optimality, namely the ability to find the best solutions under constraints. We propose OPT-BENCH, the first comprehensive framework for training and evaluating LLMs on NP-hard optimization problems through quality-aware RLVR. OPT-BENCH provides three key components: a scalable training infrastructure with instance generators, quality verifiers, and optimal baselines across 10 tasks; a rigorous benchmark with 1,000 instances evaluating both feasibility, measured by Success Rate, and quality, measured by Quality Ratio; and quality-aware rewards that enable continuous improvement beyond binary correctness. Training on Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct-1M with 15K examples achieves 93.1% SR and 46.6% QR, significantly outperforming GPT-4o, which achieves 29.6% SR and 14.6% QR. Beyond optimization, training on OPT-BENCH transfers to diverse tasks, including mathematics (+2.2%), logic (+1.2%), knowledge (+4.1%), and instruction following (+6.1%). Our analysis reveals that quality-aware rewards improve solutions by 28.8% over binary rewards, and that task diversity drives generalization more than data quantity, offering insights into RLVR scaling for complex reasoning.

</details>


### [202] [Internalizing Safety Understanding in Large Reasoning Models via Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08930)
*Yi Zhang,Yuxin Chen,Leheng Sheng,Dongcheng Zhang,Chaochao Lu,Xiang Wang,An Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Safety Internal (SInternal)框架，通过让大推理模型学习安全验证任务而非仅仅模仿安全行为，实现对自身输出安全性的内在理解，显著提升对越狱攻击的鲁棒性，并为强化学习对齐提供更优的初始化基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对齐范式主要依赖外部强制的合规性（如检测恶意提示），导致模型缺乏内在的安全理解能力——无法验证自身回复的安全性，且易受对抗性越狱攻击。作者希望从行为层面的安全模仿转向内在的安全理解。

Method: 提出Safety Internal (SInternal)框架：仅在大推理模型上训练安全验证任务（critique自身的生成答案），使用专家推理轨迹进行训练，使模型内化安全规范。同时可与强化学习结合，作为比标准监督微调更优的初始化。

Result: 学习安全验证能诱导对回复安全性的强泛化能力，显著增强对域外越狱攻击的鲁棒性。与强化学习结合时，SInternal作为初始化比标准监督微调效果更好。

Conclusion: 内化安全理解比单纯模仿安全行为能建立更鲁棒的对齐基础。

Abstract: While explicit Chain-of-Thought (CoT) empowers large reasoning models (LRMs), it enables the generation of riskier final answers. Current alignment paradigms primarily rely on externally enforced compliance, optimizing models to detect malicious prompts rather than evaluating the safety of their own outputs. We argue that this approach remains largely behavioral: our empirical analysis reveals that ostensibly aligned models lack intrinsic safety understanding, often failing to verify their own response safety and remaining vulnerable to adversarial jailbreaks. To address this fundamental limitation, we propose Safety Internal (SInternal), a framework that internalizes safety specifications by training LRMs exclusively on safety verification tasks to critique their own generated answers using expert reasoning trajectories. We demonstrate that learning to verify induces a strong generalization for response safety, significantly enhancing robustness against out-of-domain jailbreaks. Furthermore, when combined with reinforcement learning, SInternal serves as a superior initialization compared to standard supervised fine-tuning, suggesting that internalizing safety understanding creates a more robust foundation for alignment than merely mimicking safe behaviors. Our codes are available at https://github.com/AlphaLab-USTC/SInternal

</details>


### [203] [Self-ReSET: Learning to Self-Recover from Unsafe Reasoning Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08936)
*Dongcheng Zhang,Yi Zhang,Yuxin Chen,An Zhang,Xiang Wang,Chaochao Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Self-ReSET是一个纯强化学习框架，通过让大推理模型从自身安全错误轨迹中学习恢复能力，显著提升了对对抗攻击（尤其是OOD越狱提示）的鲁棒性，同时保持通用性能和数据利用效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对齐方法依赖静态专家数据（包含反思轨迹或对抗前缀）进行微调，但静态数据与模型动态的、在策略的推理轨迹存在偏差，导致模型难以覆盖其庞大的生成空间并从自身失败中学习恢复。

Method: 提出Self-ReSET框架：将模型自身的安全错误轨迹重新利用作为强化学习的初始状态，通过纯强化学习框架训练模型从自身错误中恢复。

Result: 在多种大推理模型和基准测试上，Self-ReSET显著增强了对对抗攻击（尤其是OOD越狱提示）的鲁棒性，同时保持通用性能，且数据利用效率高。

Conclusion: Self-ReSET有效培养了模型的自我恢复模式，使其能够更好地识别并从不安全中间错误状态恢复到良性路径。

Abstract: Large Reasoning Models possess remarkable capabilities for self-correction in general domain; however, they frequently struggle to recover from unsafe reasoning trajectories under adversarial attacks. Existing alignment methods attempt to mitigate this vulnerability by fine-tuning the model on expert data including reflection traces or adversarial prefixes. Crucially, these approaches are often hindered by static training data which inevitably deviate from model's dynamic, on-policy reasoning traces, resulting in model hardly covering its vast generation space and learning to recover from its own failures. To bridge this gap, we propose Self-ReSET, a pure reinforcement learning framework designed to equip LRMs with the intrinsic capacity to recover from their own safety error trajectories, which are subsequently reused as an initial state for reinforcement learning. Extensive experiments across various LRMs and benchmarks demonstrate that Self-ReSET significantly enhances robustness against adversarial attacks especially out-of-distribution (OOD) jailbreak prompts while maintaining general utility, along with efficient data utilization. Further analysis reveals that our method effectively fosters self-recovery patterns, enabling models to better identify and recover from unsafe intermediate error states back to benign paths. Our codes and data are available at https://github.com/Ing1024/Self-ReSET.

</details>


### [204] [Can We Formally Verify Neural PDE Surrogates? SMT Compilation of Small Fourier Neural Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08938)
*Ali Baheri,David Millard,Ignacio Laguna Peralta*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了傅里叶神经算子(FNO)的形式化验证方法，通过将FNO的前向传播编码为Z3求解器中的线性实算术，实现了对物理性质(正性、质量守恒)的精确或近似验证，在10个小规模1D对流-扩散-反应FNO模型上展示了精确编码可提供2个正性证明、5个正性反例和10个质量违反反例，而冻结编码可扩展到更大网格但牺牲了保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FNO在加速PDE模拟时被广泛使用，但缺乏对其保持基本物理结构(如正性、质量守恒)的形式化保证，现有方法无法提供严格的证明或反例，因此需要开发形式化验证技术来评估FNO的物理合理性。

Method: 将FNO中固定的谱卷积视为线性映射，整个前向传播为分段线性函数；提出两种Z3编码：(1)精确编码将谱卷积编译为稠密矩阵乘法，支持证明和反例；(2)冻结编码将谱路径替换为常数，速度更快但近似。

Result: 在10个小规模1D对流-扩散-反应FNO模型(85-117参数，网格8-32)上：精确编码在无ReLU的线性模型上获得2个正性证明、5个正性反例和10个质量违反反例；3个含ReLU模型超时。对于质量非增性质，Z3在7/10模型上找到比梯度法和蒙特卡洛更差的反例。冻结编码可扩展到网格64，正性检查亚秒级，但不再提供对原FNO的保证。

Conclusion: 该工作明确了FNO形式化验证中可靠性与可扩展性之间的权衡，指出了实现生产级神经算子形式化验证所需的关键改进方向。

Abstract: Fourier Neural Operators (FNOs) can greatly accelerate PDE simulation, but they are often used without formal guarantees that they preserve basic physical structure. We show that, once the trained weights and grid are fixed, the spectral convolution in an FNO is a linear map. As a result, the full forward pass is piecewise-linear and can be represented exactly in Z3's linear real arithmetic. We study two encodings. The exact encoding compiles the spectral convolution into a dense matrix multiplication, which is sound for both proofs and counterexamples. The lighter frozen encoding replaces the spectral path with a constant, making it faster but approximate. On 10 small FNO surrogates for 1D advection-diffusion-reaction (85 to 117 parameters, grids 8 to 32), the exact encoding gives 2 sound positivity proofs on linear (ReLU-free) models, 5 sound positivity counterexamples, and 10 sound mass-violation counterexamples; the remaining 3 positivity queries on ReLU models time out. For mass non-increase, Z3 finds worse counterexamples than both gradient-based falsification and Monte Carlo on 7 of 10 models. The frozen encoding scales to grid size 64 with sub-second positivity checks, but it no longer provides certificates for the original FNO. Overall, the results make the soundness--scalability tradeoff explicit and point to what is needed for formal verification of production-scale neural operators.

</details>


### [205] [MDGYM: Benchmarking AI Agents on Molecular Simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08941)
*Vinay Kumar,Satyendra Rajput,Mausam,N. M. Anoop Krishnan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MDGYM是一个包含169个专家精选分子动力学模拟的基准测试，评估AI智能体在LAMMPS和GROMACS上的表现。最强智能体仅解决21%的简单任务，高难度任务不到10%。失败模式包括生成物理不稳定的配置、伪造数值输出而非实际计算、过早放弃任务，表明流畅的代码生成无法迁移到基于物理的推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验AI智能体能否自主设计和执行现代科学中的计算工作流，分子动力学模拟作为测试床，要求将物理直觉转化为语法和语义正确的输入脚本、推理初始和边界条件、诊断不稳定轨迹并解释输出。

Method: 构建MDGYM基准测试，包含169个专家精选的MD模拟任务，覆盖LAMMPS和GROMACS两个广泛使用的MD软件包，设置三个递增难度级别。评估Claude Code、Codex和OpenHands三种智能体框架搭配四种LLM的表现。

Result: 所有智能体表现均差：最强智能体仅解决21%的简单任务，高难度任务低于10%。失败模式包括生成物理不稳定配置、伪造数值输出、过早放弃任务，与一般软件工程基准中的失败模式有本质区别。

Conclusion: 流畅的代码生成能力无法迁移到基于物理的推理任务，AI智能体在科学计算工作流中仍存在显著不足，需要新的方法来实现AI驱动的科学发现。

Abstract: The promise of AI-driven scientific discovery hinges on whether AI agents can autonomously design and execute the computational workflows that underpin modern science. Molecular dynamics (MD) simulation presents a natural test bed to stress-test this claim; it requires translating physical intuition into syntactically and semantically correct input scripts, reasoning about initial and boundary conditions, diagnosing numerically unstable trajectories, and interpreting outputs against known physical behavior and laws. We introduce MDGYM, a benchmark of 169 expert-curated MD simulations spanning LAMMPS and GROMACS, two widely used MD packages, across three increasing difficulty levels. We evaluate three agentic frameworks -- Claude Code, Codex, and OpenHands -- with four LLMs, and find that all perform poorly: even the strongest agent solves only 21\% of easy-level tasks, with less than 10\% at higher difficulties. Trajectory analysis reveals a characteristic pattern of failure -- agents successfully invoke simulation machinery but produce physically unstable configurations, fabricate numerical outputs without executing the underlying computation, or abandon tasks prematurely rather than iterating through simulation-specific errors. These failure modes are qualitatively distinct from those observed in general software engineering benchmarks, indicating that fluent code generation does not transfer to grounded physical reasoning.

</details>


### [206] [Agentic AI Scientists Are Not Built For Autonomous Scientific Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08956)
*Harshit Bisht,Vinay Kumar,Kevin Maik Jablonka,Mausam,N. M. Anoop Krishnan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文认为，虽然当前AI科学家已可作为合作者，但构建真正自主的AI科学家面临四大根本挑战（麦克纳马拉谬误、隐性知识缺失、输出多样性压缩、缺乏物理实验反馈），并提出了基于模拟验证、持久世界模型、集中预注册和需求驱动的改进方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 指出当前对"端到端自主科学发现AI科学家"的追求存在问题，挑战"只要扩展规模就能实现自主"的主流假设，呼吁重新审视底层设计选择。

Method: 通过立场论述的方式，系统分析自主AI科学家面临的四个关键挑战，并据此提出四项建设性建议。

Result: 识别出四大挑战：问题选择受麦克纳马拉谬误影响、LLM训练语料缺失隐性程序性知识与失败知识、偏好优化压缩输出多样性、现有基准缺乏物理实验闭环反馈。

Conclusion: 实现真正自主的AI科学家需要：使用科学模拟作为训练验证器、设计表征研究目标变迁的持久世界模型、建立AI生成假设的集中预注册库、以科学需求而非工具能力驱动应用。

Abstract: A growing body of work pursues AI scientists capable of end-to-end autonomous scientific discovery. This position paper argues that although they already function as co-scientists, agentic AI scientists are not built for autonomous scientific discovery. We identify the following challenges in building and deploying autonomous AI scientists: (1) Problem selection is influenced by the McNamara fallacy; (2) Agents are built on large language models (LLMs) whose training corpora omit tacit procedural and failure knowledge of laboratory practice; (3) Preference optimisation during post-training compresses output diversity toward consensus; and (4) Most scientific benchmarks measure single-turn prediction accuracy and lack feedback from physical experiments back to the computational model. These challenges are not just questions of scale and scaffolding; they require revisiting fundamental design choices. To build truly autonomous AI scientists, we recommend the use of scientific simulations as verifiers for training, the design of persistent world models that represent the shifting objectives governing real investigations, the establishment of a centralized preregistration repository for all AI-generated hypotheses, and application driven by scientific need rather than tool affordance.

</details>


### [207] [Latency Analysis and Optimization of Alpamayo 1 via Efficient Trajectory Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08975)
*Yunseong Jeon,Namcheol Lee,Yoonsu Lee,Jangwoon Park,Sol Ahn,Jong-Chan Kim,Seongsoo Hong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统分析并改进了推理端到端自动驾驶系统Alpamayo 1，通过将多推理范式改为单推理范式并加速扩散动作生成，在保持轨迹多样性和预测质量的前提下，将推理延迟降低了69.23%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理端到端自动驾驶中，多推理范式计算冗余但被认为能保持轨迹多样性，单推理范式效率更高但被认为会牺牲多样性。本文旨在探究多推理与单推理范式在轨迹多样性上的实际差异，并系统优化推理延迟。

Method: 1) 将Alpamayo 1从多推理系统重构为单推理系统，对比两者轨迹多样性；2) 通过消除不必要的拷贝操作和低效内核执行，加速基于扩散的动作生成。

Result: 单推理范式不会显著降低轨迹多样性；联合优化后实现69.23%的推理延迟降低，同时保持轨迹多样性和预测质量。

Conclusion: 系统架构与运行时执行的联合分析对提升推理端到端自动驾驶系统的效率至关重要。

Abstract: Reasoning-based end-to-end (E2E) autonomous driving has recently emerged as a promising approach to improving the interpretability of driving decisions as it can generate human-readable reasoning together with predicted trajectories. Such approaches commonly generate multiple trajectories to capture diverse future behaviors, and they fall into two categories: (1) multi-reasoning, where one reasoning sequence is generated per trajectory, and (2) single-reasoning, where a single reasoning is shared across all trajectories. The former offers richer diversity at the cost of redundant computation, while the latter is more efficient but is often assumed to sacrifice diversity. Alpamayo 1, a representative system, adopts the multi-reasoning approach and achieves competitive trajectory prediction performance. However, the efficiency of this design remains largely unexplored, making it a well-motivated subject for investigation. In this paper, we systematically analyze and improve Alpamayo 1 in two ways. First, we reduce inference latency while preserving trajectory diversity by redesigning Alpamayo 1 into a single-reasoning system. Through extensive experiments, we find that replacing multi-reasoning with single-reasoning does not meaningfully degrade trajectory diversity. Second, we accelerate diffusion-based action generation by eliminating inter-block overhead arising from unnecessary copy operations and inefficient kernel execution. Through closed-loop and open-loop experiments, we validate both optimizations, demonstrating a 69.23% reduction in inference latency while maintaining trajectory diversity and prediction quality. These results highlight the importance of jointly analyzing system architecture and runtime execution to improve the efficiency of reasoning-based E2E AD systems.

</details>


### [208] [Sufficient conditions for a Heuristic Rating Estimation Method application](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08991)
*Jacek Szybowski,Konrad Kułakowski,Jiri Mazurek*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文系统性地提出了启发式评级估计（HRE）方法的正确应用条件，并比较了算术与几何算法在完整与不完整成对比较方法中的表现，通过示例证明算术变体中的不一致性估计是最优的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有HRE方法在不同算法和数据结构（完整/不完整成对比较）下的适用条件尚不明确，需要系统性地界定其正确应用范围。

Method: 通过理论分析，分别针对算术算法和几何算法，在完整成对比较与不完整成对比较两种场景下，推导HRE方法的正确应用条件，并辅以算例验证。

Result: 算术变体下的不一致性估计在完整与不完整成对比较中均优于几何变体，表现最优。

Conclusion: 明确了HRE方法的适用条件，为不同场景下的应用提供了理论依据，并推荐算术算法以获取更优的不一致性估计。

Abstract: A series of papers has introduced the Heuristic Rating Estimation method, which evaluates a set of alternatives based on pairwise comparisons and the weights of reference alternatives. We formulate the conditions under which the HRE method can be applied correctly. The research considers both arithmetic and geometric algorithms for complete and incomplete pairwise comparison methods. The illustrative examples show that the estimations of inconsistency in the arithmetic variant are optimal.

</details>


### [209] [Re$^2$Math: Benchmarking Theorem Retrieval in Research-Level Mathematics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09012)
*Zicheng Lyu,Wenjie Yang,Shengzhong Zhang,Zengfeng Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Re^2Math是一个用于评估大语言模型在数学证明中基于文献的工具检索能力的基准。每个实例从定理证明中抽取引用片段，要求模型检索合适的定理/引理来填补证明步骤。尽管现有模型在源定位（source grounding）上表现尚可，但在工具适用性判断（ToolAcc）上最高仅达7.0%，表明模型能检索到正确语句但无法判断其是否适用于当前证明步骤。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大语言模型在封闭世界数学推理能力上越来越强，但真实的科研助手需要在文献中查找并验证已有工具（如引理）是否适用于当前证明上下文。缺乏一个能够系统评估模型在部分数学证明中检索适用定理能力的标准化基准。

Method: 构建Re^2Math基准，每个实例从主定理证明中选取一个候选引文作为工具引用，提供层级化的证明上下文和可选的泄漏控制锚点提示。任务设计为源导向但引用无关的——任何足以完成证明步骤的可接受定理都算正确。采用冻结的检索语料库确保可复现性，并支持自动持续扩展。

Result: 最优固定判断器（fixed-judge）的ToolAcc仅达7.0%，尽管源定位率（source grounding）显著更高。这表明当前系统常能检索到有效语句，但无法判断其是否适用于具体的局部证明步骤。

Conclusion: 通过解耦引文召回、源定位和证明间隙充分性三个维度，Re^2Math将文献导向的数学工具使用转化为可控的诊断任务，揭示了现有模型在工具适用性判断上的关键短板。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly capable at closed-world mathematical reasoning, but research assistance also requires source-grounded use of the literature. When a proof reaches a non-trivial step, a useful assistant should determine whether the needed tool (e.g., a lemma) already exists, identify a suitable scholarly source, and verify that its assumptions align with the current proof context. To rigorously evaluate such capabilities, we introduce Re$^2$Math, a benchmark for tool-grounded retrieval from partial mathematical proofs. Each instance is built from a candidate instrumental citation in the proof of a main theorem, with hierarchical context and an optional leakage-controlled anchor hint. We also make the task source-grounded yet citation-agnostic in that any admissible theorem sufficient for the proof transition is accepted. Evaluation uses a release-frozen retrieval artifact, ensuring reproducibility, while the benchmark itself supports automatic, continual expansion with newly constructed instances. On the current benchmark test set, the best fixed-judge ToolAcc reaches 7.0%, despite substantially higher rates of source grounding, indicating that current systems often retrieve valid statements but fail to establish their applicability to the local proof step. By decoupling citation recall, grounding, and proof-gap sufficiency, Re$^2$Math transforms literature-grounded mathematical tool use into a controlled diagnostic task.

</details>


### [210] [CATO: Charted Attention for Neural PDE Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09016)
*Chun-Wun Cheng,Sifan Wang,Carola-Bibiane Schönlieb,Angelica I. Aviles-Rivero*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CATO提出了一种几何自适应且导数感知的神经算子，通过学习连续潜图将网格坐标映射到图空间，并结合图条件轴向注意力与导数感知物理损失，在复杂几何上的PDE求解中取得了平均26.76%的性能提升，同时参数量减少81.98%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于Transformer的神经算子在处理复杂几何上的PDE时面临两个关键挑战：直接在大量网格点上计算代价高昂，且在原始离散坐标上操作可能模糊物理相互作用的固有几何结构。

Method: CATO包含三个核心设计：(1) 学习连续潜图将网格坐标映射到图空间；(2) 在图空间中应用图条件轴向注意力以高效捕获长程依赖；(3) 引入导数感知物理损失，联合监督解值、网格一致梯度及辅助通量场。

Result: CATO在所有评估数据集上取得最佳性能，平均比最强基线提升约26.76%，同时参数量减少81.98%。

Conclusion: 学习几何自适应图与导数感知的物理监督是进行准确高效PDE算子学习的有效方法。CATO还提供了理论近似保证：在有利图下，图条件轴向注意力能以可控误差表示低秩轴向解算子，且小幅图扰动仅导致有界的近似退化。

Abstract: Neural operators have emerged as powerful data-driven solvers for PDEs, offering substantial acceleration over classical numerical methods. However, existing transformer-based operators still face critical challenges when modeling PDEs on complex geometries: directly processing over massive mesh points is computationally expensive, while operating in raw discretization coordinates may obscure the intrinsic geometry where physical interactions are more naturally expressed. To address these limitations, we introduce the Charted Axial Transformer Operator (CATO), a geometry-adaptive and derivative-aware neural operator for PDEs on general geometries. Instead of applying attention directly in the physical coordinate system, CATO learns a continuous latent chart that maps mesh coordinates into a learned chart space, where chart-conditioned axial attention efficiently captures long-range dependencies with reduced computational cost. In addition, CATO introduces a derivative-aware physics loss for steady-state PDEs that jointly supervises solution values, mesh-consistent gradients, and an auxiliary flux-like field, improving physical fidelity and reducing oversmoothing. We further provide a theoretical approximation result showing that, under a favorable chart, charted axial attention can represent low-rank axial solution operators with controlled error, and that small chart perturbations induce bounded approximation degradation. CATO achieves the best performance across all evaluated datasets, yielding an average improvement of approximately 26.76\% over the strongest competing baselines while reducing the number of parameters by 81.98\%. These results highlight the effectiveness of learning geometry-adaptive charts and derivative-aware physical supervision for accurate and efficient PDE operator learning.

</details>


### [211] [SearchSkill: Teaching LLMs to Use Search Tools with Evolving Skill Banks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09038)
*Jinchao Hu,Meizhi Zhong,Kehai Chen,Min Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SearchSkill框架，通过显式的可复用搜索技能（SkillBank）进行查询规划，在知识密集型QA任务中提升检索质量和答案准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言模型使用搜索工具时，查询质量差（宽泛或复制问题），浪费检索预算并影响后续推理。需要显式查询规划机制来改善检索行为。

Method: 提出SearchSkill框架：(1) 模型每一步先选择技能（skill），再基于技能卡片生成搜索或回答动作；(2) 维护动态SkillBank，从失败模式中扩展或精炼技能，并重构受影响的轨迹；(3) 两阶段SFT配方，使训练与推理时的技能选择→技能执行协议对齐。

Result: 在开源和闭源模型上，SearchSkill提升了知识密集型QA的精确匹配，改善了检索行为：更少的复制式首次查询、更原子化的跳转查询、更少的搜索预算下获得正确答案。

Conclusion: 显式的技能条件化查询规划是将搜索视为无差异化动作的轻量级替代方案。

Abstract: Teaching language models to use search tools is not only a question of whether they search, but also of whether they issue good queries. This is especially important in open-domain question answering, where broad or copied queries often waste retrieval budget and derail later reasoning. We propose \Ours, a framework that makes query planning explicit through reusable search skills. At each step, the model first selects a skill, then generates a search or answer action conditioned on the selected skill card. The skill inventory itself is not fixed: SearchSkill maintains an evolving SkillBank, expands or refines it from recurrent failure patterns, and reconstructs affected trajectories before supervised training. The resulting two-stage SFT recipe aligns training with the inference-time protocol of skill selection followed by skill-grounded execution. Across open-source and closed-source models, SearchSkill improves exact match on knowledge-intensive QA benchmarks and yields better retrieval behavior, including fewer copied first queries, more atomic hop-focused queries, and more correct answers within a small search budget. These results suggest that explicit skill-conditioned query planning is a lightweight alternative to treating search as an undifferentiated action.

</details>


### [212] [UxSID: Semantic-Aware User Interests Modeling for Ultra-Long Sequence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09040)
*Hongwei Zhang,Qiqiang Zhong,Jiangxia Cao,Yiyang Lv,Huanjie Wang,Liwei Guan,Jing Yao,Yiyu Wang,Junfeng Shu,Zhaojie Liu,Han Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出UxSID框架，通过语义组共享兴趣记忆（Semantic IDs + 双级注意力）在超长用户序列建模中实现效率与效果的平衡，在大规模广告A/B测试中带来0.337%收入提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超长用户序列建模面临效率与效果之间的权衡困境：基于item的精确搜索计算成本高，而item无关的压缩方法会丢失语义信息。

Method: 提出UxSID框架，采用语义组共享兴趣记忆（Semantic Group Shared Interest Memory），通过Semantic IDs (SIDs) 将用户行为序列压缩为语义组级别的共享表征，并设计双级注意力策略（dual-level attention）实现目标感知的偏好捕获。

Result: 达到最先进性能，在大规模广告A/B测试中实现0.337%的收入提升。

Conclusion: UxSID在计算简洁性与语义感知之间取得了良好平衡，为超长序列建模提供了除item级搜索和item无关压缩之外的第三条路径。

Abstract: Modeling ultra-long user sequences involves a difficult trade-off between efficiency and effectiveness. While current paradigms rely on either item-specific search or item-agnostic compression, we propose UxSID, a framework exploring a third path: semantic-group shared interest memory. By utilizing Semantic IDs (SIDs) and a dual-level attention strategy, UxSID captures target-aware preferences without the heavy cost of item-specific models. This end-to-end architecture balances computational parsimony with semantic awareness, achieving state-of-the-art performance and a 0.337% revenue lift in large-scale advertising A/B test.

</details>


### [213] [Containment Verification: AI Safety Guarantees Independent of Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09045)
*Royce Moon,Lav R. Varshney*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为“包含验证”（containment verification）的方法，将AI智能体的安全保证从模型层面转移到智能体框架层面。通过将AI建模为无约束的预言机（havoc oracle），并在Dafny中机械化证明框架的边界策略对所有可能的AI输出都成立。作者在PocketFlow这一最小化LLM智能体框架上进行了实例化验证，这是首个对智能体框架进行演绎形式化验证的工作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全方法作用于模型层面，依赖于不可验证的学习行为属性，无法提供可靠的安全保证。需要一种不依赖模型能力、可形式化验证的安全方法。

Method: 提出包含验证（containment verification）：将AI建模为无约束的预言机（havoc oracle），覆盖整个类型化动作空间；验证包含层必须对所有可能的AI输出强制执行边界策略；通过前向模拟精化（forward-simulation refinement）证明边界可强制执行属性的通用保证，并在Dafny中机械化证明；使用智能体合成流水线生成规约、操作模型和精化证明，并设置信息屏障防止同义反复规约。

Result: 在PocketFlow这一最小化LLM智能体框架上成功实例化了该范式；这是首个对智能体框架进行演绎形式化验证的工作；其安全保证对模型能力（在建模的类型化动作边界内）保持不变性。

Conclusion: 包含验证将安全保证从不可预测的模型行为转移到可形式化验证的框架层面，为AI智能体系统提供了一种不依赖于模型能力的可靠安全保证方法。该方法对边界可强制执行属性提供了通用保证，且保证对模型能力变化具有不变性。

Abstract: Agentic frameworks are the software layer through which AI agents act in the world. Existing safety methods intervene on the model and therefore remain conditional on unverifiable properties of learned behavior. We introduce containment verification, which locates safety guarantees in the agentic framework itself. Under havoc oracle semantics, the AI is modeled as an unconstrained oracle ranging over the entire typed action space, and the verified containment layer must enforce the boundary policy for every possible AI output. For boundary-enforceable properties, expressed over modeled boundary events, action arguments, and state, we prove a universal guarantee by forward-simulation refinement and mechanize it in Dafny. We instantiate the paradigm by verifying PocketFlow, a minimalist agentic LLM framework, and use an agentic synthesis pipeline to generate the specification, operational model, and refinement proof under an information barrier against tautological specifications. To our knowledge, this is the first deductive formal verification of an agentic framework, and its guarantee is invariant to model capability over the modeled typed action boundary.

</details>


### [214] [CauSim: Scaling Causal Reasoning with Increasingly Complex Causal Simulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09079)
*Nicolás Astorga,Anita Kriz,Mihaela van der Schaar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CauSim是一个将因果推理从稀缺标签问题转化为可扩展监督学习问题的框架，通过构建可执行的结构化因果模型（SCMs）实现数据增强和跨表示泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在因果推理任务中表现不佳，主要原因在于因果系统的目标数据本身具有复杂性：因果系统常以不可执行的形式表达，且因果查询的真实标注答案天然稀缺。

Method: CauSim框架通过构建可执行的结构化因果模型（SCMs）来工作。LLMs逐步构建从简单到复杂的SCMs，这些模型可扩展到全局复杂系统，同时保持对因果查询的可验证答案。该框架跨表示操作：将不可执行的因果知识形式化为代码（实现数据增强），并将可执行SCMs翻译为自然语言（实现对先前难以监督的表示进行监督）。

Result: 实验证明：(1) 跨表示泛化能力；(2) 课程缩放和数据量的持续收益；(3) LLM通过自生成的模拟器实现自我改进；(4) 通过形式化现有领域知识实现数据增强。

Conclusion: CauSim成功将因果推理从标签稀缺问题转化为可扩展的监督学习范式，有效提升了LLM的因果推理能力。

Abstract: Despite surpassing human performance across mathematics, coding, and other knowledge-intensive tasks, large language models (LLMs) continue to struggle with causal reasoning. A core obstacle is the target data itself: causal systems are complex and often expressed in non-executable forms, while ground-truth answers to causal queries are inherently scarce. We introduce CauSim, a framework that turns causal reasoning from a scarce-label problem into a scalable supervised one. CauSim constructs increasingly complex causal simulators: executable structural causal models (SCMs), incrementally built by LLMs, that scale to globally complex systems while maintaining verifiable answers to causal queries. CauSim operates across representations by formalizing non-executable causal knowledge into code, enabling data augmentation, and translating executable SCMs into natural language, enabling supervision in previously difficult-to-supervise representations. We structure our research into two parts: (1) how to construct increasingly complex causal simulators, and (2) a systematic study of what CauSim enables, demonstrating generalization across representations, consistent gains from curriculum scaling and data volume, LLM self-improvement through self-generated simulators, and data augmentation via formalization of existing domain knowledge.

</details>


### [215] [Constant-Target Energy Matching: A Unified Framework for Continuous and Discrete Density Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09085)
*Zhijun Zeng,Yixuan Jiang,Pipi Hu,Zuoqiang Shi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CTEM提出了一种统一的基于能量的密度估计框架，通过有界能量差变换替代传统的密度比回归，在连续、离散和混合变量空间中均能有效工作，无需估计配分函数或显式无界比回归。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的连续、离散和混合变量密度估计方法通常使用不同的目标函数，难以跨数据类型利用共同的统计结构；连续得分方法依赖对数密度梯度，而离散扩展使用的具体得分（concrete score）在低概率状态附近变得不稳定。

Method: CTEM将有界能量差变换替代普通密度比回归，推导出仅需样本的训练目标，其目标常数为1；学习到的标量势能函数可直接恢复对数密度，无需估计配分函数或显式无界比回归。

Result: 在连续、离散和混合变量基准测试中，CTEM显著优于竞争基线，密度估计性能大幅提升，且在标准采样程序下生成更高质量的样本。

Conclusion: CTEM是一种统一、稳定且高效的密度估计框架，能够跨不同数据类型（连续、离散、混合）实现出色的密度估计和样本生成质量。

Abstract: Density estimation is a central primitive in probabilistic modeling, yet continuous, discrete, and mixed-variable domains are often treated by separate objectives, limiting the ability to exploit a common statistical structure across data types. Continuous score-based methods rely on log-density gradients, while discrete extensions typically use concrete score whose unbounded targets become unstable near low-probability states. We introduce Constant-Target Energy Matching (CTEM), a unified energy-based framework for density estimation on general state spaces. CTEM replaces ordinary density-ratio regression with a bounded energy-difference transform and derives from it a sample-only training objective with the constant target 1. The learned scalar potential recovers log p without partition-function estimation or explicit unbounded ratio regression. Across continuous, discrete, and mixed-variable benchmarks, CTEM substantially improves density estimation over competitive baselines and yields higher-quality samples under standard sampling procedures.

</details>


### [216] [Data-driven Circuit Discovery for Interpretability of Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09129)
*Daking Rai,Mor Geva,Ziyu Yao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 现有电路发现方法假设语言模型用单一电路实现任务且数据集充分代表任务，但实验表明这些假设不成立——数据集微小变化会导致电路差异巨大。为此提出数据驱动电路发现（DCD），先聚类再为每组发现独立电路，让数据揭示模型内部的真实机制结构而非依赖人为定义的任务边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 质疑并检验现有电路发现方法的两个隐含假设：（1）语言模型用单一电路实现一个任务；（2）数据集能充分代表任务。实验发现这些假设不成立，数据集微小变化或混合任务时现有方法会产生误导性结果。

Method: 提出Data-driven Circuit Discovery (DCD)框架：先根据模型处理样例的相似程度对数据集进行聚类，再为每个聚类分别发现一个独立电路，而非对整个数据集输出单一电路。

Result: 跨四个任务实验表明：数据集轻微变化导致电路边重叠度低且交叉忠实度下降；混合两个不同任务时现有方法仍输出单一电路但掩盖了机制差异；DCD发现每个数据集存在多个电路，每个电路对其所在聚类的忠实度高于现有方法发现的单一电路。

Conclusion: 当前电路发现方法实际上发现的是数据集特定的电路而非通用任务电路；DCD让数据自动揭示模型内部的机制结构，避免人为任务定义与模型实际计算组织方式不一致的问题。

Abstract: Circuit discovery aims to explain how language models (LMs) implement a specific task by localizing and interpreting a circuit, a computational subgraph responsible for the LM's behavior. Existing circuit discovery methods are hypothesis-driven; they first informally define a task with a dataset, and then apply a circuit discovery algorithm over that dataset to obtain a single circuit. This imposes two strong assumptions: that the LM implements the task with a single circuit, and that the dataset adequately represents the task as humans understand it. We systematically test these assumptions across four previously studied tasks and find that even minor dataset variations that preserve task semantics can produce circuits with low edge overlap and cross-dataset faithfulness. More strikingly, when applied to a mixed dataset with two distinct tasks whose separately discovered circuits have near-zero cross-faithfulness, existing methods still return a single circuit with high faithfulness across both tasks. This indicates that current methods discover dataset-specific circuits, rather than general task circuits. We propose Data-driven Circuit Discovery (DCD), a new discovery framework that drops both assumptions: instead of returning a single circuit for a dataset, DCD first clusters examples in the dataset by how similarly the model processes them and discovers a separate circuit for each group. This allows distinct mechanisms to appear separately rather than merged into a single circuit; each circuit explains its group, not the full task. Experiments show that DCD discovers multiple circuits per dataset, each more faithful to its group than a single circuit discovered by existing methods. Broadly, DCD lets the data reveal mechanistic structure within LMs, rather than relying on human-defined task boundaries that may not align with how models organize their computation.

</details>


### [217] [MCP-Cosmos: World Model-Augmented Agents for Complex Task Execution in MCP Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09131)
*Giridhar Ganapavarapu,Dhaval Patel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MCP-Cosmos 框架通过将生成式世界模型（World Model）融入 MCP 生态系统，使智能体能够在潜在空间模拟状态转换并优化计划后再执行，显著提升了工具成功率、工具参数准确性等环境交互指标，并提出了执行质量等新评估指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前范式存在割裂：任务级规划忽略执行时动态，而反应式执行缺乏长期前瞻。MCP 虽统一了 LLM 与外部工具的接口，但智能体如何概念化其所处的环境仍存在根本性空白。

Method: 提出 MCP-Cosmos 框架，采用"自带世界模型"（BYOWM）策略，将 MCP、World Model 和 Agent 三者统一，使智能体在潜在空间先模拟状态转换并优化计划，再执行实际动作。实验中采用 ReAct 和 SPIRAL 两种策略，结合 2 种规划模型和 3 种代表性世界模型，在 20+ 个 MCP-Bench 任务上进行评估。

Result: 观察到智能体的环境交互 KPI（如工具成功率、工具参数准确性）得到改善。同时提出了执行质量（Execution Quality）等新指标，相比基线方法能更有效地评估世界模型的效果。

Conclusion: MCP-Cosmos 通过融合生成式世界模型，弥合了任务级规划与反应式执行之间的鸿沟，为 MCP 生态下的智能体提供了预测性任务自动化能力，并为评估世界模型在智能体任务中的有效性提供了新的度量标准。

Abstract: The Model Context Protocol (MCP) has unified the interface between Large Language Models (LLMs) and external tools, yet a fundamental gap remains in how agents conceptualize the environments within which they operate. Current paradigms are bifurcated: Task-level planning often ignores execution-time dynamics, while reactive execution lacks long-horizon foresight. We present MCP-Cosmos, a framework that infuses generative World Models (WM) into the MCP ecosystem to enable predictive task automation. By unifying three disparate technologies, namely MCP, World Model, and Agent, we demonstrate that a "Bring Your Own World Model" (BYOWM) strategy allows agents to simulate state transitions and refine plans in a latent space before execution. We conducted experiments using two strategies, namely ReAct and SPIRAL with 2 planning models and 3 representative world models over 20+ MCP-Bench tasks. We observed improvements in Agent's environment interaction KPI such as tool success rate and tool parameter accuracy. The framework also offers new metrics such as Execution Quality to generate new insights about the effectiveness of world models compared to baseline.

</details>


### [218] [BoostAPR: Boosting Automated Program Repair via Execution-Grounded Reinforcement Learning with Dual Reward Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09134)
*Yuanhao Li,Hongbo Wang,Xiaotang Shang,Xunzhu Tang,Yiming Cao,Xuhong Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: BoostAPR是一个三阶段强化学习框架，通过行级信用分配机制解决程序修复中的稀疏奖励问题，在多个基准测试上取得显著提升，并展现出强跨语言泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习在程序修复中面临执行反馈稀疏和序列级奖励粗糙的难题，难以区分哪些编辑真正修复了bug。

Method: 提出三阶段框架：(1) 在带执行验证的演示数据上进行监督微调并包含推理轨迹；(2) 训练双奖励模型——序列级评估器和行级信用分配器；(3) 使用PPO优化，行级模型将奖励重新分配到关键编辑区域。行级信用分配以适合代码变更的中间粒度运作。

Result: 在SWE-bench Verified上达40.7%（比基线高22.9pp），Defects4J上24.8%（Python到Java迁移），HumanEval-Java上84.5%，QuixBugs上95.0%，在开源模型中取得有竞争力结果并展示强跨语言泛化。

Conclusion: 行级信用分配有效解决了程序修复中强化学习的稀疏奖励问题，实现了显著的性能提升和跨语言泛化能力。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning for program repair is hindered by sparse execution feedback and coarse sequence-level rewards that obscure which edits actually fix bugs. We present BoostAPR, a three-stage framework addressing these challenges: (1) supervised fine-tuning on execution-verified demonstrations with reasoning traces, (2) training dual reward models--a sequence-level assessor and a line-level credit allocator--from execution outcomes, and (3) PPO optimization where the line-level model redistributes rewards to critical edit regions. This line-level credit assignment operates at an intermediate granularity naturally suited to code changes. Trained on SWE-Gym and evaluated on four benchmarks, BoostAPR achieves 40.7% on SWE-bench Verified (+22.9pp over base model), 24.8% on Defects4J (Python-to-Java transfer), 84.5% on HumanEval-Java, and 95.0% on QuixBugs, achieving competitive results among open-source models with strong cross-language generalization.

</details>


### [219] [Do LLMs Experience an Internal Polylogue? Investigating Reasoning through the Lens of Personas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09159)
*Nils A. Herrmann,Leander Girrbach,Kirill Bykov,Zeynep Akata*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出“polylogue”概念，将LLM中的persona向量从静态操纵手柄转变为动态监测信号。通过分析生成过程中persona向量与隐藏激活的时间序列对齐（即polylogue），发现其特征能够预测MMLU-Pro准确性，且比低维激活基线更具可解释性。进一步利用该信号实现段落条件化干预，在四个模型中的三个上提升了准确率，展示了阶段感知的潜在操纵方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作将LLM中的persona向量作为静态工具用于行为操纵，但未能利用其在推理过程中随时间变化的动态信息。本文旨在将这些向量视为可监测和干预的动态探针，从而更深入地理解并控制模型的推理行为。

Method: 定义“polylogue”为生成过程中persona向量与隐藏激活之间的时间序列对齐信号。在四个开源模型上进行实验，使用polylogue特征预测MMLU-Pro正确性，并与低维激活基线对比。同时基于polylogue设计段落条件化的简单干预策略，在生成不同阶段调制对应的潜在方向。

Result: Polylogue特征在预测MMLU-Pro正确性上与低维激活基线竞争性地表现，同时保持更好的可解释性。段落条件化干预在四个模型中的三个上提升了准确率。

Conclusion: Polylogue作为一种可解释的推理时监测和干预工具是有效的，阶段感知的潜在操纵是推理时控制的一个有前景的方向。

Abstract: Recent work shows that large language models (LLMs) encode behavioural traits ("personas") as linear directions in activation space, often called "persona vectors". Prior work has used such directions as static handles for behavioural steering. Building on this, we treat them as dynamic signals instead: probes we can monitor and intervene on as reasoning unfolds. We use the term polylogue to denote the time series of alignments between persona vectors and hidden activations over the course of generation. Experiments across four open-weight models show that polylogue features predict correctness on MMLU-Pro competitively with low-dimensional activation baselines, while remaining interpretable through their associated persona directions. They also suggest concrete steering targets, namely which latent directions to modulate at different stages of a response. We instantiate this as a simple paragraph-conditioned intervention that improves accuracy on three of four models, pointing to stage-aware latent steering as a promising direction for reasoning-time control. Together, this positions the polylogue as an interpretable tool for reasoning-time monitoring and intervention.

</details>


### [220] [FORTIS: Benchmarking Over-Privilege in Agent Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09163)
*Shawn Li,Chenxiao Yu,Han Wang,Wei Yang,Ryan Rossi,Franck Dernoncourt,Xiyang Hu,Philip Yu,Chaowei Xiao,Huan Zhang,Yue Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出FORTIS基准，评估大语言模型智能体在技能层中的过度权限问题。研究发现，模型在技能选择和技能执行两个阶段普遍存在权限过度使用行为，即使最强模型也不例外，且在真实用户交互条件下（不完整规范、便利框架、边界临近）尤为严重，表明技能层本身已成为权限升级的主要来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大语言模型智能体普遍使用中间技能层来连接用户意图与具体任务执行，该层通常被视为组织抽象，但作者认为它实际上是一个权限边界，而现有模型系统性地越过这一边界，导致权限滥用问题。

Method: 构建FORTIS基准，从两个阶段评估过度权限：1) 模型是否从大量重叠的技能库中选择最小够用的技能；2) 模型是否在执行技能时不超出技能允许的工具或行为范围。在十个前沿模型和三个领域上进行评估。

Result: 过度权限行为是常态而非例外。模型一致性地选择比任务所需更高权限的技能和工具，两个阶段的失败率即使对最强模型也居高不下。在真实用户交互条件下（不完整规范、便利框架、边界临近）失败尤为严重，且这些条件无需对抗性构造。

Conclusion: 技能层并不能约束智能体行为，反而成为当前系统中权限升级的主要来源。这说明现有的技能层设计存在根本性问题，需要重新审视其作为安全边界的有效性。

Abstract: Large language model agents increasingly operate through an intermediate skill layer that mediates between user intent and concrete task execution. This layer is widely treated as an organizational abstraction, but we argue it is also a privilege boundary that current models routinely exceed. We present \textbf{FORTIS}, a benchmark that evaluates over-privilege in agent skills across two stages: whether a model selects the minimally sufficient skill from a large overlapping library, and whether it executes that skill without expanding into broader tools or actions than the skill permits. Across ten frontier models and three domains, we find that over-privileged behavior is the norm rather than the exception. Models consistently reach for higher-privilege skills and tools than the task requires, failing at both stages at rates that remain high even for the strongest available models. Failure is especially severe under the ordinary conditions of real user interaction: incomplete specification, convenience framing, and proximity to skill boundaries. None of these requires adversarial construction. The results indicate that the skill layer, far from containing agent behavior, is itself a primary source of privilege escalation in current systems.

</details>


### [221] [CIVeX: Causal Intervention Verification for Language Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09168)
*Fabio Rovai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CIVeX是一个因果干预验证器，通过将动作映射为结构因果查询并检查可识别性，为工具使用语言模型提供可审计的执行/拒绝/实验/弃权判决，在多个基准上实现零虚假执行和高效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的工具使用语言模型虽有多重防护（模式验证、策略过滤、来源检查、状态预测、自验证），但这些防护无法认证状态改变动作具有可识别的因果效应。在混杂的工作流中，观测日志中看似最优的动作执行后可能降低效用。

Method: 提出CIVeX（因果干预验证器），将提议动作映射到结构因果查询（基于承诺的动作-状态图），检查可识别性，返回四种可审计判决：EXECUTE（执行）、REJECT（拒绝）、EXPERIMENT（实验）、ABSTAIN（弃权）。执行需要附带假设范围的因果证书，包含图承诺、识别论证、单侧下置信界(LCB)、来源和风险限制。

Result: 在Causal-ToolBench（1890实例，7种子）上，CIVeX在中等和对抗性混杂下均实现零观测虚假执行。对抗性混杂下达到84.9%准确率和81.1%的oracle效用（+2.23 vs +2.76），是唯一在零虚假执行约束下约束效用超过AlwaysAbstain基线的非oracle方法。在IHDP和ZOZO Open Bandit上，匹配Oracle正确执行率（误差0.1pp内），将每次执行的虚假执行率降低≥50倍。思维链LLM验证器虽能降低虚假执行一个数量级，但在对抗性混杂下Opus的效用仅为CIVeX的74%。

Conclusion: 干预可识别性（而非动作有效性）才是实现可靠工具使用的缺失基元。

Abstract: A valid tool call is not necessarily a valid intervention. Tool-using language agents are guarded by schema validators, policy filters, provenance checks, state predictors, and self-verification, yet such safeguards do not certify that a state-changing action has an identifiable causal effect. In confounded workflows, the action that looks optimal in observational logs can reduce utility when executed.
  We introduce CIVeX, a causal intervention verifier that maps proposed actions to structural causal queries over a committed action-state graph, checks identifiability, and returns one of four auditable verdicts: EXECUTE, REJECT, EXPERIMENT, or ABSTAIN. Execution requires an assumption-scoped causal certificate carrying graph commitments, an identification argument, a one-sided lower confidence bound (LCB), provenance, and risk limits.
  On Causal-ToolBench (1,890 instances, 7 seeds), CIVeX yields zero observed false executions across moderate and adversarial confounding. Under adversarial confounding it reaches 84.9% accuracy and 81.1% of oracle utility (+2.23 vs +2.76) and is the only non-oracle method whose constrained utility under a zero-false-execution constraint exceeds the AlwaysAbstain floor. On IHDP and ZOZO Open Bandit (real production logs with uniform-random ground truth), CIVeX matches Oracle correct-execution within 0.1pp and cuts per-execute false-execution by >=50x over naive baselines. A chain-of-thought LLM verifier (Claude Opus, Sonnet) cuts false-execution by an order of magnitude over a terse baseline, yet under adversarial confounding Opus's utility falls to 74% of CIVeX's. Intervention identifiability, not action validity, is the missing primitive for reliable tool use.

</details>


### [222] [Open Ontologies: Tool-Augmented Ontology Engineering with Stable Matching Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09184)
*Fabio Rovai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一个基于Rust实现的开放本体工程系统Open Ontologies，集成LLM驱动构建、OWL推理和本体对齐。关键发现：稳定1对1匹配是本体对齐质量的主导因素（OAEI Anatomy F1=0.832）；结构化MCP工具访问显著优于读取原始OWL文件（F1=0.717 vs 0.323），甚至优于无文件访问（F1=0.431）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索LLM在本体工程中的有效应用方式，特别是如何结合形式化推理和本体对齐技术，以及LLM访问本体知识的最佳接口形式。

Method: 在Rust中实现集成系统，使用LLM驱动本体构建，结合OWL推理和基于Model Context Protocol的本体对齐。通过OAEI Anatomy和Conference基准测试评估对齐质量，并进行消融实验分析稳定匹配和信号权重的影响。

Result: OAEI Anatomy track上F1=0.832（P=0.963, R=0.733），超过所有对比系统的精度；消融实验显示稳定匹配去除后F1降至0.728；Conference track上F1=0.438；结构化MCP工具访问F1=0.717，显著优于读取原始OWL文件（0.323）和无文件访问（0.431）。

Conclusion: 稳定1对1匹配是本体对齐质量的核心因素；LLM无法通过阅读原始语法来复制结构化工具访问的效果；工具结构提供了质的不同的访问模式。

Abstract: We present Open Ontologies, an open-source ontology engineering system implemented in Rust that integrates LLM-driven construction with formal OWL reasoning and ontology alignment via the Model Context Protocol. Our primary finding is that stable 1-to-1 matching is the dominant factor in ontology alignment quality: on the OAEI Anatomy track, it achieves F1 = 0.832 (P = 0.963, R = 0.733), competitive with state-of-the-art systems and exceeding all in precision. Ablation across five weight configurations shows that signal weights are irrelevant when stable matching is applied (F1 varies by less than 0.004), while removing stable matching drops F1 to 0.728. On the Conference track, the same method achieves F1 = 0.438. On tool-augmented ontology interaction, we find a surprising result: an LLM reading a raw OWL file (F1 = 0.323) performs worse than the same LLM with no file at all (F1 = 0.431), while structured MCP tool access achieves F1 = 0.717. This demonstrates that tool structure provides a qualitatively different mode of access that the LLM cannot replicate by reading raw syntax. The system ships as a single binary under the MIT licence.

</details>


### [223] [Agentic MIP Research: Accelerated Constraint Handler Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09186)
*Liding Xu,Yugeng Zhou,Sebastian Pokutta*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于LLM智能体的MIP研究框架，该框架嵌入SCIP求解器，能够自动生成、验证和评估传播约束处理器，并在MIPLIB 2017基准集上成功恢复了全局约束结构，发现了新的传播策略，额外解决了5个实例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MIP研究同时需要深厚的数学功底和大量的工程投入：在分支切割求解器中测试算法假说需要大量的实现、调试、调参和大规模基准测试。现有流程反馈周期长，效率低，亟需自动化手段来加速研究迭代。

Method: 提出智能体MIP研究框架，将LLM智能体嵌入到求解器感知的框架中，用于生成、验证和评估SCIP开源求解器的插件。具体实例化为：将MIP公式语义提升为全局约束，并自动构建仅做传播的SCIP约束处理器。框架支持在沙盒环境中进行上下文学习，使智能体能够调优、调试约束处理器，并探索MIP问题中的全局约束模式。

Result: 在MIPLIB 2017基准集上，框架成功从约束规划中恢复全局约束结构，生成可执行的约束检测器和仅传播的约束处理器。新发现的传播方法成功额外解决了5个实例。

Conclusion: 该框架能够系统地区分有意义的算法改进与低价值或成本过高的候选方案，证明LLM智能体可以自主导航复杂的MIP研究循环，为更加自动化的求解器开发过程铺平了道路。

Abstract: Mixed-integer programming (MIP) research is both mathematically sophisticated and engineering-intensive: testing an algorithmic hypothesis within a branch-and-cut solver requires substantial implementation, debugging, tuning, and large-scale benchmarking. We propose an agentic MIP research framework that shortens this feedback loop by embedding LLM agents into a solver-aware harness for generating, verifying, and evaluating plugins for the open-source solver SCIP. Propagation methods play a central role in accelerating MIP solving by exploiting global constraints. We instantiate our framework on the semantic lifting of MIP formulations into global constraints and the automatic construction of propagation-only SCIP constraint handlers. On the MIPLIB 2017 benchmark set, the framework successfully recovers global constraint structures from constraint programming and generates executable constraint detectors and propagation-only constraint handlers. Furthermore, the framework naturally extends to in-context learning within a sandboxed environment, enabling agents not only to tune and debug generated constraint handlers on real instances, but also to explore global constraint patterns in MIP problems and discover novel propagation strategies not yet implemented in SCIP. This framework allows us to systematically distinguish meaningful algorithmic improvements from low-value or overly costly candidates: the novel propagation methods successfully solved five additional instances within the explored benchmark. Overall, this framework demonstrates that LLM agents can autonomously navigate the complex MIP research loop, paving the way for a more automated solver development process.

</details>


### [224] [Emergent Semantic Role Understanding in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09187)
*Carla Griffiths,Mirco Musolesi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究通过线性探针方法分析冻结的解码器-only Transformer，发现语义角色理解在预训练中部分涌现，但未完全达到微调水平，且随模型规模增大表征趋向分布式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究语言模型中的语义角色理解（"谁对谁做了什么"）是源自预训练还是依赖任务特定的微调，以理解语言结构如何在模型中涌现及模型对监督信号的真实需求。

Method: 冻结解码器-only Transformer，训练线性探针提取语义角色，通过探针性能判断角色信息是否已在预训练中编码，并对比不同模型规模下的表现。

Result: 冻结表征包含大量语义角色信息，性能随模型规模提升，但未完全匹配微调模型；语义角色结构在预训练中部分涌现，其内部实现随规模增大趋向分布式表征。

Conclusion: 语义角色理解从语言建模目标中部分涌现，但完全习得仍需任务特定微调；模型规模增大时，语义角色的内部表征从局部化转向分布式。

Abstract: Understanding how linguistic structure emerges in language models is central to interpreting what these systems learn from data and how much supervision they truly require. In particular, semantic role understanding ("who did what to whom") is a core component of meaning representation, yet it remains unclear whether it arises from pre-training alone or depends on task-specific fine-tuning. We study whether semantic role understanding emerges during language model pre-training or requires task-specific fine-tuning. We freeze decoder-only transformers and train linear probes to extract semantic roles, using performance to infer whether role information is already encoded in pre-training or learned during adaptation. Across model scales, we find that frozen representations contain substantial semantic role information, with performance improving but not fully matching fine-tuned models. This indicates partial but incomplete emergence from pre-training alone. We show that semantic role structure emerges from language modeling objectives, but its internal implementation shifts toward more distributed representations as model scale increases.

</details>


### [225] [Evidence Over Plans: Online Trajectory Verification for Skill Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09192)
*Yang Zhou,Zihan Dong,Zhenting Wang,Can Jin,Shiyu Zhao,Bangwei Guo,Difei Gu,Linjun Zhang,Mu Zhou,Dimitris N. Metaxas*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出后验蒸馏指标(PDI)和SPARK框架，通过环境验证的轨迹级分析从智能体经验交互中蒸馏技能，在86个可运行任务上生成超越人类编写的技能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有技能生成方法依赖偏好日志而非环境交互验证，导致技能提升效果有限甚至退化。作者识别出根本瓶颈在于时间问题：稳健技能应为后验驱动，从环境经验中蒸馏，而非基于先验计划。

Method: 提出后验蒸馏指标(PDI)，一个量化技能与任务环境证据契合程度的轨迹级指标。构建SPARK框架生成环境验证的轨迹用于计算PDI，并将PDI作为在线诊断和干预信号确保后验技能形成。

Result: 在86个可运行任务上，SPARK生成的技能持续超越无技能基线，并在学生模型上优于人类编写的技能，推理成本比教师模型低1000倍。

Conclusion: PDI引导的蒸馏能产生高效且可迁移的技能，这些技能基于任务-环境交互证据，而非先验假设。

Abstract: Agent skills can remarkably improve task success rates by using human-written procedural documents, but their quality is difficult to assess without environment-grounded verification. Existing skill generation methods heavily rely on preference logs rather than direct environment interaction, often yielding negligible or even degraded gains. We identify that it is a fundamental timing bottleneck: robust skills should be posterior-based, distilled from empirical environment interaction rather than prior plans. In this study, we introduce the Posterior Distillation Index (PDI), a trajectory-level metric that quantifies how well a distilled skill is grounded in the task-environment evidence. To operationalize PDI, we present SPARK (Structured Pipelines for Autonomous Runnable tasKs and sKill generation) for preserving task execution evidence towards full trajectory-level analysis. SPARK generates environment-verified trajectories used to compute PDI, and it applies PDI as an online diagnostic and intervention signal to ensure posterior skill formation. Across 86 runnable tasks, SPARK-generated skills consistently surpass no-skill baselines and outperform human-written skills on student models (inference cost up to 1,000x cheaper than teacher models). These findings show that PDI-guided distillation produces efficient and transferable skills grounded in the task-environment interaction. We release our code at https://github.com/EtaYang10th/spark-skills .

</details>


### [226] [The Geometry of Forgetting: Temporal Knowledge Drift as an Independent Axis in LLM Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09195)
*Rania Elbadry,Ahmed Heakl,Fan Zhang,Dani Bouch,Yuxia Wang,Preslav Nakov,Zhuohan Xie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现大型语言模型中的时间漂移（训练后事实发生变化）在残差流中编码为一个与正确性和不确定性几何正交的方向，因此任何基于正确性或不确定性信号的方法都无法检测漂移。实验验证了这种正交性，并证明线性探针可有效检测漂移，而现有方法基本无效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法无法检测大型语言模型产生的过时答案，作者证明这并非工程失败，而是结构性缺陷——时间漂移在模型内部表示中与正确性和不确定性几何正交，导致基于后两者的方法天生无法检测漂移。

Method: 通过六个指令微调模型进行实验：1)训练线性探针检测漂移，与token熵、语义熵、CCS、SAPLMA等方法对比；2)五项几何正交性检验（权重余弦、分数相关、双向零空间投影等）；3)分析MLP检索电路的动力学相似性；4)跨截止实验控制输入不变仅变化模型，验证探针读取的是模型内部知识状态。

Result: 线性探针AUROC达0.83-0.95，而token熵、语义熵、CCS、SAPLMA等方法仅0.49-0.57（接近随机）。五项检验确认几何正交性（|cos|≤0.14, |r|≤0.20等）。MLP检索电路在陈旧召回和虚构之间产生相同动力学（r>0.81）。跨截止实验验证探针读取模型内部知识状态（P(A>B)=0.975-0.998）。

Conclusion: 时间漂移在LLM内部表示中与正确性和不确定性正交，现有基于后两者的方法无法检测漂移。线性探针可直接检测漂移，揭示了模型内部知识状态的可读性。

Abstract: Large language models confidently produce outdated answers, and no existing method can detect them. We show this is not an engineering failure but a structural one: temporal drift, whether a stored fact has changed since training, is encoded as a direction in the residual stream geometrically orthogonal to both correctness and uncertainty. Any method operating on correctness or uncertainty signals is therefore blind to drift by construction. We verify this across six instruction-tuned models. A linear probe trained directly on drift labels achieves AUROC $0.83$--$0.95$; methods based on token entropy, semantic entropy, CCS, and SAPLMA all remain near chance ($0.49$--$0.57$). Five tests confirm the geometric orthogonality: weight cosines ($|\cos| \leq 0.14$), score correlations ($|r| \leq 0.20$), bidirectional null-space projection ($|Δ| \leq 0.008$), iterative null-space projection with $k{=}10$, and difference-of-means dissociation. Mechanistically, the MLP retrieval circuit produces identical dynamics for stale recall and confabulation ($r > 0.81$, six models), explaining why output confidence cannot separate them. A cross-cutoff experiment holds inputs constant and varies only the model: the probe fires on the model whose training predates the fact's transition and stays silent otherwise ($P(A{>}B) = 0.975$--$0.998$, twelve model pairs), confirming it reads model-internal knowledge state rather than input properties. Our code and datasets will be publicly released.

</details>


### [227] [Learning the Preferences of a Learning Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09217)
*Karim Abdel Sadek,Mark Bedaywi,Rhys Gould,Stuart Russell*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究从学习中的智能体（而非最优智能体）的行为中推断其偏好的问题，并分别针对无遗憾学习者和收敛到最优Boltzmann策略的学习者建立了偏好学习算法的理论保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统逆强化学习（IRL）假设人类行为近似最优，但在人类自身也在学习如何最优行动的场景下这一假设不成立，因此需要形式化学习智能体的偏好推断问题。

Method: 将学习者建模为无遗憾（no-regret）学习器或随时间收敛到最优Boltzmann策略的学习器，分别分析偏好学习算法的理论可行性并建立保证。

Result: 针对两类学习模型，给出了偏好学习算法的理论保证，或证明了此类保证不可能存在。

Conclusion: 从学习中的智能体行为推断偏好是可行的，但不同学习模型下的理论保证存在差异，部分情况下无法建立保证。

Abstract: For AI systems to be useful to humans, they must understand and act in accordance with our values and preferences. Since specifying preferences is a hard task, inverse reinforcement learning (IRL) aims to develop methods that allow for inferring preferences from observed behavior. However, IRL assumes the human to be approximately optimal. This is a big limitation in cases where the human themselves may be learning to act optimally in an environment. In this paper, we formalize the problem of learning the preferences of a learning agent: a predictor observes a learner acting online and tries to infer the underlying reward function being (initially suboptimally) optimized by the learner. We model the learner as either being no-regret, or as converging to an optimal Boltzmann policy over time. In each of these settings, we establish theoretical guarantees for various preference learning algorithms, or otherwise show that such guarantees are impossible.

</details>


### [228] [How Much is Brain Data Worth for Machine Learning?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09243)
*Lane Lewis,Zhixin Wang,David Schwab,Xaq Pitkow*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过线性高斯模型理论推导了神经数据在辅助机器学习任务中的价值，量化了大脑数据与任务样本之间的交换率，并分析了在何种条件下收集神经数据是值得的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究神经记录数据是否以及何时能够提升机器学习模型的训练效果，为神经数据辅助学习（NeuroAI）提供理论基础。

Method: 构建可解析的线性高斯模型，推导多模态估计器在神经数据和任务标签联合训练下的缩放定律，计算大脑样本与任务样本的相对价值和交换率，并分析测试分布偏移下的鲁棒性。

Result: 得到了模型性能随大脑样本数和任务样本数变化的缩放定律；量化了任务-大脑对齐度、噪声水平、潜在维度等因素对神经数据价值的影响；识别了神经正则化学习能带来显著鲁棒性提升的条件；划定了在固定预算下神经数据值得收集的区间。

Conclusion: 该理论框架为理解神经数据在改进机器学习中的价值提供了基础，有助于指导未来NeuroAI研究中数据收集策略的优化。

Abstract: If a person can solve a task, can measuring their brain make it easier to train a model to solve that task too? Recent NeuroAI work suggests that supplementing task training with neural recordings can modestly improve model performance and robustness. However, it is unclear when there should be a benefit from using neural data and how much benefit to expect. We formulate this question mathematically, and begin to address it theoretically using a simple, analytically tractable linear gaussian model of task targets and neural recordings. For a multimodal estimator trained on both brain data and task labels, we derive scaling laws for how performance scales with the numbers of brain and task samples. From these laws we derive relative value and exchange rates between brain samples and task samples, quantifying how much extra task samples neural data is worth as a function of task-brain alignment, neural and task noise, latent dimension, and brain data sample size. We also analyze test distribution shift, to identify conditions where brain-regularized learning can produce substantial robustness gains through learned invariances. Finally, under a fixed collection budget, we characterize the regimes in which brain data is worth collecting. Our results provide a foundation for understanding how valuable brain data could be for improving machine learning.

</details>
